# Torii Quest — Progress Dashboard

Current version: v0.2.823-alpha - GLB WORK QUEUES + HARDENING (AUDIT F08b): the three generation/processing sub-items of F08. (1) A synchronous concurrency gate (server/character/concurrencyGate.js) now bounds both heavy GLB paths — headless-FP-body authoring (Draco decode/re-encode) and paid/free Meshy generation — globally (MAX_CONCURRENT_HEADLESS / MAX_CONCURRENT_GENERATIONS, default 2) and per-pubkey (1). The headless route returns 429 'glb processing queue full'; the paid confirm route un-claims to retryable (settled invoice kept, no re-charge) and returns 429 'generation queue full'. (2) headlessGlb.js enforces a decompressed geometry/texture budget (DECOMPRESSED_BUDGET_BYTES 256 MiB, injectable) after parse — summed accessor + texture bytes — returning 'glb-too-large' → 413, so a 32 MiB ENCODED GLB can no longer inflate unbounded during decode. (3) meshyClient._request now carries an AbortSignal (MESHY_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS 30s, injectable) so a hung upstream request aborts instead of escaping the operation timeout. +6 tests (3 concurrency gate, 2 meshy abort, 1 headless budget). No new test file; suite 4568 / 367 green. F08 fully closed. Version markers + CSP re-bumped.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.822-alpha - CONNECTION CAPS BOUND PER-CLIENT INGRESS/EGRESS (AUDIT F08, connection path): closes the three unbounded per-client costs the finding flagged — (1) the WebSocketServer now sets maxPayload=64 KiB so an inbound frame is rejected BEFORE parse/decode instead of at ws's 100 MiB default (a 32-peer WELCOME is < 8 KiB); (2) sendTo / broadcastToOthers / broadcastToAll now check ws.bufferedAmount and terminate a slow reader once its outbound queue passes 1 MiB, so a stuck client can't accumulate unbounded memory on the server's behalf; (3) the login challenge and session-token Maps are now bounded by COUNT (MAX_CHALLENGES / MAX_TOKENS = 10_000 each, injectable) in addition to TTL — issueChallenge()/issueToken() return null at cap, and GET /mp/auth-challenge returns 429. +5 tests (3 sessionTokens cap cases, 2 arena-ws source-level locks). No new test file; suite 4562 / 367 green. F08 generation-path sub-items (per-npub/global GLB work queue, decompressed-GLB budget, Meshy AbortSignal) remain open as a follow-up. Version markers + CSP re-bumped.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.821-alpha - PLAINTEXT LISTENER BINDS LOOPBACK (AUDIT F07): server/arena-ws.js HOST default is now 127.0.0.1 instead of 0.0.0.0, so a host deployment that does not set HOST binds loopback and never exposes the raw HTTP/WS port to the public internet. The live VPS had been listening on 0.0.0.0:8788 (externally reachable, HTTP 404 from the public IP), while nginx already proxied /mp and its login/admin endpoints to 127.0.0.1:8788 — so loopback binding is fully compatible with the existing reverse proxy; only the raw wildcard bind (the leak) is removed. The Dockerfile arena-ws stage now sets ENV HOST=0.0.0.0 explicitly so a container still listens on container-internal 0.0.0.0 (docker-compose publishes no host port) rather than inheriting loopback. +3 tests to the deploy-artifact contract (server default is loopback and not 0.0.0.0; container opts back to 0.0.0.0 explicitly; bare-metal unit pins 127.0.0.1). No new test file; suite 4557 / 367 green. Version markers + CSP re-bumped.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.820-alpha - DURABLE PAID-GENERATION JOB STORE (2026-09-10 AUDIT F02): new server/character/generationStore.js replaces the transient in-memory pendingGenerations Map with a durable, bounded job-store state machine (pending → claimed → completed|retryable) persisted to a single JSON snapshot written atomically (write .tmp then rename). Fixes F02's five payer-stranding / double-spend hazards: (1) an atomic synchronous compare-and-set claim (pending|retryable → claimed) after verifySettled resolves, so two concurrent confirms can never double-run Meshy on one invoice; (2) a Meshy failure marks the settled job 'retryable' (invoice + prompt retained) so a re-confirm re-verifies the already-settled invoice and retries with NO re-charge (ADR-0108 R8); (3) a 'completed' record caches glbUrl for an idempotent re-confirm; (4) only never-claimed pending entries are volume-capped (maxPending 512) and swept after the 15-min TTL, while claimed/retryable/completed are retained for a 24-h settled window — a payer is never evicted or expired. arena-ws.js confirm now routes through the store with 409 'generation in progress' for the lost claim race and an idempotent 200 on a completed re-confirm. +1 test file (tests/generation-store.test.js, 18 tests: durability / atomic-claim / complete-fail / sweep / bounding + source-level wiring lock). Full suite 4554 / 367 green; ADR-0110 added; curated test-count captures bumped. Version markers + CSP hash re-bumped by bump-ver.sh.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.819-alpha - DEPLOY SUDOERS sudo-rs COMPAT (2026-09-10 AUDIT F05/F06): ops/install-deploy-ssh.sh pins the deploy sudoers `tee` to the fixed `manual.json` request file instead of a `$REQ_DIR/*` glob — sudo-rs (the default sudo on Ubuntu 25.04+, and the live chiefmonkey.art VPS) rejects wildcards in command arguments, so the old glob failed `visudo -c` on a fresh install. The hook now writes that fixed filename (the `manual-${ts}.json` timestamp is dropped; the runner resolves the latest tag itself and deletes every `*.json` after processing, so the timestamp carried no meaning), and a `visudo -c` failure now prints the offending sudoers text instead of a bare FAILED. +1 test file (tests/deploy-ssh-sudoers-contract.test.js, 6 tests) locking fixed-filename tee, no `$REQ_DIR/*` glob, no stale `/opt` path, scoped service verbs, preserved forced-command, and visudo validation. Full suite 4536 / 366 green; curated test-count captures bumped (CURRENT_TEST_STATUS + DEFAULT_TEST_STATUS + NEXT_ACTION_STATE.json). Version markers + CSP hash re-bumped by bump-ver.sh.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.818-alpha - CLEAN-INSTALL DEP ARTIFACT + PROXY CONTRACT (2026-09-10 AUDIT F03/F04): (F03) Dockerfile `arena-ws` stage and `install/lib/bare-metal.sh` now consume the SAME generated runtime manifest (`dist/package.json`, written by tools/write-server-runtime-manifest.mjs) instead of hand-listing only `ws`. draco3d is imported at server startup by the headless-GLB path, so a ws-only clean install crashed on boot. (F04) `Caddyfile` routes `/mp` and `/mp/*` with `handle` (preserving the prefix the server matches) instead of `handle_path` (which stripped it to `/`, `/session`); the Dockerfile CSP build step now APPENDS `wss://$DOMAIN` to the canonical `connect-src` from tools/csp.mjs instead of replacing the relay list with a stale one that dropped Gamestr/Routstr/Vertex/Plebeian. +1 test file (tests/deploy-artifact-contract.test.js, 6 tests). Full suite 4530 / 377 green; curated test-count captures bumped (CURRENT_TEST_STATUS + DEFAULT_TEST_STATUS + NEXT_ACTION_STATE.json). Version markers + CSP hash re-bumped by bump-ver.sh.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.817-alpha - QUEST ENGINE P1 CLUSTER (2026-09-10 AUDIT F1/F2/F3 + F8): (F1) worldLoader.resolveWorldManifest is now consistently async — one `await fetchFn(url)` + one `await res.json()` inside the failure boundary, removing the fake-specific sync/async branch that turned every real fetch `Response`'s thenable `json()` into "unreadable → fallback:legacy" for a valid manifest. Tests now mix real Node `Response` fixtures (async-body success, async JSON rejection, real 404) with the existing sync-fake cases, all awaiting. (F2) remoteAvatarRoster gains `reconcile(peers)` — applies an authoritative WELCOME/reconnect roster snapshot by upserting listed peers and REMOVING absent ones, so a peer that left while the client was disconnected (whose missed `peerLeft` frame never arrived) is no longer stranded; multiplayerHost's `roster` case now delegates to it. (F3) upsert() generation-guards async avatar loads with `roster.get(peer.id) === entry` on BOTH success (dispose a stale result instead of attaching a second scene object) and rejection (a stale rejection no longer deletes a newer leave+rejoin slot). (F8) vite.config.js test include now also discovers `src/engine/**/*.test.js` (12 files, 243 tests) so the colocated engine tests — including the real-fetch worldLoader coverage — run in the CI gate instead of only under an audit config. Tests: worldLoader 21→25, remote-avatars 8→15 (reconcile + peer-id-reuse races). Full suite 4524 / 376 green. Version markers + CSP hash re-bumped by bump-ver.sh.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.816-alpha - SERVER PERF (AUDIT FOLLOW-UP): score-ledger snapshot() uses bounded top-k selection (fixed-capacity heap, O(n log k)) so the 32-row SCORE frame never materializes and fully sorts every retained identity, and register() reconnect resume uses an O(1) npub→id index instead of scanning the whole growing ledger. kamiReplyStore.readRepliesSince() keeps a byte-offset poll cursor so the rack parses only the NEW tail of replies.jsonl instead of re-parsing the whole growing history each poll (non-monotonic ?since falls back to a full read; a shrunken file resets the cursor). Tests: score-ledger 13→16, kami-reply-store 6→10 (top-k byte-equals full-sort; monotonic no-dup/no-miss; non-monotonic fallback; fresh instance; shrink reset). Full suite 4275 / 364.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.815-alpha - BUMP-VER.SH GAP FIX: tools/bump-ver.sh now also bumps public/torii-quest-data.json (its sed list previously skipped that one served file, leaving the Continuum dashboard data a version behind until the dashboard generator was run). tools/regression-check.mjs [5] gained a matching guard so any future marker gap in the bump script fails the release gate instead of shipping silently.
Prior: Current version: v0.2.814-alpha - NODE-IDENTITY PRESENCE (HANDS-FREE INSTALL): the server presence beacon no longer requires a configured admin npub. In node-identity mode (no QUEST_ADMIN_NPUB) the beacon still mints its scoped keypair and self-enables, publishing a kind:30078 torii-gateway event attributed to the beacon's own pubkey (content.npub = beacon npub, no owner p-tag). The gateway reader resolves owner = p-tag hex || event.pubkey, so an ownerless fresh install is now discoverable and crossable with zero identity prerequisite; a configured admin npub still wins when present (Friends/Follows attribution). ADR-0094 amended with a node-identity-mode note. Tests: server-presence-beacon.test.js replaced the two fail-closed assertions with node-identity self-enable + auto-enable and added a wire-contract test (beacon-key signing, no p-tag, content.npub = beacon npub). Companion Suite change (separate repo) wires QUEST_PUBLIC_URL from the install domain and pre-creates the torii-quest service user (SB-08).
Prior: Current version: v0.2.813-alpha - NAKAMA IDENTITY + BEKKA'S FRESH-INSTALL READINESS: (1) The NAP-zone NPC now has an always-visible orange nameplate that reads '<OwnerName> Nakama' (e.g. 'Chiefmonkey Nakama', 'BitcoinBekka Nakama') and follows the walking NPC every frame. Before the owner's kind:0 name resolves, or on an ownerless install, the label is the bare 'Nakama' so the NPC always has a printable identity. The name comes from _ownerProfileName (the same kind:0 resolution used by the homepage 'Welcome <name>,' greeting), so every visitor to chiefmonkey.art sees 'Chiefmonkey Nakama' regardless of who they logged in as -- and every visitor to a Bekka-run instance will see 'BitcoinBekka Nakama'. (2) Pure label rules extracted into src/engine/character/nakamaLabel.js (node-safe, dependency-free): composeNakamaLabel() strips ASCII control chars (0x00-0x1F, 0x7F), trims, caps at 32 chars, and defaults to 'Nakama' on empty/non-string input, so a hostile kind:0 can't overflow or corrupt the sprite canvas. napNpc.js re-exports the composer and adds _makeNakamaNameplate (canvas sprite: 512x128, bold 56px system-ui, Torii orange #f0a04b text on rounded black-55% backdrop, sprite scale 3.2x0.8), positioned at model.max.y + 0.35 headroom (auto-derived from the GLB bounding box, falls back to 2.4). main.js's _refreshOwnerLabel dynamically imports napNpc and calls setNapNpcName on every owner-label repaint (login/logout, capability probe, kind:0 resolve). (3) Investigated Bekka's fresh-install path end-to-end and confirmed EVERY piece she needs is already shipped and production-ready: install.sh (v0.2.699, ADR-0065) accepts --domain/--email/--admin-npub, prompts for admin npub during interactive install, plumbs QUEST_ADMIN_NPUB to both bare-metal + Docker; Character Forge (src/engine/character/) runs rig-assessment -> Blossom upload -> auto-headless FP variant -> portrait render -> kind-35100 publish end-to-end; install.sh --dry-run verified against Bekka-style inputs (test-bekka.example.com, npub1w0vt6zh...). (4) Tests: new tests/v0.2.813-nakama-nameplate.test.js (+12 tests) locks composeNakamaLabel's pure rules (empty/null/undefined/whitespace -> 'Nakama'; real names -> '<name> Nakama'; trim + 32-char cap + control-char strip; NAKAMA_NAME_MAX = 32; exports canonical suffix + default label); asserts main.js _refreshOwnerLabel wires dynamic import + setNapNpcName + uses OWNER's _ownerProfileName not viewer's; asserts napNpc.js re-exports the API + attaches sprite in scene + tracks root position + uses #f0a04b; asserts pure module has zero imports (node-safe). Full suite 4267 tests / 364 files, all green. CSP hash re-bumped to sha256-bILPi02f+qxTxbbtSLn2BUCQnywoUWBIbJ8TLkM6LhQ=. Build: napNpc chunk 3.75 kB -> 4.92 kB (+1.17 kB for the sprite factory). 4267 tests / 364 files.

Prior: v0.2.812-alpha - INACTIVE-GUEST HOVER LABEL SWAP + DIVIDER BREATHING ROOM (RESPONSIVE): (1) On hover of the inactive ENTER AS GUEST button (before a character is picked), the label text swaps from 'ENTER AS GUEST' to 'PICK A CHARACTER' -- explicit call-to-action reinforcing what the not-allowed cursor + ochre halo (v0.2.811) already signal. Implemented markup-first: the button now holds two child spans (.btn-label-base 'ENTER AS GUEST' + .btn-label-hover 'PICK A CHARACTER' aria-hidden='true'). CSS toggles display:inline/none between them scoped to #btn-enter-nap[data-armed='false']:hover so only the inactive state swaps. A stable aria-label='Enter as guest' pins the a11y name so screen readers do not perceive the visual swap. Once _armGuestEnterButton() fires it sets btn.textContent='ENTER' which wipes the spans entirely -- the armed state can never accidentally swap. (2) .torii-divider vertical margin bumped 18px -> 32px so each of the three sections (guest picker / AI create / nostr login) gets ~28px extra vertical rhythm on desktop. Responsive fallback: @media (max-height:720px), (max-width:480px) sends the margin back to 18px so short viewports (landscape phones, small desktop windows) and narrow portrait phones do not scroll the entry-panel off-screen. Tests: updated tests/v0.2.776-regression.test.js x2 (label regex now allows the intervening .btn-label-base span opener; comment cleanup for the pointer-events:none removal in v0.2.811). Updated tests/v0.2.806-regression.test.js (same regex widening). New tests/v0.2.812-regression.test.js (+9 tests) locks: aria-label='Enter as guest'; both label spans + text content + aria-hidden on the hover span; base rule display:inline; hover-rule display swap scoped to data-armed='false'; NO armed-state swap CSS; _armGuestEnterButton() still sets textContent to 'ENTER'; divider margin 32px auto (not 18px auto); @media responsive fallback to 18px auto exists. CSP hash re-bumped to sha256-V0HYPcVqAfoQ/2f54UwYt3wfEjNg/gO6NqCcQubDQzU=. 4255 tests / 363 files.

Prior: v0.2.809-alpha - UNIFIED CTA STYLING + SAGE-GREEN ARMED/HOVER: all three title-screen CTAs (ENTER AS GUEST, CREATE WITH AI, LOGIN NOSTR) now share one style contract per user direction. (1) Base state: shared transparent fill + ochre outline + cream-ochre text, lifted from the previous LOGIN NOSTR treatment. Shared selector list on the ID rule so a future edit has to touch every button at once. (2) Height parity: all three buttons inherit .btn base sizing (padding 11 20, font 13, letter-spacing 3) with only a 242px inline width. Nostr button's margin-bottom:6px stripped so all three CTAs stack with identical footprint. (3) ENTER AS GUEST turned transparent-outline like the other two; when a guest character is picked, _selectGuestCharacter still flips data-armed to true and it becomes the sage-green CTA. (4) Sage green palette (muted, blends with the ochre/rust sunset): base fill rgba(77,122,58,*) which is #4d7a3a family, accent edge rgba(110,169,77,*) which is #6ea94d family. Chosen to read clearly as green while sitting inside the sunset palette; brighter than the muted rusty sunset background but far shy of the previous bright lime #22c55e. (5) Hover: CREATE WITH AI + unarmed LOGIN NOSTR flip to sage fill (0.55 alpha) + brighter olive edge + soft two-stop glow. Guest button when unarmed is pointer-events:none so it never hovers. Guest + LOGIN NOSTR when ARMED get a stronger sage armed-idle rule (0.72 fill) that promotes to a punchier halo on hover so armed-idle -> armed-hover reads as pressable. (6) Guest inactive state moved from grey slate to dimmed transparent (opacity 0.55) + cursor:not-allowed + pointer-events:none preserved. Bright-lime relic colours (#22c55e / #16a34a / #eafff0) removed from the entry buttons. v0.2.776 armed-treatment test relaxed from #22c55e literal to the sage palette regex. v0.2.808 nostr-inline test asserts margin-bottom is gone. New v0.2.809-regression.test.js (+8 tests) locks the shared base selector list, the transparent-outline body, the shared hover rule, the shared armed rule, the shared armed-hover rule, the no-bright-lime invariant, and the inactive click-block. Prior: Current version: v0.2.807-alpha - VERTICAL BAND CRANKED + OVERLAY STRIPPED + CAPTIONS: v0.2.806 vertical readability band was measured too subtle to read (peak alpha 0.32 shifted underlying sunset by ~10/255 per channel). New v0.2.807-regression.test.js (+11 tests) locks the band alpha bump, the overlay removal, the backdrop-filter reset, and the caption trio. CSP hash re-bumped. 4213 tests / 358 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.806-alpha - HOMEPAGE STRIP + VERTICAL READABILITY BAND: the smoked-glass panel at #title-centre was still reading as a hard-edged box against the sunset photo, and the surrounding copy was clutter. This ship strips the entry panel down to just the flow (2 guest cards + ENTER-AS-GUEST + CREATE-WITH-AI + LOGIN-NOSTR) — removing the tagline (SOVEREIGNTY etc.), the eyebrow (CHOOSE HOW YOU ENTER), the numbered step labels, per-option subheads, the keyboard-controls cheatsheet, and every c-divider — and replaces the four .gp-ring* backdrop-blur bands + .gp-tint on #title-centre.glass-panel with a SINGLE flat .gp-band child: a vertical readability band whose opacity peaks on the centre axis and gradient-fades continuously to 100% transparent at the left and right sides (top/bottom also feather via mask). No backdrop-filter anywhere on the panel or the band → no blur-sampling edge can be drawn. ARIA labels on each entry <section> and on the char-picker retained so screen readers still announce the option purpose. v0.2.776 Bug I test migrated (no more .entry-num assertions — now checks button copy + strip invariants). New v0.2.806-regression.test.js (+15 tests) locks the strip + the band + the no-backdrop-filter invariant. CSP hash re-bumped. 4202 tests / 357 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.804-alpha - LOGIN-NOSTR/ENTER SURFACE ENFORCES LOGGED-IN NPUB CHARACTER (guest-as-nostrich → exit → click ENTER-as-npub entered as nostrich): the LOGIN-NOSTR button is armed to "ENTER" after login and routes through window.__toriiEnterArenaFromTitle, which just clicked #btn-enter-nap. NOSTR_LOGIN only fires on the FIRST login of a session, so its guest-reset (v0.2.800/v0.2.803) never re-ran on later clicks. Fix: __toriiEnterArenaFromTitle now enforces the logged-in npub character path when state.nostrPubkey is a valid 64-hex — resets _guestCharChosen + _pendingGuestChar to the full-height built-in and deselects the guest cards before delegating into the shared boot. Guarded by the 64-hex check so a genuine guest ENTER (no login) is unchanged. +4 source-lock tests. 4187 tests / 356 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.803-alpha - LOGGED-IN NPUB FALLS BACK TO FULL-HEIGHT FP BODY (looking down as chiefmonkey showed poo-poo-head's yellow feet): after fresh load → tap poo-poo-head guest card → log in as npub → enter, the world mesh loaded the player's own kind-35100 character but looking down still showed the guest torso's yellow cartoon feet. Root cause: the v0.2.800 login handler cleared _guestCharChosen but not _pendingGuestChar, so getCharacter() still returned 'guest' and firstPersonBody.js fell back to FP_BODIES['guest'] (guest-headless.glb) whenever the manifest carried no headlessHash. Fix: NOSTR_LOGIN now also resets _pendingGuestChar to 'chiefmonkey' so a manifest without a headlessHash falls back to the full-height built-in FP body instead of the poo-poo-head torso. +3 source-lock tests. 4183 tests / 355 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.802-alpha - FP BODY RE-SHOWS WHEN LOOKING AWAY FROM MIRROR (no body/feet when looking down): the v0.2.768 mirror guard hid the ENTIRE first-person headless body whenever the player was within MIRROR_HIDE_DIST (3m) — distance-only, regardless of facing. Standing by the glass and looking DOWN (away from the reflection) therefore showed no body or feet at all. Fix: the mirror-hide now ALSO requires the player to be roughly FACING the mirror (camera forward · direction-to-mirror > MIRROR_FACING_DOT = 0.3 ≈ cos72°); a downward/away glance re-shows chest → feet. +3 source-lock tests. 4180 tests / 354 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.801-alpha - STAND-SHOOT BLEND + MIRROR-GUN RELOAD FIX: (1) the animation library has no stand-and-shoot clip (only Run_Forward_Firing), so firing while stationary played a run cycle. New src/engine/character/standShootBlend.js procedurally synthesizes Stand_Shoot by blending the FIRE clip's upper body (spine/arms/head from Run_Forward_Firing) over the IDLE clip's lower body (Hips/legs/feet from Idle_02), emitting per-bone quaternion tracks plus a single rooted Hips position track; wired into animationLibrary.js (_clips + GAME_STATE_TO_CLIP.STAND_SHOOT) and playerModel.js (standing fire now plays Stand_Shoot, falling back to Run_Forward_Firing). +3 tests. (2) MIRROR GUN GONE AFTER LOGIN/CHARACTER SWAP: the world gun attached only once (guarded by if(!_worldGun)), so a body reload left it parented to the removed old RightHand bone — swapping guest→chiefmonkey showed no gun in the mirror. setRightHandBone now detaches the prior gun and re-attaches to the new bone on rebind (idempotent for the same bone; +3 source-lock tests). OUTSTANDING: first-person 'no feet when looking down' still diagnosed (headless meshes carry feet geometry and the neck clip-plane doesn't slice them — needs a screenshot). 4177 tests / 353 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.800-alpha - POSITION-TRACK RETARGET (character half-in-ground): uploaded characters stood upright after the world-delta fix but sank ~76cm into the floor. Root cause: the library clips carry a CONSTANT position track on EVERY bone (centimetre / Z-up bind offsets), and retargetClipWorldDelta name-remapped ALL of them verbatim — so the target Hips got the library's Y=19.63 instead of its own Y=95.98. Fix: verbatim remap removed; retargetClipWorldDelta now emits ONE Hips position track (world delta frame-mapped Z-up→Y-up, height-scaled h_t/h_l, added onto the target bind position) and every other bone keeps its bind position — exactly matching tools/glb_retarget.py root motion. +1 regression test. ALSO — NPUB LOGIN OVERRIDES GUEST PICK: tapping a guest torso card then logging in kept the guest torso instead of the player's own kind-35100 mesh (sticky _guestCharChosen flag). The NOSTR_LOGIN handler now resets _guestCharChosen + deselects the cards so the player's own mesh seats; an explicit card tap after login still wins. +1 source-lock test. 4171 tests / 351 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.799-alpha - RETARGET RUNTIME FIX (uploaded meshes adopt the library): the runtime retarget was silently THROWING and falling back to the uploaded mesh's OWN clips, so an uploaded character walked but never fired (its baked clips lack Run_Forward_Firing; the game-state names no-oped). Two bugs: (1) sampleQuat's hemisphere alignment called Quaternion.multiplyScalar(-1) — a Vector3-only method — so the slerp branch threw whenever two stored keyframes sat in opposite quaternion hemispheres (dot<0; every real library clip hits this); fixed with an explicit component negate. (2) the world-delta loop premultiplied the parent inverse into wt in place and then STORED that already-local result, so every bone below the hips used a local where a world was required — chiefmonkey's +19deg Hips rest made the feet/head ~170deg off; fixed by keeping wtWorld separate and computing local = inv(parentWorld)*wtWorld. Validated dot~1 vs tools/glb_retarget.py across all 24 bones and start/mid/end frames on Idle_02, Run_Forward_Firing, Reload_Hand_Gun. +2 regression tests (off-axis 3-bone parent-world; opposite-hemisphere slerp). 4169 tests / 351 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.798-alpha - A_BONE ALIGNMENT (custom-mesh head pitch + firing arms): the first runtime port (v0.2.797) stood the character upright but left a residual twist — the head pitched back (chin up, looking at the sky) and the firing pose's arms washed out (firing in the mirror read as walking). Root cause: world-delta retrieves the axis-frame but not each bone's child-axis twist; the offline bake (tools/glb_retarget.py) also shortest-arc-aligns every parent→child WORLD direction (its A_bone step), which the first port skipped. Fix: new buildBoneAlignment() computes the per-bone shortest-arc alignment (source child axis frame-mapped Z-up→Y-up onto the target child axis) and retargetClipWorldDelta now applies it (wt = dw * A_bone * target.bind_world); leaf bones keep identity. Validated against the offline bake on the real animation-library.glb → chiefmonkey7.glb pair: all 24 bones match dot≈1 on Idle_02 AND Run_Forward_Firing. buildRigBind now also emits childOf (first child per bone). +2 tests (head-twist alignment); 4167 tests / 351 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.797-alpha - CUSTOM-MESH ORIENTATION FIX (world-delta retarget, Bug N): an uploaded character was lying SUPINE in the NAP mirror because the shared animation-library.glb is authored Z-up (its Hips rest is ~-71deg about X) while an uploaded mesh (chiefmonkey7.glb) is Y-up, and the runtime retarget was a NAME-ONLY remap (animationRetarget.retargetClip) that applied the library's Z-up hips rotation verbatim to the Y-up target. Fix: new src/engine/character/retargetWorldDelta.js ports the offline world-delta bake (tools/glb_retarget.py) to runtime — per bone it computes the master's world rotation, subtracts its bind (rest), frame-maps Z-up->Y-up (RotX +90deg), then re-expresses it in the target's local frame, so the target stands upright and animates like the built-in characters. loadAnimationLibrary now also caches the master rig's bones (getAnimationLibraryBones) and loadPlayerModel retargets via buildRigBind + retargetClipWorldDelta instead of the name remap; position tracks remain name-remapped (in-place game clips carry no root motion). +4 tests (retarget-world-delta: bind-cancel, finite-normalised output, null guards). 4165 tests / 351 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.796-alpha - CHARACTER PORTRAIT SNAPSHOT (avatar everywhere): (1) New src/engine/character/characterPortraitRenderer.js — an offscreen GLB-to-PNG portrait renderer (GLTFLoader + DRACOLoader, transparent background, neutral studio lighting, Z-up standing fix mirroring playerModel, oversize/URL guards, WebGL context disposal) that rasterises a player's mesh into a 256px avatar PNG. (2) The torii.character manifest gains an optional portrait.hash (the PNG's Blossom sha256) — added to emptyCharacterManifest + validateCharacterManifest + the kind-35100 build/parse round-trip (new 'portrait' tag) + resolveCharacterPortraitUrl in characterMesh. (3) _uploadCustomMesh now auto-renders + uploads a portrait and folds it into the SAME publish (fails soft; a character without one still publishes), and a new 'Generate portrait' action (_generateOwnPortrait) lets legacy characters generate one from the found view. (4) The Character Forge found view swaps its 'C' initial for the rendered portrait (cf-summary-portrait-photo) and shows the generate affordance when none exists. +22 tests (portrait manifest validation, event round-trip, URL resolution, v0.2.796-regression source-lock; 2 fixed stale source-lock assertions). 4161 tests / 350 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.795-alpha - CHARACTER TAB PERF + STICKERS OWN TABS: (1) The Character settings screen no longer triple-queries relays — fetchOwnCharacter now short-circuits through a ~60s in-memory TTL cache (login-time mesh check + Character-tab open share ONE fan-out; repeat opens are instant), and publishCharacter busts that cache on a successful publish so a fresh character is never masked by a warm stale entry. (2) The inline "Edit stickers" / sticker editor is REMOVED from the Character Forge (no more 'edit' mode, add/remove-sticker handlers, or sticker count on the card). Stickers move to a NEW dedicated "Stickers" settings tab (renderStickerPanel) — a browse/library surface (curated STICKER_LIBRARY grid + owned-count) with a "collecting + placing in-world coming next" note; they'll become the Nap-zone social-expression primitive (replacing bullets) in a later slice. The in-world sticker PLACEMENT path (_confirmSelfViewPlacement, ADR-0088) is untouched. +11 tests (sticker-panel renderer + v0.2.795-regression source-lock; -3 obsolete sticker-editor/handler assertions). 4139 tests / 349 files.

Prior:  BLOSSOM BUD-11 AUTH (UPLOAD FIX): uploading a custom mesh ("Replace character" / "Create with AI") failed with the opaque "upload-failed" toast. Root cause: uploadBlossom signed a NIP-98 style auth event (kind 27235, `u`+`method` tags only) — but Blossom uses its OWN authorisation scheme (BUD-11): kind 24242 with a `t` action verb (`upload`), an `x` tag scoping the token to the exact blob's sha256, a NIP-40 `expiration`, and a human-readable `content`. blossom.primal.net rejected the old token with 401; that 401 carried no CORS headers, so the browser blocked reading the status, `fetch` threw, and the error collapsed to "upload-failed" rather than "upload-http-401". Fix: `BLOSSOM_AUTH_KIND` -> 24242; `buildBlossomAuthEvent` now emits `t`/`x`/`expiration` (+ harmless `u`/`method`) with content "Upload Blob"; `uploadBlossom` computes the file's sha256 client-side (noble sha2) and threads it into the token's `x` tag AND the PUT's `X-SHA-256` header. Verified end-to-end against blossom.primal.net (200 + correct sha256). +8 tests (character-publish: BUD-11 tag assertions + x-tag/header scoping; new v0.2.794-regression source-lock). 4131 tests / 347 files.

Prior:  CHARACTER NAME FROM NPUB, NOT FILE: v0.2.792 defaulted a newly-uploaded character's display name to the uploaded file's stem (chiefmonkey7.glb → "chiefmonkey7"). Player pushed back: a character is named after the person, not the file. Fix: `_uploadCustomMesh` now takes the character's display name from `state.nostrName` (already sanitised at login by nostr.js from the player's kind:0 `name` / `display_name`), with a short-pubkey-slice fallback when the profile is empty and a final 'Custom' fallback for guests. So chiefmonkey's own npub uploading `chiefmonkey7.glb` publishes a character named "chiefmonkey" (their kind:0 name), not "chiefmonkey7". +4 tests (new v0.2.793-regression source-lock: name sourced from state.nostrName, pubkey-slice fallback shape, 'Custom' guest fallback, no empty-string manifest.name in the upload block). v0.2.792 regression test relaxed to only lock the non-empty-manifest invariant it introduced (the source-of-name assertion now lives in v0.2.793-regression). 4123 tests / 346 files.

Prior:  REPLACE CHARACTER + NAME FROM FILE: a returning player with a stale kind-35100 character (a guest-demo "Nostrich" whose mesh was never on Blossom, so in-world fell back to the guest "poo poo head" default) had no way to swap back to their own mesh — the Character Forge "found" view offered only "Edit stickers". Fix: the found view now has a "Replace character" action that re-opens the upload flow (signs + publishes a fresh kind-35100, replacing the old via the parameterized-replaceable d-tag), and a new upload defaults the character's display name to the uploaded file stem (chiefmonkey7.glb → chiefmonkey7) instead of an empty "Unnamed". +4 tests (new v0.2.792-regression source-lock) + a found-view assertion; `.cf-summary-actions` layout row added. 4119 tests / 345 files.

Prior:  CONTENT-HASHED ENTRY (STALE-BUILD LOOP FIX): a returning player was stuck in a 'Stale build detected — refreshing to sync…' loop on their normal profile (cleared cache + unregistered workers twice) while a fresh private tab worked. Root cause: the entry chunk was pinned to a STABLE filename (`torii-entry.js`) and cache-busted only with a per-build `?v=<timestamp>` query — fragile, because a stale cached chunk could still resolve the CURRENT entry file under an old `?v=` URL, re-evaluate `torii-entry.js` a second time, and trip the duplicate-boot guard, which reloaded with `?nuked=1` but re-served the same stale pair and looped. Fix: the entry is now CONTENT-HASHED (`torii-entry-<hash>.js` via `entryFileNames: 'assets/torii-entry-[hash].js'`), so a stale chunk can only point at an old, now-404 entry file — stale and fresh builds can never collide at the module-URL level and the double-boot guard can no longer fire. The bundler already emits every chunk's back-reference as the relative `./torii-entry-<hash>.js`, so the old write-time `?v=` rewrite (BUILD_STAMP / ENTRY_BASE / ENTRY_IMPORT_RE / entryUrlFor*) is deleted from vite.config.js; the plugin resolves the hashed entry filename from the emitted bundle (`hashedEntryFileName`). The `__toriiShellImported` idempotency guard, the main.js module guard, and the index.html self-heal all remain (now unreachable but kept belt-and-braces). CSP inline-script hash re-derived (sha256-1QcVJg5wavLJvXB68TG4Ifm4Zer7ZBblKpTly2CwMLA=). +6 tests (new v0.2.791-regression source-lock: hashed entryFileNames, no timestamp-machinery remnants, hashedEntryFileName helper, hashed relative entryUrl injection, hashed ENTRY_IMPORT_LINE) + rewrote tests/quest-base-entry (share-one-content-hash + relative forms incl. the runtime registry's bare `assets/…`) + the v0.2.773 build-injection lock. 4115 tests / 344 files.

Prior: v0.2.790-alpha - DOCS-PPLX-PURGE (sovereign-host cleanup): removed every reference to the retired third-party AI-agent hosting platform (the `*.pplx.app` subdomains and the "Perplexity" product name) from Quest's runtime, tests, tooling, static assets, ADRs, and state files — the solution now runs fully self-hosted (chiefmonkey.art) with no third-party publish/deploy dependency. `src/main.js` `allowPrivate` gate tightened to localhost-only (dropped the `*.pplx.app` private-visit allowlist); all live-host constants and fixtures (torii-quest.pplx.app / quest-torii.pplx.app) replaced with chiefmonkey.art; deploy docs rewritten to the sovereign Torii Suite installer flow (operator-triggered deploy, no forced update). 28 archival files (22 version-indexed `torii-v*.md` reports, 5 `MVP_*` release snapshots, `torii-source-reconciliation-report.md`) frozen as Option B — their point-in-time references are historically accurate and left untouched. +3 tests (new v0.2.790-regression source-lock: zero-third-party-host sweep across src/public/tools/tests/docs-adr/index.html, localhost-only allowlist, chiefmonkey.art fixtures). CSP inline-script hash re-derived (sha256-h/i+/b/aAqJC0/qETDIF8PqLDiVVWRIFneohv95V8as=). 4109 tests / 343 files.

Prior: v0.2.789-alpha - CUSTOM-MESH LOAD FALLBACK ('can't enter the world after login'): a logged-in player whose signed kind-35100 character referenced a Blossom mesh that 404s (the 'nostrich' preset shipped its blob hash 07566cdf… but the operator never uploaded nostrich-master.glb to Blossom, so resolveCharacterMeshUrl produced a dead URL) could not enter the world — loadPlayerModel() did `await loader.loadAsync(customUrl)` with NO fallback and re-threw in its catch, so _arena.boot() died and ENTER showed 'Arena failed to load …'. This ship makes loadPlayerModel() fall back to the built-in default character (assetUrl(char.file)) when the custom mesh fails to load, tracking a `usedCustomMesh` flag so the runtime animation-retarget branch (only correct for an actually-loaded arbitrary rig) is skipped for the fallback default mesh. +5 tests (new v0.2.789-custom-mesh-fallback source-lock). 4106 tests / 342 files.

Prior: v0.2.788-alpha - SETTINGS RE-RENDER STOPS WIPING FORM INPUT: the ~10s server-beacon re-sync (_syncServerBeacon, driven by the rAF _shellTick every ~600 frames) unconditionally called renderActiveSettingsTab() whenever the Settings panel was open, which rebuilt the WHOLE active tab via innerHTML — so the Create-with-AI prompt textarea got replaced by a fresh empty one a few seconds after typing, discarding text + focus. This ship gates the periodic repaint to the Heartbeat tab only (the only tab that sync actually refreshes): `if (isSettingsPanelOpen() && getActiveSettingsTab() === 'heartbeat') renderActiveSettingsTab();`. Other tabs keep their own event-driven re-renders. +3 tests (new v0.2.788-forge-prompt-preserved source-lock) + updated the v0.2.781 source-lock to the guarded form. 4101 tests / 341 files.

Prior: v0.2.787-alpha - GENERATION ERROR SURFACING (ADR-0108 follow-up): the "Create with AI" failure view rendered a bare "Something went wrong" that hid the real error, so a signed-out player hitting the session-gated generation (requestMeshGeneration short-circuits client-side with 'no-session-token' BEFORE the request ever leaves the browser) had no idea why it failed. This ship surfaces the error through a new _friendlyAiError() mapper in characterForgePanel.js: 'no-session-token'/'session required' → "Sign in to generate — AI character generation needs a Nostr session"; 'generator unavailable' → "Generator is offline"; 'prompt too long' → "Description too long"; 'generation failed' → a retry hint; any unknown message falls back to showing it verbatim (escaped). Purely the render layer — no server/network/Meshy change. +3 tests (session mapping, offline mapping, raw-message fallback). 4098 tests / 340 files.

Prior: v0.2.786-alpha - MESHY REMESH FIX + GENERATION PROGRESS (ADR-0108 follow-up): v0.2.785's live AI flow produced the textured model fine (preview→refine, ~1.9M faces) but Meshy rigging hard-rejects models over 320k faces, so every generation died with "Meshy 400 … exceeds the 320,000 face limit". This ship inserts a REMESH (decimate) pass between refine and rig: server/character/meshyClient.js gains createRemeshTask (POST /v1/remesh) + getRemeshTask (GET /v1/remesh/:id), decimating to REMESH_TARGET_POLYCOUNT 90000 — matched to chiefmonkey7.glb (90,367 tris / 2.40 MiB) so generated characters sit inside the built-in roster's render/file-size budget and clear the 320k ceiling ~3.5×. generateCharacterGlb now runs preview→refine→remesh→rig (rigging the remeshed task, not the refine task) and accepts an injected onStage() callback the route wires to log.info so journalctl shows which pass is running. Client: the "Generating character…" view gains a CSS spinner + honest copy ("four Meshy passes … several minutes"). +5 tests (remesh body/default+explicit+under-ceiling, onStage sequence, fail-closed). 4095 tests / 340 files.

Prior: v0.2.785-alpha - PAID AI CHARACTER CREATION (LNURL-pay + NIP-57 zaps, ADR-0108): v0.2.784's live text-to-3D was operator-paid and session-gated only, so any signed-in visitor could spend the operator's Meshy credits — an open tap at public launch. This ship adds a two-phase payment gate so the operator charges sats per generation and covers cost + margin with NO self-hosted Lightning node: (1) new server/character/lightningInvoice.js — a pure, injectable LNURL-pay client (resolveLightningAddress / mintInvoice / verifySettled) that mints a BOLT11 invoice from the operator's hosted lightning address and confirms settlement, FAIL-CLOSED (an unconfirmed invoice never yields a generation; verify URL is the de-facto lnurlpay convention, tunable per-operator); (2) server/arena-ws.js splits POST /mp/mesh/generate into two phases — generate mints an invoice and returns {requirePayment, generationId, invoice, amountSats}, and a new POST /mp/mesh/generate/confirm verifies settlement (via a bounded, swept pendingGenerations map with the prompt held server-side so a confirm can't swap it) BEFORE calling Meshy; two new per-instance env vars drive it — MESH_GEN_PRICE_SATS (0/unset = operator-paid, unchanged) and MESH_GEN_LUD16 (the operator's receiver); 503 payment-unconfigured / 502 invoice-failed / 402 payment-required / 408 expired, all fail-closed; (3) src/engine/character/liveMeshGeneration.js — requestMeshGeneration() now surfaces the requirePayment shape and adds confirmMeshGeneration(generationId); (4) main.js _generateAICharacter() pauses on a payment sheet (new 'payment' _forgeAIState) and adds _payForGeneration (one-tap window.webln) / _confirmGeneration / _copyGenerationInvoice; (5) characterForgePanel.js renders the payment sheet (amount, read-only copyable BOLT11, Pay/Copy/I've-paid/Cancel). NIP-57 zap receipts (kind 9735) are the P1 robustness upgrade; P0 uses the raw invoice + verify. +16 tests (10 lightning-invoice: resolve/mint/verify incl. millisat amount, fail-closed no-pr/zero-amount/not-settled, verify-URL precedence + fallback; +6 live-mesh-generation: requirePayment shape + confirmMeshGeneration contract); CSP hash sha256-oqDfh2f+COVXm5Dt7G0Am5qplvPJuxHO2SwpXOhaxpc=; 4090 tests / 340 files.

Prior: v0.2.784-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE LIVE TEXT-TO-3D (Step C, ADR-0091): the 'Create with AI' flow was a LOCAL MOCK (runMockGeneration / meshGenerationMock.js) that returned a canned fixture and produced nothing. This ship swaps in a REAL Meshy text-to-3D pipeline, operator-paid behind a server-side proxy so the key never reaches the browser: (1) new server/character/meshyClient.js — a pure, injectable Meshy REST client (createTextTo3D preview/refine, createRigTask, waitForTask, riggedGlbUrl, generateCharacterGlb) orchestrating preview → refine → rig → rigged-GLB URL; (2) server/arena-ws.js adds POST /mp/mesh/generate (session-gated; 401 no session / 400 bad or over-long prompt / 503 no MESHY_API_KEY / 502 upstream failure) returning the rigged GLB URL, reading MESHY_API_KEY from the environment; (3) new src/engine/character/liveMeshGeneration.js — the browser wrapper requestMeshGeneration() that POSTs the prompt with the session bearer and returns {ok, glbUrl, error}; (4) main.js _generateAICharacter() is now async: it requests a real mesh, downloads the rigged GLB, and hands it to _uploadCustomMesh() for the existing validator-first publish (glbInspect → assessRig → headless → Blossom → publish → seat) — the mock result and its rAF 'thinking' resolution are removed; (5) characterForgePanel.js 'Generate demo' → 'Generate' + live copy. meshGenerationMock.js stays (its tests + the pure validateGeneratedMesh gate remain) but is no longer wired in main.js. +18 tests (12 meshy-client: auth/request shape, preview+refine body, waitForTask success+FAIL, riggedGlbUrl tolerant parsing, generateCharacterGlb orchestration+fail; +6 live-mesh-generation: prompt/trim/auth/error/URL contract); CSP hash sha256-0d5okIqgB+6NLrfzybe7pbUDdit/dPTy7Yh2AyeyNE8=; 4074 tests / 339 files. DEPLOY NOTE: the live feature also needs MESHY_API_KEY on the VPS systemd environment — until then the endpoint returns 503 'generator unavailable' (graceful).

Prior: v0.2.783-alpha - HEARTBEAT SWITCH DEAD-CLICK: Settings > Heartbeat's on/off switch ignored every click — no toggle, no toast, whether or not the operator was signed in as admin. Root cause was the delegated click router, NOT the write path (v0.2.781's server-beacon-truth work left setBeacon/_applyHeartbeatToggle intact): the switch is a `<button data-action="publish-node">` whose entire clickable surface is nested `<span>`s (`.settings-switch-track` > `.settings-switch-knob` + `.settings-switch-state`), and main.js's document-level router read `data-action` off `e.target` directly — so e.target was always an inner span with no data-action, getAttribute returned null, and the router bailed before reaching the publish-node branch. Fix (src/main.js, one place): resolve the action element with `t.closest('[data-action]')` instead of `e.target`, and read the sibling data-* attributes (`data-relay` / `data-sticker` / `data-index`) off that same resolved element — identical behaviour for every previously-working plain-text button (those already resolved to the button itself) while nested-span buttons (the switch) now resolve. No architecture change. +4 tests (v0.2.783-heartbeat-switch-click: source-lock that closest() replaces the e.target read + data-* reads use the resolved element; behavioural lock that the rendered switch's nested spans closest() back to publish-node); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-+d+mnjmSPqORBtIZ4shtckRQJNBl0x3FgThIhd8QElA=; 4051 tests / 337 files.

Prior: v0.2.782-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE RUNTIME AUTO-RIG: the forge validated a .glb upload's rig (assessRig → "Rig OK") but the runtime never animated it — the skeleton contract (mapBonesToRoles) was VALIDATION-ONLY, and playerModel.js only played clips baked onto built-in GLBs offline (tools/glb_retarget.py), so any uploaded mesh rendered as a static bind pose. This ship closes the seat-the-mesh gap: (1) new src/engine/character/animationRetarget.js — a pure, node-safe bone-name retargeter (buildBoneRebind / remapTrackName / retargetClip / collectTrackBoneNames) that maps the animation-library clips (Mixamo) onto an arbitrary uploaded humanoid skeleton (Tripo/Meshy/Biped/generic) through the canonical role contract, remapping each track's bone name and dropping unmapped optional roles. (2) playerModel.js loadPlayerModel() now, when a _customMeshUrl is set, loads the shared animation library and retargets it onto the loaded skeleton at RUNTIME (falling back to the mesh's own clips when nothing maps), so a freshly-uploaded rig actually walks/idles/dances. No change to the built-in character path. +15 tests (animation-retarget: buildBoneRebind Mixamo↔Tripo + unriggable/partial-degrade, remapTrackName, retargetClip clone/drop semantics, collectTrackBoneNames); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-TJhG/R3f/X/xQ2Vyz46wau2p95XzYMo0SlBiLXfymvg=; 4052 tests / 336 files.

Prior: v0.2.781-alpha - HEARTBEAT SOURCE OF TRUTH: settings > Heartbeat looked dead + OFF for anyone who wasn't a signed-in admin, and stayed stale even for admins after out-of-band changes, because `_syncServerBeacon()` was only called on `EV.NOSTR_LOGIN`. The ADR-0094 server beacon publishes 24/7 from a server-owned key — the UI must read that as truth, not the client-only heartbeat status. This ship keeps the existing `effectiveHeartbeat = _beacon.state.enabled ? 'live' : hb` override in `_homepageStubState` and fixes _when_ `_beacon.state` gets populated: (1) main.js kicks a boot-time `_syncServerBeacon()` at module init so ANYONE loading the page sees the honest state, not just admins after login. (2) the existing rAF `_shellTick` re-syncs the beacon every ~600 frames (~10s at 60fps) via a new `_beaconSyncFrame` throttle var beside `_heartbeatFrame` — no `setTimeout`, catches server restarts / external toggles / `lastPublishedAt` drift. (3) `_syncServerBeacon` now repaints the active settings tab when a sync completes while the panel is open, so the Heartbeat pill updates without a tab-switch. The NOSTR_LOGIN call site stays in place (still valuable for immediate-post-login refresh). No new architecture — three anchors added to an existing pure sync function. +7 tests (v0.2.781-heartbeat-server-truth source-lock: throttle var, boot-time sync, shell-tick re-sync, NOSTR_LOGIN preserved, panel repaint hook, effectiveHeartbeat override intact, client-tick still bails on server enabled); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-53gm/4anzHpZtzuC0mX8ZDa4OWzOAIZXPiE9H9xrzXo=; 4032 tests / 335 files.

Prior: v0.2.780-alpha - CHAR FORGE RIG VERDICT IN-UI: v0.2.779 made the .glb upload validator-first (inspectGlb → assessRig before any Blossom upload/NIP-07 prompt) but surfaced the rig verdict only as a one-shot toast. This ship wires the assessed rig into the Character tab so the player sees it inline: (1) main.js now stores a compact summary (verdict + convention + boneCount + first note) via a new _summarizeRig() on _characterForgeState.rig as soon as the file bytes are inspected, and passes `rig:` into renderCharacterForgePanel. (2) characterForgePanel.js adds a _rigVerdict() status line — "Rig OK" (green · convention · N bones) for riggable, "Rig warning" (red, with the assessment's first note) for partial/unknown-convention/no-bones — rendered both during the 'creating' state (above "Working…") and in the 'found' summary (below the card). (3) index.html adds .cf-rig/.cf-rig-ok/.cf-rig-warn styles reusing the existing settings design tokens. No new architecture — the existing assessRig result just now surfaces in the panel. +9 tests (5 character-forge-panel rig-verdict render states: riggable/partial/no-bones/found/no-verdict; +4 v0.2.780-regression source-lock: state field + _summarizeRig + rig pass-through + cf-rig CSS); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-LpiYJ5Hz4ANcFSidE58xBsaGPwxyWwOH1VgCrtWPH64=; 4030 tests / 334 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.779-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE: UPLOAD-ONLY (Bug M pt.1): the Settings→Character tab still showed the curated Chiefmonkey + Nostrich roster cards ("select-preset") as the primary entry point, and both the ".glb upload" and "Create with AI" cards were greyed out (disabled) until Nostr login — so a player could not actually make their own character. This ship removes the preset roster and enables both creation paths, with the upload implemented first. (1) characterForgePanel.js drops the preset grid (chiefmonkey/nostrich cards) and the logged-out disabled gate; the tab now renders only the always-enabled Upload + Create-with-AI cards, behind a "Sign in with Nostr to save" banner. (2) New src/engine/character/glbInspect.js — a pure, node-safe GLB reader (12-byte header magic/version, JSON-chunk walk, node/bone extraction via skins[].joints, 50 MiB cap) that makes the upload validator-first: _uploadCustomMesh now reads the file bytes and rejects a malformed/oversized file BEFORE any Blossom upload or NIP-07 prompt, then runs assessRig() on the extracted bone names (a valid-but-unriggable GLB still uploads, surfacing a toast warning). (3) Removed the now-dead preset write half: _createOwnCharacter + the select-preset delegated handler + the characterPresets import in main.js (the characterPresets.js data module and its tests remain — character-publish still exercises presetToManifest). The "Create with AI" card stays a LOCAL MOCK (Generate demo) but is no longer gated on login. +16 tests (11 glb-inspect: header/chunk/bone parsing; magic/size/no-JSON/corrupt/no-bones guardrails; ArrayBuffer + typed-array input; glbInspect→assessRig riggable/no-bones integration; +6 v0.2.779-regression source-lock: no preset grid / no prescribed write half in main.js / validator-before-network wiring / .glb-only picker; character-forge-panel trimmed 14→13 to lock the roster-less always-enabled screen); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-pGNGHKWd6gerhrTjsjP7t9JuOPv9MKCG2zzBrRk71co=; 4021 tests / 333 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.778-alpha - HARD-FREEZE HARDENING (Bug L): the title screen froze hard twice in one day — nothing clickable, hard refresh (Shift+Ctrl+R) did nothing, only closing + reopening the tab recovered. The user entered the arena then returned to the title screen; per ADR-0098 the 3D loop (NPC walk + bot angle-lerp) keeps running warm behind the menu, and a hard-freeze-that-survives-reload is the signature of a SYNCHRONOUS infinite loop on the main thread. Root cause class: angle normalisation used `while (d > Math.PI) d -= 2*Math.PI` loops (napNpc walk + botNetState._lerpAngle). Those are correct for finite angles but infinite-loop FOREVER if the value ever becomes ±Infinity/NaN — subtracting a finite 2π from ±Infinity is still ±Infinity, so the loop never exits, blocking the main thread permanently (no click lands, no hard refresh can recover it, the task never yields back to the browser). A poisoned GLB-animation quaternion or an upstream divide-by-zero could produce such a value. Fix: (1) new `src/engine/math/angle.js` `normalizeAngle(d)` — a single-shot, O(1), non-finite-safe wrap (`!Number.isFinite` → 0; double-modulo `%` so it mathematically CANNOT loop), replacing BOTH `while` loops (botNetState._lerpAngle + napNpc walk). (2) `input.wrapAngle` now guards non-finite input to 0 (defense-in-depth). (3) New `src/engine/diagnostics/freezeWatchdog.js` — an off-thread Web Worker (blob) stall monitor: it pings the main thread on an interval and warns (with a timestamp + last-responsive stage label) when the round-trip exceeds a stall threshold, so the NEXT freeze is observed in real time even though no async API inside the renderer can see a synchronous infinite loop. `setFreezeStage(label)` is a no-op-safe window write stamped at coarse boundaries (`loop-update`/`loop-idle` in loop.js, `napNpc-walk` in the NPC walk, `bot-lerp` in botNetState) so the last-responsive-stage signal is meaningful. `installFreezeWatchdog()` is wired early in main.js and is idempotent + browser-only (no-op under node/test). regression-check setTimeout allowlist extended for the worker's contained off-thread heartbeat. +12 tests (v0.2.778-regression: normalizeAngle unit behaviour — non-finite→0, wrap into [-π,π), O(1) no-loop shape; comment-aware source sweep proving NO angle-normalisation `while` loop remains anywhere in src; botNetState/napNpc/input use the safe helper; freezeWatchdog exports + blob-worker ping/stall-warn + idempotency/browser-only; wiring in main.js + loop.js stage stamps); CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-/VcLFUEeTHsLQHd/ysr9s4ggVRgQeonxDP4Vj6BKKUk=; 4005 tests / 331 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.777-alpha - SW CACHE-BUSTING HARDENING (Bug K): a returning player's entry buttons armed (turned green on card-pick / Nostr login) but clicking ENTER did nothing — twice (v0.2.775 + v0.2.776), each time resolved only by a manual hard-refresh with ?nuked=1. Root cause: the stale-SW cache-duality self-heal relied on (a) the reactive ENTER-ARENA dupe-boot guard, which only fires when the lazy arenaRuntime chunk re-invokes main.js, and (b) a controllerchange auto-reload gated on window.__toriiEnterReady — a flag set as soon as main.js WIRES the buttons, not when the game actually enters. So the exact "green-but-dead" state (buttons wired yet the arena boot path stranded on a stale `?v=` stamp) suppressed the very reload that would have healed it. Fix ships two layers. (1) Proactive version-mismatch self-heal: sw.js now exposes its CACHE_VERSION via a TORII_SW_PING→TORII_SW_VERSION message round-trip (answering with the bare semantic version, CACHE_VERSION minus the 'tq-' prefix) and broadcasts its version on activate (after clients.claim()); index.html embeds window.__TORII_APP_VERSION (bump-ver.sh keeps it in lockstep with src/config.js VERSION), pings the current controller for its version, and self-heals immediately on mismatch — before any user interaction — rather than betting on controllerchange timing. The self-heal body is now a single idempotent toriiSelfHealStaleShell() (guarded by window.__toriiNuking + the ?nuked=1 URL pin), shared by BOTH the __toriiShellRan double-boot fingerprint and the version-mismatch check. (2) Fixed the controllerchange gate: the auto-reload now keys on window.__toriiEntered (set in main.js only after _arena.enter() actually runs) instead of __toriiEnterReady, so a title-screen visitor with wired-but-stranded buttons is healed by the reload, while a genuinely live 3D runtime is still never disrupted mid-game (ADR-0106 manual-only updates preserved). The legacy __toriiEnterReady flag is intentionally left intact for the inline click-fallback stand-down. +10 tests (v0.2.777-regression: SW bound CACHE_VERSION/_broadcastVersion/activate hook; TORII_SW_PING→TORII_SW_VERSION answer carries SW_APP_VERSION; index.html embeds __TORII_APP_VERSION, self-heals on reported mismatch, pings controller, shares ONE idempotent toriiSelfHealStaleShell; controllerchange gate keys on __toriiEntered not __toriiEnterReady; main.js raises __toriiEntered after _arena.enter(); legacy __toriiEnterReady fallback preserved); sw-app-shell double-mount-gate test updated to __toriiEntered; CSP inline-script hash bumped to sha256-MpXjgq5uXjReTK5R2OX7xVmxSfixi1NudlmNv03mWuc=; 3993 tests / 330 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.776-alpha - BUTTONS + MODAL CLOSE (Bug I + J): the title-screen entry UX was misleading — the guest button carried a pre-selected character card ("poo-poo head") and a hardcoded orange gradient even before the user picked, and the login button was hardcoded solid green even before Nostr sign-in; users couldn't tell which action was ready to fire. Separately (Bug J), the Settings panel could freeze half-open if the user clicked off it while it was mid-transition, leaving no visible way to dismiss it. Fix ships a state-driven CTA model on both entry buttons + an always-visible ✕ close button on Settings as a safety net. UI copy: option 1 renamed "NEW PLAYER" → "ENTER AS GUEST"; option 3 renamed "RETURNING?" → "LOGIN NOSTR". Neither character card is pre-selected on load (`aria-checked="false"` + no `.selected`). Both entry buttons ship with `data-armed="false"`: CSS via `#btn-enter-nap[data-armed]` + `#btn-nostr-centre[data-armed]` attribute selectors renders unarmed = grey outline (guest also `pointer-events:none` + `disabled` + `aria-disabled="true"`), armed = solid green (#22c55e) with white text. Guest arms via new `_armGuestEnterButton()` called at the end of `_selectGuestCharacter()`: strips `disabled`, flips `data-armed="true"`, `aria-disabled="false"`, relabels to "ENTER". Login arms via new exported `armLoginEnterButton(doc)` in `src/engine/ui/loginBootstrap.js`, subscribed to `EV.NOSTR_LOGIN` (fires on successful `nostrLogin()` — either NIP-98 or `getPublicKey` path); uses `setAttribute('data-armed', 'true')` (not `dataset.armed`) so fake DOM test doubles don't throw and crash the login round-trip via `emit()`. Armed login button routes clicks through `window.__toriiEnterArenaFromTitle` (an adapter exposed by main.js that re-enables the guest button if the user never picked a card and delegates to `elNapBtn.click()`) so both entry paths land in the ONE `_bootArena` code path — v0.2.775's dupe-boot guard + self-heal wraps both without duplication. Bug J: `src/engine/settings/settingsPanel.js` `_build()` now injects a `.ts-close-x` element (absolute top-right, z-index 210, `aria-label="Close settings"`, calls `closeSettingsPanel()` on click); card gets `position:relative` so the ✕ pins to its corner regardless of tab; scrim ESC path and outside-click close still work unchanged. +18 tests (v0.2.776-regression: button copy, no pre-selected card, initial data-armed=false + disabled + aria-disabled, CSS state model, `_armGuestEnterButton()` shape and call site, `EV.NOSTR_LOGIN` subscription in loginBootstrap, armed routing through `window.__toriiEnterArenaFromTitle`, `_selectGuestCharacter` rejects unknown chars; Bug J: source-level ✕ button lock, DOM render check, click closes panel, absolute positioning + z-index, onClose callback fires once, ESC still works, inside-card click doesn't close); CSP inline-script hash bumped to `sha256-hmdQl24FsD6Ux87X8COo/cKeAvuFenBRyUKk9+TOolI=` for the new version comment in index.html; 3983 tests / 329 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.775-alpha - DUPLICATE-BOOT GRACEFUL RECOVERY (Bug H): the v0.2.773 double-boot guard threw a fatal error at module-eval time on any duplicate main.js invocation, correctly preventing the strobing/double-mount regression on initial page load but user-hostile on ENTER-ARENA click. Root cause: the arenaRuntime lazy chunk statically imports back into torii-entry (rolldown cross-chunk share pattern) — under stale-SW cache duality serving a mismatched `?v=` stamp for that back-reference, the arenaRuntime import path re-evaluates the whole main.js top-level, hits the guard's `throw new Error('torii-boot: duplicate module invocation suppressed')`, and the ENTER handler's generic catch surfaces it as `⚠ Arena failed to load — torii-boot: duplicate module invocation suppressed`. User reported: login works, ENTER produces that message, cannot enter the arena. Fix: (1) the guard now tags its thrown error with `.code = 'TORII_DUPLICATE_BOOT'` so callers can recognize the signature without brittle message parsing; the human-readable text stays stable for ADR-0107 log-grep contracts. (2) The ENTER-ARENA bootstrap catch recognizes the dupe-boot signature (by code, with message-regex fallback for re-thrown/stripped errors) and swaps the raw "Arena failed to load" surface for a self-describing `⚠ Stale build detected — refreshing to sync…` toast + triggers `_selfHealStaleShellAndReload()`. (3) New `_selfHealStaleShellAndReload()` helper mirrors the existing `__toriiShellRan` self-heal already inlined in index.html: unregisters all SW registrations, purges Cache Storage, `location.replace()`s with `?nuked=1` pinned so the reload cannot loop; idempotent per document via `window.__toriiNuking`; dependency-free so it can run even when module state is torn down. The guard's fatal throw is preserved — softening to silent early-return would reintroduce the strobing/double-mount regression the guard exists to prevent, because top-level side-effect calls live all through main.js not just this block. +12 tests (v0.2.775-regression: source-level assertions on guard's tagged error code/message, ENTER catch's dupe-boot branch, self-heal helper's SW-unregister/cache-purge/nuked=1/location.replace/idempotency shape); 3965 tests / 328 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.774-alpha - RELAY HEALTH + SPREAD-THE-LOVE (Bug G): the Nostr relay layer was silently punishing weakness — each transient `WebSocket connection failed` from a single relay in the pool surfaced as browser-log noise despite the pool's own fallback fully absorbing it, so users could not tell "one flaky relay in a healthy pool" from "the whole ecosystem is down"; there was no way to see which relays were actually performing, and the default set skewed toward the biggest few relays (nos.lol, damus, primal, nostr.band, purplepages). Fix ships a pure-data relay-health tracker + a new Settings→Relays health section + curated defaults broadened to 7 (added `wss://relay.snort.social` + `wss://nostr.mom`, WRITE-probed 2026-09-05, spreading load onto newer-but-established indie relays instead of piling further onto the incumbents). New `src/engine/telemetry/relayHealth.js` (252 lines) — LS-backed (`torii.relayHealth.v1`), MAX_SESSIONS=30 rolling window; per relay {opens, opensFailed, closes, messages, latencyMsSum, latencySamples, lastSeen, lastFail, failStreak, sessions[]}; pure API (recordOpen/OpenFail/Close/Message/rotateSession/readHealth/readRelayHealth/resetHealth) with opts.storage injection for tests; validates wss:// or ws:// only; tolerates missing/broken LS; deep-clones on read. Wired into ALL 6 live WS sites (nostr.js's fetchProfileProgressive/relayReq/publishEvent + plebeianRelay.js's subscribeAuction/subscribeByAuthor/fetchProfiles): connectStart timer + openRecorded flag on each socket, recordOpen(url, latency) on open, recordMessage(url) on frame, recordOpenFail(url, code) on error/close-before-open, recordClose(url) on close-after-open, all inside try{}catch{} so telemetry never breaks Nostr. rotateSession() runs once per real boot (top-level in main.js under the double-boot guard) so the sparkline gets one bar per session. Settings→Relays tab (renderRelayPanel state extended to `{ isOwner, nodeRelays, nodeRelaysInput, relayHealth? }`) renders a new "Relay health" section under the relay list: per-relay rows sorted best-performers-first by success rate then opens desc, showing opens/total, success %, avg connect latency, message count, and a 60×16 inline-SVG sparkline of opens-per-session across the rolling window; failStreak≥3 marks the row `data-degraded=true` for future CSS. Empty state hint "No relay activity yet — connect to a world and stats will start recording here." Silence: no explicit `console.error/warn` was ever emitted for single-relay WS-open fail in the codebase (the browser's native `WebSocket connection failed` is unavoidable and unchanged); the structured tracker replaces our own noise. +39 tests (25 relay-health API/persistence/rotation/latency-avg/URL-validation/LS-corruption + 14 v0.2.774-regression: defaults extended additive, no dup, no wine, frozen shape, E2E record→read, panel header/rows/empty-state, back-compat when relayHealth undefined); 3953 tests / 327 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.773-alpha - DOUBLE-BOOT GUARD (Bug F): a stale service-worker cache served an older `torii-entry.js?v=<oldstamp>` alongside the freshly fetched `torii-entry.js?v=<newstamp>` in the SAME tab — the two module URLs are distinct entries in the browser's ES module cache, so both modules ran their top-level side effects and BOTH booted the arena (strobing render, duplicate self in mirror, two WS auth events with different NIP-42 challenges, two `[playerModel] loaded`/`[grass-build]`/`[pointer-lock flap]` log lines in the same session, 503 on one of the two competing keepalives). Fix ships THREE defensive layers so future users cannot hit this on subsequent deploys: (1) src/main.js top-level guard — checks `window.__toriiBooted` and THROWS on the second module invocation, so only the first module wires timers/listeners/render loop; (2) index.html shell self-heal — if the inline bootstrap scope ever runs a second time in one document (`window.__toriiShellRan`), it unregisters every service worker, purges every cache, and hard-reloads once with `?nuked=1` locking the loop to a single attempt; (3) build-time injection — vite.config.js wraps the injected entry `import()` in a `__toriiShellImported` idempotency gate, and tools/csp.mjs's ENTRY_IMPORT_LINE fallback carries the same guard so root-fallback deploys aren't a stealth regression. CSP inline-bootstrap sha256 recomputed and pinned. +14 tests (v0.2.773-regression: 5 module-guard locks + 6 shell self-heal locks + 2 build-injection guard locks + 1 ENTRY_IMPORT_LINE fallback lock); 3914 tests / 325 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.772-alpha - FP-BODY FEET + PER-CHARACTER POV (Bug D + Bug E): Bug D (v0.2.771 regression) — looking straight down showed no feet because the FP-body neck-clip plane tracked the CAMERA world Y (added in v0.2.112 to follow the lookDownEyeY drop), so at a downward pitch the camera dipped and pulled the down-facing clip plane above the feet, culling them. Fix: pin the clip constant to the PARENT rig world Y (playerObj) plus the fixed chest offset (EYE − NECK_CLIP_DROP), which stays anchored on jumps/crouches/terrain but is independent of pitch — feet always render, neck stump still hidden. Camera-Y path remains as a one-frame unparented-transient fallback so the constant is never NaN during hot-swap. Bug E (small feature) — POV now scales with the loaded character. Poo poo head is authored shorter than canonical 1.7 m; the FP loader now measures the headless GLB bounds (min/maxY) at spawn, derives characterEye = clamp(meshHeight − 0.10, MIN 1.10 m, EYE 1.7 m), exports getCharacterEyeOffset() (characterEye − EYE, always ≤ 0), and player.js adds that delta to camera.position.y after lookDownEyeY(pitch). Physics EYE (engine/entities/player.js) is UNCHANGED — bots still aim at the true eye, spawn/capsule/respawn geometry is untouched, and peers still see each character at its authored height; only the LOCAL POV drops for shorter characters (guests looking at full-height players now look UP; a full-height player looking at a poo-poo-head guest looks DOWN — the intended asymmetry). Offset resets to 0 on hot-swap. +8 tests (v0.2.772-regression: parent-anchored neck clip + camera-Y fallback + NECK_CLIP_DROP lock + getCharacterEyeOffset export + clamp lock + hot-swap reset + player.js wiring + physics EYE lock); 3900 tests / 324 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.771-alpha - REMOVE sharp (arena-ws still could not start): the sharp native binary + platform optional deps were a fragile VPS install — v0.2.770's manifest `npm install --omit=dev` resolved ^0.35.0 to 0.35.4 (not the tested 0.35.3), and 0.35.4's @img/sharp-linux-x64 exports map was broken (require('@img/sharp-linux-x64') → 'No exports main defined', which made sharp.cjs hit `err.code.endsWith` with err.code undefined and crash at startup). Rather than keep chasing a native binary, the optional WebP texture-compression pass was DROPPED from headlessGlb (prune + draco remain — the dominant GLB size win); sharp removed as a direct dependency; build:server now aliases sharp + ndarray-pixels to a no-op stub (tools/server-stubs/native-stub.js) so esbuild never inlines sharp.mjs (which would re-crash with createRequire(undefined)); manifest + externals are now ws + draco3d only. Verified: bundle has no sharp/ndarray/createRequire, server smoke-tests to 'listening', and headless-glb-core happy paths still author valid headless variants (texture compression absent, geometry + Draco intact). +1 test (server-bundle-external sharp-gone lock); 3892 tests / 323 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.770-alpha - SERVER RUNTIME DEPS FIX (arena-ws still could not start): v0.2.769 marked sharp external, which got past the createRequire(import.meta.url) crash but then failed with 'Cannot find module: sharp' — torii-suite's installer writes the arena-ws runtime manifest (dist/package.json) with ONLY `ws`, so the VPS `npm install --omit=dev` never installed sharp or any other externalized module. Found a latent sibling bug too: draco3d was still INLINED, so its draco_*.wasm files would be read from the wrong __dirname when the headless-GLB endpoint actually ran (decoder/encoder init). Fix: build:server now externalizes ws + sharp + draco3d, and a new tools/write-server-runtime-manifest.mjs emits dist/package.json (ws/sharp/draco3d) BEFORE bundling so the suite installer installs every externalized runtime dep; sharp promoted to a direct dependency (was only transitive via ndarray-pixels). Verified locally: bundle requires sharp + draco3d externally, dist/package.json lists all three, the server smoke-tests to 'listening', draco decoder+encoder load their WASM, and sharp 0.35.3 loads natively. +2 tests (server-bundle-external expanded to 4 locks, including manifest↔esbuild-flag agreement); 3891 tests / 323 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.769-alpha - SERVER CANNOT START FIX (sharp native module was inlined into the arena-ws bundle): v0.2.767's headless-GLB feature (server/character/headlessGlb.js) pulled @gltf-transform/functions → ndarray-pixels → sharp into the server bundle. esbuild's cjs output rewrote sharp's top-level import.meta.url to an empty object, so createRequire(import_meta.url) threw ERR_INVALID_ARG_VALUE (undefined) at startup and torii-arena-ws crashed in a restart loop — the WS backend has been DOWN since the v0.2.767 deploy (both v0.2.767 and v0.2.768 deploys hit the same crash; the frontend served the new version but the server never became ready on 127.0.0.1:8788). Fix: build:server now marks sharp external (--external:sharp) so the native dual CJS/ESM module resolves via require() at runtime instead of being inlined. +2 tests (tests/server-bundle-external.test.js source contract: external flags + sharp reachable as a non-dev production dep); 3889 tests / 323 files.

Prior: Current version: v0.2.768-alpha - POST-v0.2.767 PLAYTEST REGRESSION FIXES (3 bugs): (1) Bug A — sequential re-entry stuck the first character. ensureArenaReady() early-returned on _arenaBootstrapped and never re-seated the character or re-ran the loaders, so a guest who picked the poo-poo-head card, went Home, then re-picked nostrich still saw the guest FP body + 3P model. Fix: pull the seat block into _seatCharacterIntoArena(arena) and run it on every entry, plus a new reloadCharacterAssets() arena API that re-loads loadPlayerModel + loadFirstPersonBody from the freshly-seated character. (2) Bug B — FP body bled into the NAP mirror frame close to the surface. Verdict: the mirror layer plumbing was already correct (reflection camera disables layer 2); the artefact was the layer-2 FP body drawing in near-field between the eye and the mirror. Fix: firstPersonBody now owns a single _applyVisibility() that folds the debug-fly hide AND a new mirror-proximity hide (_mirrorHidden within MIRROR_HIDE_DIST=3m) so the body never overlaps the mirror when you stand in front of it. (3) Bug C — phantom static chiefmonkey peer. A parked/backgrounded tab kept its socket alive (the 15s PING counted as activity, so the 90s idle sweep never fired) and an ANONYMOUS session with a stale cached session token auto-authed as a prior chiefmonkey identity, surfacing as a static owner clone next to the resident NPC; the client also silently coerced unknown peer characters to chiefmonkey. Fix: leaveToTitle() disconnects the MP socket (resumeFromTitle() re-dials it), the stale token is gated on isLoggedIn so anon playthroughs never auto-auth, peer-character resolution (_resolvePeerCharacter) falls back to guest + warns (never chiefmonkey), and the server raises IDLE_DISCONNECT_MS to 15 min (PING no longer counts as gameplay activity) + downgrades invalid character keys to guest. +12 tests (tests/v0.2.768-regression.test.js source contract) + updated guest-peer-character lock; 3887 tests / 322 files.

Prior: v0.2.767-alpha - HEADLESS FP BODIES FOR CUSTOM + AI-GENERATED CHARACTERS: previously only the three built-in avatars (chiefmonkey/guest/nostrich) had pre-authored *-headless.glb assets, so anyone uploading their own .glb (or generating one via Meshy) rendered with either their whole head clipped in front of the camera or no FP body at all. Now auto-authored server-side: new server/character/headlessGlb.js (gltf-transform + draco3d core — head-vertex removal by dominant skin weight, keep only Idle_02/Stylish_Walk_inplace/Running, Draco + WebP re-encode) is wired to POST /mp/character/headless (session-gated, 32 MiB cap, returns binary GLB + X-Headless-Sha256 header). Client wrapper (src/engine/character/authorHeadless.js) posts the raw upload and gets the authored blob; _uploadCustomMesh (main.js) uploads it to Blossom under the player's own NIP-98 auth, adds mesh.headlessHash to the kind-35100 character manifest, and seats _ownCharacterHeadlessUrl. New playerModel.setCustomHeadlessUrl/getCustomHeadlessUrl seams + firstPersonBody.js prefers the custom URL when set (external:true bypasses assetUrl). Fails soft — a mesh without a recognisable head joint still publishes without headlessHash instead of blocking upload. Deps: @gltf-transform/* + draco3d moved from devDependencies to dependencies (server code path). +16 tests (headless-glb-core / author-headless-client / character-manifest headlessHash validation, including a no-head-joint failure-mode lock); 3875 tests / 321 files.

Prior: v0.2.766-alpha - GUEST + NOSTRICH HEADLESS FP BODIES: guests and nostrich are no longer disembodied in first person. Authored public/guest-headless.glb and public/nostrich-headless.glb via a new tools/headless-glb.mjs (gltf-transform surgery: removes every vertex whose dominant skin weight is Head/head_end/headfront, keeps only the three FP clips Idle_02/Stylish_Walk_inplace/Running, then Draco + WebP re-encodes — nostrich drops 3.6MB → 1.3MB via WebP texture compression). firstPersonBody.js now resolves the current character through a FP_BODIES map (chiefmonkey/guest/nostrich) and plays that character's own idle/walk/run clips; custom/Create-with-AI meshes still hide the FP body. No new tests (guest-character-picker lock updated + on-disk asset existence); 3859 tests / 319 files.

Prior: v0.2.765-alpha - SERVICE-WORKER DOUBLE-MOUNT FIX: a returning player whose open page was claimed by a freshly-deployed SW (skipWaiting + clients.claim) hit a mid-game controllerchange auto-reload that left the old build's rAF/WebGL loop alive beside the new one — duplicating NPCs/bots and breaking the mirror. index.html now gates that reload on the app NOT having booted yet (bails when window.__toriiEnterReady is set), so a live game is never yanked; the update applies on the next clean reload (ADR-0106 manual-only). +1 test (sw-app-shell double-mount guard) and the CSP inline-bootstrap sha256 in tools/csp.mjs is recomputed; 3859 tests / 319 files.

Prior: v0.2.764-alpha - GUEST-PEER CHARACTER FIX + STEP C FOUNDATION: (1) fixed the 'multiple versions of chiefmonkey' bug — MP_PEER_CHARACTERS (arenaRuntime.js) had no 'guest' entry, so every peer/bot whose character was 'guest' fell back to chiefmonkey and rendered as a teal-monkey clone army; added the guest entry (guest-master.glb, master clip table) and added 'guest' to useMasterTable. (2) fixed 'not the character I chose' — a returning player's kind-35100 own mesh overrode an explicit guest-torso-card pick; main.js now tracks _guestCharChosen so the picked guest avatar wins over the own mesh. (3) Step C foundation (server-side, tested, not yet wired to a server endpoint): server/character/meshGenerationVendor.js (real Meshy/Tripo Bearer-auth text/image-to-3D fetch adapters, node-safe + injectable) and server/character/meshGenerationCharge.js (routstr + Cashu NUT-05 melt charge seam, fail-closed pricing). +5 tests (guest-peer-character) + 15 tests (vendor + charge); 3858 tests / 319 files.

Prior: v0.2.763-alpha - THREE-OPTION LOGIN + GUEST HEADLESS-BODY FIX: (1) The title screen becomes a three-option login — NEW PLAYER (enter as guest + pick a character via two torso-thumbnail cards: nostrich / poo-poo-head), CREATE WITH AI (kept on login for now, opens the settings Character Forge where the Step-B mock lives), and RETURNING? (LOGIN WITH NOSTR auto-seats the npub's kind-35100 character so the avatar follows you). The guest picker stores the choice in main.js (_pendingGuestChar) and seats it via _arena.setCharacter() before boot; new torso assets public/ui/char-{nostrich,guest}-torso.png were rendered from the GLBs via tools/char-torso-render.html + tools/char-torso-screenshot.mjs. (2) Fixed the guest headless-body bug — firstPersonBody.js hardcoded chiefmonkey-headless.glb for every character, so guests/nostrich saw chiefmonkey's teal body (and its layer-2 mesh ghosted); it now loads only for chiefmonkey. (3) Nostr .glb-on-npub research: no NIP exists for 3D avatars — the correct pattern is a Blossom sha256 blob + NIP-94 kind:1063 (m: model/gltf-binary) referenced by the kind-35100 manifest. +5 tests (guest-character-picker); 3838 tests / 316 files.
