Prior: v0.2.762-alpha - CREATE WITH AI MOCK (character-creation Step B): the Character Forge's disabled "Create with AI" placeholder is now a living LOCAL mock proving the full prompt → thinking → validate → verdict loop with zero network/payment/signing. New src/engine/character/meshGenerationMock.js composes the REAL seams — planGeneration() builds/validates the request, a canned generic-humanoid (Meshy-style, 24-bone) fixture result is gated by validateGeneratedMesh() — so only the external generator's output is mocked (it passes assessRig 'riggable'). characterForgePanel.js swaps the "Coming soon" card for a prompt textarea + "Generate (demo)" action and a dedicated ai-flow view (thinking / accepted / rejected), driven by a new _forgeAIState in main.js with the thinking→done transition riding the shell rAF tick (no window timers). Real Meshy/Tripo + routstr/Cashu wiring is Step C (the injectable executor seam is already reserved for it). +14 tests (character-mesh-generation-mock + character-forge-panel ai-flow locks); 3833 tests / 315 files.

Prior: v0.2.761-alpha - GUEST-MASTER ANIMATION RETARGET: the master animation-library's full 18 clips are baked onto the guest avatar via offline world-delta retargeting (tools/glb_retarget.py) — both rigs share the Meshy 24-bone names, so idle / stylish-walk / walk-back / run / run-forward firing / run-back / jump / reload / hit-react / knock-down / dance / victory-cheer / melee / two falls / walk-back-with-gun all landed as a straight bake. The guest asset becomes public/models/guest-master.glb (WebP texture + Draco mesh, 1.2 MB — 82% smaller than the 6.6 MB uncompressed bake), replacing the sparse 3-clip guest-head4.glb (5.9 MB, deleted). playerModel.js's 'guest' CHARACTER gains the full 15-slot master anim table and joins the GAME_STATE_TO_CLIP branch alongside chiefmonkey/nostrich, so the anonymous default now has identical locomotion + combat coverage. Infra (no client-version bump, PR #131): corrected the ADR-0101 deploy-hook request path (stale /opt/torii-quest → /apps/quest = the runner's APPS_ROOT) and added ops/install-deploy-ssh.sh so GitHub-triggered deploys stop silently no-op'ing. No new tests; 3819 tests / 314 files.

Prior: v0.2.760-alpha - GUEST-FIRST ENTRY + GUEST AVATAR: the title screen no longer makes visitors pick a character — everyone enters anonymously as a GUEST with the new guest avatar (guest-head4.glb, the Meshy head4 re-export). Step 1 is now ENTER AS GUEST (the #btn-enter-nap id is kept so the existing entry handler needs no logic change), Step 2 is the optional LOGIN WITH NOSTR; the SELECT YOUR CHARACTER heading and the chiefmonkey/nostrich .char-btn buttons are removed (character selection/forging now lives in Settings). playerModel gains a 'guest' CHARACTER and defaults to it: Meshy clips Walking/Running/baselayer map through the generic anim branch — full locomotion/combat coverage is deferred until the master animation-library is retargeted onto guest-head4.glb (the nostrich-master path). The server AUTH character whitelist is extracted to server/auth/characterKeys.js and now accepts 'guest' alongside the legacy presets and a 64-hex Character-Forge mesh hash. +5 tests (tests/character-keys.test.js); 3819 tests / 314 files.

Prior: v0.2.759-alpha - GENERAL HUMANOID BONE CLASSIFIER (ADR-0107): the Character Forge validator now maps any humanoid rig, not just Mixamo/Biped. Added TRIPO_BONE_MAP (Hip / L_Thigh / L_Calf / L_Upperarm / L_Forearm / L_Hand / Waist / L_Clavicle) and GENERIC_HUMANOID_BONE_MAP (Hips / Spine / LeftArm / LeftForeArm / LeftUpLeg / LeftLeg / lowercase neck — Meshy's prefix-stripped Mixamo style, also the Unreal Mannequin), plus a conservative classifyBone() keyword+side heuristic as a per-bone fallback for arbitrary future generators (word prefixes LeftArm, short prefixes L_Thigh, Unreal suffixes thigh_l). detectConvention now scores over all tables instead of prefix-sniffing; mapBonesToRoles resolves per-bone across every table then the heuristic; rigAssessment verdict is mapping-based (riggable when every required role maps, regardless of convention label). Unblocks the anonymous-entry guest avatars (raw Tripo head + Meshy head4 re-export) and the "Create with AI" backend wiring with no further bone-name work. +10 test locks (Tripo/Meshy fixtures use the real guest-avatar GLB bone names); 3814 tests / 313 files.

Prior: v0.2.758-alpha - KAMI DEV MENU SYNC + POINTER-LOCK FLAP DIAGNOSTIC: (1) The Kami dev panel (switches) was delayed on show/hide — the old 1Hz refresh throttle left it appearing/vanishing up to a second after the emagake + ema chat, breaking the "all at once" expectation. Removed the throttle; the driver now re-renders every frame but is still gated by the snapshot-key change detector (zero DOM churn on unchanged frames), so the panel syncs within the same frame as Kami enter/exit. (2) Added a bounded pointer-lock flap diagnostic for the playtest report "cursor switches to mouse and back when firing in the NAP zone" — logs a concise console.warn on rapid lock↔unlock cycles while playing (max 4) so the next report names the trigger. No new tests; 3804 tests / 313 files.

Prior: v0.2.757-alpha - STICKER BAKED-DECAL LOCAL-SPACE PARENTING FIX: the baked (DecalGeometry) stickers STILL wouldn't stick to static meshes (trees, the torii gate, the sats symbol) even after the DoubleSide fix — they stuck to the NPC (plane path) but not to objects. Root cause: the decal's WORLD-space vertices were parented to the hit mesh with a quaternion-inverse + local-position counteraction that only cancelled rotation/translation (not scale), double-transforming the decal out of view. Fix: bake the DecalGeometry's vertices/ normals into the hit mesh's LOCAL space (applyMatrix4 of inverse matrixWorld) and parent with an identity transform, so it inherits position/rotation/scale exactly and conforms to the surface. Baked mode (switch OFF) now sticks to objects and the NPC alike, so the A/B is finally testable. +2 test locks (local-space parenting asserts); 3804 tests / 313 files.

Prior: v0.2.756-alpha - STICKER BAKED-DECAL VISIBILITY + KAMI LEAK/ESC FIXES: four fixes from the playtest report. (1) Baked (DecalGeometry) stickers were INVISIBLE with the Force-Plane toggle OFF — the baked material used the default FrontSide culling, but DecalGeometry clips in WORLD space and is parented to the hit mesh with a pre-inverted quaternion, so its winding doesn't reliably face the camera; back-facing triangles were culled and nothing stuck. The material is now built by createBakedDecalMaterial() with side:DoubleSide (matching the plane path), so baked and plane render visibly and are finally comparable. (2) Esc wouldn't exit Kami while the ema note was open — it discarded only the note and left the rack up, reading as "Esc broken". Esc now discards the note AND exits Kami in ONE press (matching the ✕ button + the "ESC EXIT" badge). (3) The Kami "N EMA ON THE RACK" tray (#kami-tray) leaked as an empty amber-bordered pill at bottom-center (the "little orange box") because its cssText set display:flex, which overrode the [hidden] attribute — the same ADR-0027 gotcha as #kami-overlay; it now toggles style.display directly. (4) The owner-side dev menu (#torii-dev-menu) is now top-aligned with the vertically-centered emagake (top:27vh) instead of bottom-docked. New tests/sticker-baked-decal-material.test.js (4 locks) + 1 lock in pause-input.test.js; 3802 tests / 313 files.

Prior: v0.2.755-alpha - UPDATE-BUTTON DIAGNOSTIC + KAMI DEV-MENU CUTOFF FIX: (1) The update button stayed hidden despite "Latest v0.2.752" showing. The button gate is `admin && updateAvailable`; version-compare is correct, so the failure is runtime (likely capability probe adminPubkey null OR a timing miss). Added a one-shot console.warn when updateAvailable && !show that dumps {admin, adminPubkey, nostrPubkey, selfUpdate, latest, current} so the next report names the exact cause. (2) Kami dev menu (#torii-dev-menu) was cut off at the bottom: it was docked at top:calc(8vh + 3*(27vh + 10px)) ≈ 89vh, pushing its bottom off-screen on narrow windows. Now bottom-anchored (bottom:14px) with max-height + overflow-y so it never leaves the viewport. No new tests (diagnostic + CSS positioning); 3797 tests / 312 files unchanged.

Prior: v0.2.754-alpha - STICKER FIRING POLISH (muzzle origin + preload): (1) Stickers visibly LEFT THE PLAYER\u0027S FACE instead of the gun. fireStickerAtNpc(aim, ad) passed the AIM (camera) origin as BOTH the ray origin and the sprite spawn point, so the sticker popped out of the camera. Now fireStickerAtNpc(origin, dir, spawnOrigin) targets along the aim ray but spawns the sprite at the gun muzzle (arenaRuntime passes the gun `origin`), matching bullets (spawnBullet(origin, dir)). (2) The first few shots showed the PINK 0xff00ff fallback because _preloadTexture() only ran inside fireStickerAtNpc — the FTFF PNG was still streaming. Moved preload to tickStickerNpc() so it streams on arena entry (guarded, idempotent). Also confirmed via console the raycast crash culprit was Mesh:grass-instanced (procedural InstancedBufferGeometry, no position attribute — now correctly skipped). New tests/sticker-muzzle-origin.test.js (5 locks). 3797 tests / 312 files.

Prior: v0.2.753-alpha - STICKER RAYCAST CRASH FIX ("stickers not working" + arena firing): firing in the NAP zone threw, on EVERY shot, "Uncaught TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading \u0027getX\u0027)" from Three.js r184 Mesh.raycast → getVertexPosition() → fromBufferAttribute(geometry.attributes.position). A scene Mesh whose geometry has NO `position` attribute made _raycastScene\u0027s intersectObjects() throw mid-fire, aborting fireStickerAtNpc before any sticker could spawn (hence "nothing at all"). Fix (two layers): (1) _getMeshes() now SKIPS any mesh lacking geometry.attributes.position, warning once per offender so the culprit is named for root-cause follow-up; (2) _raycastScene() wraps both intersectObjects calls (static + instanced) in try/catch so a mesh disposed/rebuilt between the 2s cache refills can never sink a fire. New tests/sticker-raycast-position-guard.test.js (4 locks). 3792 tests / 311 files, 21/21 gates.

Prior: v0.2.752-alpha - SELF-VIEW STICKER ORBIT FIX (P-key "beneath ground"): pressing P dropped the orbit camera beneath the terrain/grass. Root cause: stickerSelfView._characterCenter computed the centre as mesh.getWorldPosition().y + (geometry.boundingBox.max.y - min.y)*0.5 — the LOCAL bind-pose Y span. For a Z-up GLB the local Y span is the character DEPTH not height, and for an Armature-scaled rig it is unscaled, so the centre landed at (or under) ground level. Fix: the centre now comes from the SKELETON torso bones (hips→spine, both mixamorig colon and no-colon forms), whose WORLD positions track the animated pose AND the player world transform exactly. Falls back to mesh world origin + 0.9 only for an unrigged/bone-less mesh. New source-level lock tests/sticker-self-view-center.test.js (4 tests). 3788 tests / 310 files, 21/21 gates.

Prior: v0.2.751-alpha - HEARTBEAT SETTINGS FIX + SETTINGS CLOSE-UX + PROFILE FORM REDESIGN. (1) Heartbeat: the Settings > Heartbeat tab showed OFF/red and couldn't be switched on even while the world was live — it rendered the raw client heartbeat status ('idle' → OFF) and its toggle (onPublishNode) ran a client-only publish that never mirrored to the ADR-0094 server beacon, unlike the in-game menu. Fix: extracted a shared _applyHeartbeatToggle(next) (the server-beacon-mirroring path) used by BOTH the menu's onToggleHeartbeat and the settings tab's onPublishNode, and _homepageStubState now applies the server-beacon override (effectiveHeartbeat = _beacon.state.enabled === true ? 'live' : hb) so the tab's status + toggle agree with the menu. (2) Close UX: removed every ✕ close button from the settings panel (top-right ts-close-x) and the Access tab (is-close) — the panel now closes only on backdrop click ('click anywhere outside') and ESC; the dead data-action='close' handler and ts-close-x/is-close CSS were removed. (3) Profile form: each .pf-row is now a two-column grid (label left-aligned in a 160px cell, input/textarea in the adjacent right cell), and inputs use a warm desaturated 'pulp' terracotta tone (complementary to the green success accent) with 12px rounding + roomier padding — replacing the previous UNSTYLED native-white boxes (the .pf-* classes referenced no CSS after the v0.2.739 design-system consolidation orphaned them). New source-level lock tests/main-settings-heartbeat-beacon.test.js (5 tests); the instance-settings close-button assertion now pins no-close-button. 3784 tests / 309 files, 21/21 gates.

Prior: v0.2.750-alpha - NEVER FORCE-UPDATE (ADR-0106): deploys are manual-only. The ADR-0101/0105 auto-deploy-on-tag pipeline is switched off — `.github/workflows/deploy-on-tag.yml` is renamed to `deploy-manual.yml` and re-triggered via `workflow_dispatch` only (a human runs it, optionally naming a tag); `tag-release.yml` (commit → tag) stays automated because tagging is inert and ships nothing. The runtime capability flag `autoUpdate` is renamed `selfUpdate` everywhere (server + client + tests) to drop the misleading 'auto-' — it means 'this host can self-update when the admin opts in', never 'updates by itself'. User-facing copy now reads 'update available', not 'auto-update' (button 'UPDATE AVAILABLE · MANUAL INSTALL', fallback label 'update available — manual install on the host'). The release-metadata safety field `update.autoUpdate` is deliberately KEPT (it asserts a release does NOT auto-update, and validateReleaseMeta rejects autoUpdate:true). Principle documented in torii-quest-strategy.md Core Principles. New source-level lock tests/deploy-manual-trigger.test.js (4 tests). 3779 tests / 308 files, 21/21 gates.

Prior: v0.2.749-alpha - DUPLICATE PLAYER CHARACTER FIX (layer-1 leak): the full player character (render layer 1, normally visible only to the NAP mirror's reflection camera and the P-key self-view) was leaking into first-person view, leaving a full-body duplicate of the player clipped in front of the FPS camera. Fix is a defensive guard in the arena render loop (`src/arenaRuntime.js`): whenever sticker self-view is NOT active, `camera.layers.disable(1)` plus `camera.layers.enable(2)` re-assert the headless first-person body state every frame, so a leaked layer-1 enable (a self-view entry never cleanly exited) can no longer persist. New source-level regression lock `tests/self-view-layer-guard.test.js` (3 tests). 3775 tests / 307 files, 21/21 gates.

Prior: v0.2.746-alpha — Beacon relay coverage refresh (ADR-0104). `DEFAULT_NODE_RELAYS` is now the writable-verified set only: `main.relay.gamestr.io` (kind-30078 blocked) and `relay.vertexlab.io` (DVM-only, kinds 5312–5315) removed; `relay.damus.io` and `relay.primal.net` added. Every default relay now accepts + round-trips our presence writes (5/5, up from 2/5). Live probe committed at `tools/relay-probe.mjs` so the list stays maintainable. gamestr leaderboard reads (kind-30762) unaffected — they still hit `main.relay.gamestr.io` via `gamestrScore.js`, which is a separate hardcoded target.

Prior: v0.2.744-alpha — Recording-ring toggle (ADR-0100) + auto-deploy pipeline landed (ADR-0101 Accepted). Every future ship deploys itself on tag push — no more "VPS deploy pending" step. Ship reports get one row shorter starting v0.2.746.

Prior: v0.2.745-alpha — CHARACTER FORGE — accept the `mixamorig:` colon form of Mixamo bone names (ADR-0103). The Mixamo bone-map in `src/engine/character/skeleton.js` was keyed on `mixamorigHips` (Adobe FBX form), but every real Mixamo GLB emitted through Blender / Ready Player Me / the glTF pipeline uses `mixamorig:Hips` (with colon). Exact-key lookup missed every bone → verdict `unknown-convention` → "Missing required roles: Hips, Spine, Neck, Head, …" → Character-tab upload flow blocked for the common case. Fix is a pure `normalizeBoneName(name)` in `skeleton.js` that maps `mixamorig:X`, `Armature:mixamorig:X`, and namespaced non-Mixamo names to the canonical no-colon form the map is keyed on; `mixamorigX` stays a fast-path no-op. `detectConvention` and `mapBonesToRoles` both normalize before lookup; the original bone name (with colon) is preserved in `mapped[role]` so downstream code still addresses the actual scene node. Sticker-zone raycast (`stickerPlacement.resolveZoneFromBoneNames`) inherits the fix automatically — it calls `mapBonesToRoles`. Verified end-to-end against `8thwall/web/examples/aframe/animation-mixer/mixamo-animated-lowpoly.glb` (real 1.1 MB Mixamo GLB, 65 colon-form bones): verdict flips from `unknown-convention` to `riggable`, 22/22 roles mapped, zero required missing. 12 new tests (`normalizeBoneName` unit tests + colon-form `detectConvention` / `mapBonesToRoles` regressions). 3772 tests / 306 files.

Prior: v0.2.744-alpha — Recording-ring toggle (ADR-0100) + auto-deploy pipeline landed (ADR-0101 Accepted). Every future ship deploys itself on tag push — no more "VPS deploy pending" step. Ship reports get one row shorter starting v0.2.745.

ADR-0100 detail: owner-controlled pause for the ADR-0055 1Hz auto-capture ring. New pure gate module (default ON — no behavioural change for anyone who never touches the menu). Registered as the second row on the ADR-0099 Kami dev-menu shell. `ToriiDebug.recording.enabled(false)` console mirror flips the same flag. On-screen recording indicator dot goes dark exactly when captures stop. 11 new tests. 3763 tests / 306 files, 21/21 gates.

ADR-0101 detail: deploy-only ed25519 SSH key on the VPS locked by authorized_keys forced-command to `/usr/local/bin/torii-deploy-hook`, sudoers narrows ubuntu to four verbs, private key in `TORII_DEPLOY_SSH_KEY` repo secret (only copy). New workflow `.github/workflows/deploy-on-tag.yml` fires on every `v*-alpha` tag push, SSHes, kicks the update service, polls the live page for up to 80s, and fails loud on mismatch. Verified end-to-end with a throwaway tag today — SSH + hook path works; first live deploy runs on the next real ship.

Prior: v0.2.743-alpha — Kami-mode dev menu (ADR-0099). Owner-only in-world runtime toggles surface. Left-edge smoked-glass panel only visible when the admin is in Kami Mode; hidden on the public perpetual-world view. First toggle registered: sticker RENDER-MODE A/B (was ToriiDebug-only). Pure model + 1Hz DOM driver, gate enforced in CODE. 18 new tests. 3752 tests / 305 files, 21/21 gates. PR-C queues the recording-ring toggle against this same shell.

Prior: v0.2.742-alpha — Home button client-suspend (ADR-0098).

> Visual execution dashboard. `torii-quest-strategy.md` = vision/decision rules · `torii-quest-todo.md` = active task queue.
> Current version: v0.2.742-alpha - HOME CLIENT-SUSPEND (ADR-0098): pause modal Home button now silences + parks the local client without disposing the world. World is perpetual + server-authoritative, so Home never tears down peers' game. New src/engine/state/clientSuspended.js flag (single-source truth) gates playBotShoot (audio.js) + applyBotShot (bots.js) with belt-and-braces early returns; new stopLoop export in loop.js halts rAF; new suspendAudioContext/resumeAudioContext in audio.js park the WebAudio graph; arenaRuntime.js gains leaveToTitle (wired into #btn-home BEFORE the HOME transition) and resumeFromTitle (called first from enter()). MP socket deliberately stays open (idle BOT_STATE only) — dropping it would leave enter() with no bots + no peers because _mp creation lives inside once-per-page boot(). 21 new tests lock loop, flag, audio/bots guard positions, runtime wiring order, and export shape. 3734 tests / 304 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.741-alpha - UGC STICKER SYSTEM SLICE 2 (ADR-0090): any-surface stickers + baked/plane A/B renderer. Removes curated EXCLUDE_NAMES (only gun viewmodel excluded), adds InstancedMesh raycast for grass, hybrid renderer picks true DecalGeometry for static faced hits + plane decals for skinned/instanced/fallback. Pure new module src/engine/character/stickerRenderMode.js (15 tests). ToriiDebug.stickers.forcePlaneMode(true|false) flips global A/B at playtest. Slice 3 (MP broadcast) next. 3713 tests / 303 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.740-alpha - RECONCILIATION: brings PRs #96, #97, #98, #99, and #95 onto v0.2.739 base. Fixes orphan-tag drift (v0.2.734–v0.2.739 shipped as tags but never merged to main). After merge+tag+deploy, main HEAD == git tag == VPS. 3698 tests / 302 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.739-alpha - SETTINGS POLISH (ADR-0097): a targeted quality-of-experience pass on the neutral settings panel that shipped in v0.2.738. Four fixes in one commit. (1) Backdrop dim + blur — `settingsPanel.js` was creating the backdrop with `background: 'transparent'`, so the amber home screen bled through around the neutral panel; now `rgba(6,8,10,0.72)` + `backdrop-filter: blur(8px)` for proper modal scrim. (2) UI font — `#torii-settings-backdrop` was inheriting the game's `--font: 'Courier New', monospace` global, making settings read as a wall of code; now scopes an `-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif` stack to the backdrop only (0 bundle-size cost, game HUD untouched). (3) Character tab pre-login preview — the tab wrapped the entire body in `if (isLoggedIn)`, so anonymous users saw a blank gate; now renders the same preset grid + Upload + Create-with-AI cards logged-out with every action `disabled` behind a friendly "Sign in with Nostr to save your character. You can browse the roster below." banner. `_createView(presets, opts)` + `_presetGrid(presets, opts)` gained a `{ disabled }` option; test lock updated to assert `Sign in with Nostr`, `cf-preset-card`, and `data-action="..." disabled` on all three action buttons. (4) Toast system + micro-interactions — new pure module `src/engine/ui/toast.js` (`showToast` + `toastSuccess`/`toastError`/`toastInfo`, ~2.7 KB, lazily creates `#torii-toast-layer` at z-index 300, stacks toasts, 2400ms auto-dismiss with slide-in/out animations, `prefers-reduced-motion` respected). Wired into the settings action router in `main.js` — `save-profile`, `save-relays`, `remove-relay`, `publish-node` now fire colored toasts. CSS in `index.html` adds hover-lift (`translateY(-1px)`), active-press (`translateY(0) scale(0.98)`), focus-visible teal ring, and hover wash on `.settings-btn-primary` / `.settings-btn-danger` — plus a 200ms panel-rise + 160ms backdrop-fade entrance animation. `setTimeout` allowlist in `regression-check.mjs` extended to include `toast.js`. Scoped rigidly to `#torii-settings-backdrop` / `#torii-settings-panel` / `#torii-toast-layer` — game amber HUD unchanged. 3684 tests / 301 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.726-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — IN-WORLD SELF-VIEW STICKER PLACEMENT (ADR-0088 follow-up): the input binding now lands. Press `P` in-world to enter a self-view orbit camera around your own character — the direct, usable form of the NAP-zone mirror. Pointer-lock mouse orbits + aims, left-click confirms a live raycast placement, `Esc` cancels. New `src/stickerSelfView.js` (detaches the shared camera, orbits the character centre, swaps the layer mask to show layer 1 + hide the headless layer-2 FP body, drives the pure `stickerPlacementMode` + `stickerRaycast` + `stickerStudio` raycast). Confirmation flows through a new `confirmStickerPlacement` runtime hook → `main.js`, which folds `{hash,zoneId,u,v,rot}` through `addSticker` + the kind-35100 republish. `player.js` hands the camera to the self-view while active (`state.stickerPlacementActive`); the gun viewmodel is hidden; `Esc` cancels instead of pausing. Browser-playtested (enter/aim/cancel) headless-webgl. Visual decal baking on the skin remains a later rendering step.
> Prior: v0.2.725-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — IN-WORLD RAYCAST STICKER PLACEMENT (ADR-0088) + LIVE GENERATOR CLIENTS/EXECUTOR (ADR-0089). (1) In-world placement: pure `src/engine/character/stickerRaycast.js` (hit→placement — `boneNames`→zone via `resolveZoneFromBoneNames`, geometry u/v clamped, `rot` from surface-normal azimuth) + `src/engine/character/stickerPlacementMode.js` (inert enter→aim→confirm/cancel state machine; `aim` combines the resolved hit with the selected `STICKER_LIBRARY` hash — the raycast supplies position, the library selection supplies identity — and `placementToManifest` folds a confirmed draft through `addSticker`). The live THREE raycast of the player's OWN character is isolated in `src/stickerStudio.js` (reuses the proven stickerNpc.js SkinnedMesh path — recompute bounding sphere + skeleton.update → skinIndex/skinWeight bones + geometry UV — against `playerModel.getPlayerModelRoot()`, layer 1, mirror view), deliberately NOT SDK-exported. (2) Live generation: pure `src/engine/character/meshGenerationClient.js` (`buildBackendRequest`/`normalizeBackendResponse` + `createGeneratorClient({fetcher})` — INERT, never fetches without an injected fetcher) + `src/engine/character/meshGenerationExecutor.js` (client → `validateGeneratedMesh` gate → injected routstr/Cashu `charge` → plan; never signs/publishes/seats). No live network or payment is possible by default.
> Prior: v0.2.724-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — STICKER PLACEMENT UI + VALIDATOR-GATED EXTERNAL MESH GENERATION (ADR-0086 + ADR-0087): the two remaining Character Forge slices land together. (1) Sticker placement: pure `src/engine/character/stickerPlacement.js` (body-zone registry `STICKER_ZONES` + bone→zone resolution + immutable `addSticker`/`removeSticker`/`updateSticker` manifest ops with a `MAX_STICKERS` cap) plus a sticker editor in the Character tab — list/remove stickers, add one from a curated `STICKER_LIBRARY` (its decal reuses the shipped ftff-sticker.png so the hash is real + Blossom-resolvable), each edit re-signing + republishing the kind-35100 event. (2) External mesh generation: pure `src/engine/character/meshGeneration.js` — a swappable `GENERATION_BACKENDS` registry (Meshy/Tripo/Hunyuan3D), a prompt-bounded request + INERT plan (`performed:false`), and the `validateGeneratedMesh()` GATE that accepts a generated mesh ONLY when the manifest validates AND the rig is auto-riggable (composes `validateCharacterManifest` + `assessRig`). The live generator clients + routstr/Cashu executor are a later host slice.
> Prior: v0.2.722-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — PEER-AVATAR MESH LOADING (ADR-0082): peers now see each other's custom mesh. The player's mesh hash (from the kind-35100 event) is broadcast through the MP `character` field (now accepts a 64-hex sha256 alongside the skin keys), the server relays it, and the peer loader resolves the hash to a Blossom URL and loads that GLB instead of the built-in default (`_peerMeshUrl()` in arenaRuntime.js). The player path (self) + peer path now both load custom meshes — the full automatic mesh-loading loop is closed. Sticker placement UI + external mesh generation remain.
> Prior: v0.2.721-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — AUTOMATIC MESH LOADING (player path) + CUSTOM MESH UPLOAD (ADR-0082): the player's own character mesh now actually LOADS. At login the game fetches the npub's kind-35100 event, resolves its mesh hash to a Blossom URL (`src/engine/character/characterMesh.js`), and seats it before arena boot so `loadPlayerModel()` fetches the custom mesh instead of the built-in default (`setCustomMeshUrl()` in playerModel.js). The Character tab also gained an "Upload custom mesh" button — upload a .glb to Blossom (NIP-98) and publish the character event in one flow. The peer-avatar path (broadcasting each peer's mesh hash through the MP protocol) is the remaining slice.
> Prior: v0.2.720-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — CREATE ROUND-TRIP (ADR-0082): the Character tab now WRITES a character, not just reads one. Curated presets (`src/engine/character/characterPresets.js` — Chiefmonkey + Nostrich, content-addressed mesh hashes) feed a preset picker; picking one builds a `torii.character` manifest, signs the kind-35100 event via NIP-07, and publishes to the unified relay list (`publishCharacter()` in nostr.js, mirroring publishAccessSettings). Also added the Blossom upload path (`uploadBlossom()` + `buildBlossomAuthEvent()` — NIP-96/NIP-98) so a custom mesh GLB can be uploaded and its content-addressed sha256 slotted into the manifest. Sticker placement UI + external mesh generation remain follow-up slices.
> Prior: v0.2.719-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE — LIVE RELAY READ (ADR-0082 follow-up): the Character settings tab now checks the logged-in npub's kind-35100 character event over a live relay read and seats an existing character automatically (the "smooth experience" seam — no prompt, no re-upload). New pure `src/engine/character/characterRelayRead.js` (filter builder + read/reduce + newest-per-author) + `fetchOwnCharacter()` in nostr.js (reuses fanoutReq). The create round-trip (writing a new character event) is the next slice.
> Prior: v0.2.718-alpha - CHARACTER FORGE GROUNDWORK (ADR-0082): validator-first character pipeline — no in-house generation model. New pure `src/engine/character/` modules: `skeleton.js` (canonical role-based skeleton contract + Mixamo/Biped bone maps + axis detection), `rigAssessment.js` (auto-rig verdict ladder), `characterManifest.js` (`torii.character` manifest validator), `characterEvent.js` (kind-35100 character-event parser — the "smooth experience" seam). New "Character" settings tab (`src/engine/settings/characterForgePanel.js`, registered in main.js + settingsPanel.js). SDK exposure at the experimental tier. Live relay read/create round-trip is a follow-up slice.
> Post-v0.2.717 (main, untagged) - ADR-0084/0085 ARENA + STICKER STUDIO NAPPLET REGISTRATIONS (WIRING-ONLY, PR #87): factories over `createGameHandlers` / `createAvatarHandlers`, dTags `torii-arena` and `sticker-studio`, `NAPPLET_IDENTITY` `@v0-wiring` placeholder (ADR-0092 will replace with bundle hashes at release). Sticker studio declares `requires: ['torii-avatar-write']` and the handlers gate is enforced in code, not just UI. Napplets never sign, never touch relays - the shell brokers publish. 21 new tests, curated file count 279 -> 281.
> Post-v0.2.717 (main, untagged) - ADR-0082/0083 NAPPLET GAME HOST + AVATAR SHELL SCAFFOLDS (PR #84): `GAME_NAMESPACE` + `AVATAR_NAMESPACE` on `nappletEnvelope.js`; new `gameNappletSrcdoc.js` / `gameNappletHandlers.js` / `NappletGameHost.js` and `avatarNappletSrcdoc.js` / `avatarNappletHandlers.js` - game-host and avatar variants of the existing NappletSurface (ADR-0057) / productNappletHost (ADR-0058) contract. Async handler protocol (`{ __async, promise }`) so relay-mediated actions stay shell-owned. Trust boundary unchanged: `sandbox="allow-scripts"` only, `MessageEvent.source` validated, per-mount channelId nonce, CSP `default-src 'none'; connect-src 'none'; img-src 'none'`. 35 new tests, curated file count 276 -> 279. Full suite 3508 green.
> Prior: v0.2.717-alpha - SETTINGS PANEL SMOKED GLASS + PROFILE PREFILL: removed the full-screen dark backdrop behind the settings panel (it now sits on a transparent click-catcher) and gave the panel a smoked-glass look (semi-transparent + backdrop blur + shadow). The Profile tab now pre-fills from the logged-in user's published kind:0 Nostr profile (display name, bio, avatar, website, NIP-05, lightning address) when the local draft is empty — via a new fetchOwnProfile() in nostr.js.
> Prior: v0.2.716-alpha - RELAY TAB POLISH: removed the 'STARTER' badge from the Relay settings tab header (it overlapped the panel's top-right close button) and gave every relay row a Remove button — previously the curated starter relays rendered as read-only rows with a 'starter' tag and no delete. Removing a starter relay now materialises the remaining defaults as the operator's explicit config (remove-relay reads the effective list, not the raw stored string).
> Prior: v0.2.715-alpha - SINGLE UNIFIED RELAY LIST (ADR-0081): one relay list for the whole game. The separate hardcoded read-path RELAYS (nos.lol + vertexlab.io) in nostr.js is removed; profile/login/leaderboard/discovery reads AND presence/heartbeat publish now all use the single owner-editable list (localStorage torii.node.relays + <meta name=torii-relays>) with a curated 5-relay default (gamestr.io, plebeian.market, routstr.com, nos.lol, vertexlab.io). Per-action publish consent (heartbeat signer prompt, gamestr toggle, access-settings save) is unchanged — the list decides WHERE, never WHETHER.
> Prior: v0.2.712-alpha - RESTORE ACCESS SETTINGS TAB (ADR-0078): re-surfaced the instanceSettings admin surface (arrival authority + write authority, backed by the signed kind:30078) as a fifth "Access" tab in the settings panel — hidden since v0.2.676 but the underlying enforcement (handoffArrival.js / writeAuthority.js) stayed live the whole time. Restored the v0.2.400 read/save relay round-trips in main.js (readLatestAccessSettings / publishAccessSettings) + the _instanceSettingsState / _refreshInstanceSettingsAccessState / _saveInstanceSettingsAccess functions recovered verbatim from the v0.2.676-predecessor commit. The Access-tab renderer filters the model's sections to only 'access' so the inert placeholder sections ('multiplayer' / 'more' "More coming soon") are NOT surfaced — only live, useful controls. Wired delegated change (radio → draft) + submit (data-form=access-settings → _saveInstanceSettingsAccess) listeners on #torii-settings-content. settingsPanel TABS is now [profile, gateway, heartbeat, relay, access] (getSettingsTabIds() exposed for testability). No new sign/publish path (reuses nostr.js signEvent / fanoutPublish); enforcement unchanged + cannot be weakened by the edit surface. Deferred (need core refactors, not fake tabs): Audio (no master gain API), Graphics (no manual override), Controls (no keybind layer). 3449 tests / 275 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.711-alpha - HEARTBEAT AUTO-ON + RELAY CLEANUP (ADR-0076 update + ADR-0077): heartbeat is now ON by default and auto-fires the first presence publish when the owner logs in (login click authorises the NIP-07 signer prompt — approving it IS the consent; a rejection pauses so it never nags). Removed the manual-first-publish gate from _heartbeatTick (isHeartbeatDue contract unchanged). Added relay.routstr.com to DEFAULT_NODE_RELAYS (now gamestr + plebeian + routstr, all Torii-ecosystem, WS-verified live). Dropped the 503ing relay.damus.io from the read path (nostr.js RELAYS now nos.lol + relay.vertexlab.io) — fixes the connection-failed console noise. CSP updated (dropped damus, added routstr + vertexlab). Fixed bump-ver.sh (was missing toriiQuestDashboardData.js + torii-quest-dashboard tests — VERSION constant stuck at 709). Fixed regression-check.mjs EXPECTED_VERSION to read dynamically from package.json (was hardcoded, drifted since 710). 3445 tests / 274 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.709-alpha - VERSION FIX: arena multiplayer server SERVER_VERSION constant was hardcoded and never bumped (ADR-0075). Root cause found when Bekka's arena HELLO reported v0.2.602-alpha though her release was v0.2.706-alpha. Initially diagnosed as a stale build; on checking the source, server/arena-ws.js line 69 has `const SERVER_VERSION = 'v0.2.602-alpha'` as a hardcoded literal — bump-ver.sh never touched server/arena-ws.js, so the constant was stuck at 602 across tags 706/707/708 (chiefmonkey's own instance had the same bug). Fix: (1) added server/arena-ws.js to bump-ver.sh's sed file list so the constant is bumped on every future release; (2) bumped SERVER_VERSION to v0.2.709-alpha to match the release. Bekka (and any operator) can now pull v0.2.709-alpha, rebuild, and the arena HELLO will report the correct version. 3445 tests / 274 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.706-alpha - ADMIN GREETING COLOUR SPLIT (ADR-0072, supersedes ADR-0071 colour claim): only the owner's name is now orange (#f7931a); 'Welcome ', ',', and 'you are ' are the neutral caption tone (#d8c3ac); 'logged in' is green (#6DAA45) to balance the orange name; the green dot stays green. Line 1 is rebuilt via the DOM API (createElement + textContent per span, no innerHTML) so a Nostr display name can never inject markup; badge text split into a neutral 'you are ' span + a green 'logged in' span. .toc-greet no longer sets orange (only .toc-name does). Same isAdminOperator() gate, display:none fail-closed, no pubkey leak. 3445 tests / 274 files, 21/21 gates.
> Prior: v0.2.705-alpha - ADMIN LOGGED-IN CAPTION REWORDED TO A GREETING (ADR-0071): when the admin is logged in, the top-left caption now reads "Welcome <owner name>," on line 1 (prominent 13px orange, sentence-case via a new .toc-line1.toc-greet class that overrides the 9px uppercase label style) and a green dot + "you are logged in" on line 2 (badge text changed from "logged in"; .toc-loggedin text-transform set to none so it renders in the quoted sentence case, not uppercase). The name span (#torii-owner-name) is hidden on line 2 for the admin (the name moved into the greeting); non-admin/anonymous visitors see the unchanged "This torii belongs to / <name>" caption. Same isAdminOperator() gate as ADR-0070, no new auth, no pubkey leak; greeting + badge swap atomically in one _refreshOwnerLabel call (no flicker). 3445 tests green / 274 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.704-alpha - ADMIN-ONLY "LOGGED IN" BADGE ON THE OWNER CAPTION (ADR-0070): a small green pulsing dot + the text "logged in" now appears to the right of the owner's name on the top-left homescreen caption ("This torii belongs to"), shown ONLY when the logged-in viewer IS the configured instance owner. Reuses the existing isAdminOperator(viewer, adminPubkey) check (no new auth/fetch); the name was split into its own truncating span (#torii-owner-name) so the badge sits to its right without being clipped by the ellipsis; the badge is display:none by default and only .show when login is confirmed, so it never flashes before login and never appears for non-owners/anonymous; never surfaces the pubkey (toggles a CSS class only). The owner's name remains visible to all visitors, unchanged. New test file main-owner-loggedin-badge.test.js (6 source-level wiring assertions). 3445 tests green / 274 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.703-alpha - PAUSE MODAL RECOLORED TO TORII SUNSET THEME (ADR-0069): the pause modal shown after ESC releases pointer lock and transitions to PAUSED (#pause-overlay / #pause-box / #pause-title in index.html) was still on the legacy dark-neon-purple palette (rgba(20,20,35,0.97) panel + rgba(139,92,246,0.4) purple border + rgba(0,0,0,0.6) backdrop). Recolored to the torii-gate sunset theme: warm backdrop rgba(20,13,9,0.7), warm panel rgba(30,18,12,0.97) (warm twin of the old purple at the same opacity so the paused game stays blocked out) with var(--panel-glass-border) bronze border + a subtle depth box-shadow, and the PAUSED title in var(--accent) #e8842c with a warm glow. Layout, sizing, buttons (.btn / .btn-secondary already themed), the .show toggle, focus/pointer-lock behavior, and the resume flow are unchanged — color-only migration, no DOM/JS/asset change. 3439 tests green / 273 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.702-alpha - BOOT OVERLAY RECOLORED TO TORII SUNSET THEME (ADR-0068): the full-screen loading overlay shown after Nostr login + ENTER TORII (#boot-overlay in index.html) was the last player-facing surface still on the legacy dark-neon-purple palette (#140a28 / #b794f4 / #8b5cf6). Recolored to the canonical torii-gate sunset theme via the existing :root tokens (--accent #e8842c, --accent-soft #c9793a, --orange #f7931a, --panel-text, --panel-text-dim, --muted): warm radial background (#2a1810→#140d09→#0a0604), accent-orange title with warm glow, bronze→orange progress bar with a soft orange box-shadow, and warm-dim status/sub text. Layout, sizing, spacing, and the eased progress animation are unchanged — color-only migration, no DOM/JS/asset change. 3439 tests green / 273 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.701-alpha - HOMEPAGE OWNER CAPTION NOW SHOWS THE ADMIN'S REAL NOSTR NAME TO ALL VISITORS: the top-right "This torii belongs to" caption previously fell back to a shortened npub for every visitor except the owner viewing their own signed-in browser. Now any visitor sees the admin's real published Nostr display name. New `fetchOwnerProfileName(pubkey)` in `src/nostr.js` — a pure, read-only relay fetch (kind:0, via `fanoutReq` + `readProfiles`) with a 5-minute in-memory TTL cache, validated against a strict 64-char lowercase-hex pubkey regex, never mutates the viewer's own `state.nostrName`/`nostrAvatar`. `resolveToriiOwnerLabel()` gains a new tier-2 `ownerProfileName` param (4-tier priority: owner's own draft → owner's published Nostr name (NEW) → shortened npub → "not yet claimed"). Wired into `main.js`'s `_refreshOwnerLabel()` via a guarded, once-per-pubkey fetch that repaints the caption on resolution. Update button's deploy-trigger wiring confirmed untouched. 21 new tests across 3 files. 3439 tests green / 273 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.700-alpha - HOMEPAGE OWNER CAPTION + VERSION PANEL RESTYLE: moved the top-left Torii icon/menu button to the bottom-left, added a new top-left "This torii belongs to: <name>" caption (owner's displayName/name when the viewer is the admin, else a shortened npub, or "not yet claimed" pre-configuration) via the new pure `resolveToriiOwnerLabel()` helper (`src/engine/identity/toriiOwnerLabel.js`, 7 unit tests), wired to refresh on capability probes, NOSTR_LOGIN, and profile-save. Moved the version/update-check panel to the bottom-right and re-themed it from blue/navy to the bronze/orange smoked-glass palette (border, background, backdrop-filter, text tones all matched to brand; the on-brand orange "new version" highlight left untouched). Found and fixed a real CSS bug during verification: `#title-centre.glass-panel`'s `backdrop-filter` was silently creating a new containing block for the panel's `position: fixed`, anchoring it to the console instead of the viewport corner — fixed by lifting `#update-preview` out of that subtree to sit as a direct child of `#screen-title` (documented inline for future maintainers). Also fixed a long-displayName 2-line-wrap collision with the TORII wordmark by switching the caption to single-line ellipsis truncation with a `title` attribute for the full name on hover/long-press. Confirmed via screenshots at 1440×900 and 375×812 (iPhone SE) with both short and long names. Update button's functional deploy-trigger wiring untouched — visual/positional CSS only. Also merged in the previously-tagged-but-unmerged v0.2.699-alpha (ADR-0067 relay trim) commit onto this branch. 3419 tests green / 271 files, 21/21 regression gates.
> Prior: v0.2.699-alpha - RELAY TRIM (ADR-0067): fixed repeated browser-console WebSocket errors on relay.damus.io/relay.nostr.band by testing every relay in GAMESTR_RELAYS (leaderboard, src/engine/gamestr/gamestrScore.js) and nostr.js RELAYS (profile reads) with the EXACT queries the game sends, from both the sandbox and the live VPS. relay.nostr.band timed out (~8s, down); relay.damus.io 503'd the leaderboard REQ; relay.primal.net rejected the #game tag filter with a NOTICE. Trimmed GAMESTR_RELAYS from 5 relays to 2 (main.relay.gamestr.io + nos.lol — the only ones that actually work for this query); trimmed nostr.js RELAYS from 4 to 2 (relay.damus.io + nos.lol, both verified against a real kind:0 query). Fixed a latent CSP bug in tools/csp.mjs: main.relay.gamestr.io — the leaderboard's own authoritative relay — was never in connect-src; now included alongside both plebeian relays (marketStall.js uses staging + prod). PLEBEIAN_RELAYS (config.js, still staging) intentionally untouched — marketplace concern, out of scope, would risk the current test auction. torii.quest confirmed NOT a relay and not the user's domain (unrelated site, SSL fails) — no relay exists there. gamestr.io confirmed as a NIP-133-descended Nostr leaderboard protocol; main.relay.gamestr.io is its authoritative relay and was already in use. Updated the GAMESTR_RELAYS source-contract test to assert the exact trimmed list instead of a loose ≥ 4 lower bound. Repo-only change — no VPS deploy. 3412 tests green / 270 files, 21/21 regression gates.
Prior: v0.2.698-alpha - FIRST-LOAD HEARTBEAT CONSENT FIX (default-ON now actually reaches NIP-07 consent): a fresh install ships the heartbeat toggle default-ON (`adminPrefs.getHeartbeatIntent()` defaults to `'on'`), but three call sites derived "is the switch ON" from that raw stored *intent* string instead of from whether a publish had actually happened — so a brand-new owner's very first click read intent as already `'on'`, flipped it straight to `'off'`, and showed "Heartbeat OFF" with no NIP-07 signer prompt ever reachable via the UI. Root cause: intent (`'on'`/`'off'`, what the owner *wants*) was conflated with broadcast state (`'off'|'idle'|'publishing'|'live'|'stale'|'blocked:*'|'failed:*'|'paused:wallet-requires-approval'`, what is *actually happening*) — idle was being read as ON. Fix: new shared pure helper `isHeartbeatBroadcasting(status)` in `src/engine/presence/heartbeat.js` (true only for `'live'`/`'stale'`/`'publishing'`/`'paused:wallet-requires-approval'`; false for `'off'`/`'idle'`/any `'blocked:*'`/`'failed:*'`/non-string) now drives all three call sites: `heartbeatPanel.js`'s `_isOnState()` (idle-state publish label reworded to "Publish my node presence (click to give signer consent + go live)"), `toriiMenu.js`'s `hbOn` derivation, and `main.js`'s `onPublishNode` toggle-direction logic (was inverting off raw intent — `getHeartbeatIntent() === 'on' ? 'off' : 'on'` — now correctly checks `isHeartbeatBroadcasting()` so the first click on a fresh install publishes and requests NIP-07 consent instead of silently flipping to off). `homepageStub.js`'s own Publish card confirmed dead code (unreachable via any live click path) and left untouched. New tests: `isHeartbeatBroadcasting` coverage in `heartbeat.test.js` (all statuses + non-string fail-closed + cross-checks against `heartbeatStatus()`); new `heartbeatPanel.test.js` (hb-switch class/aria-checked per status, idle label wording, owner-gating); new source-level regression locks `torii-menu-heartbeat-toggle.test.js` + `main-heartbeat-consent.test.js` (toriiMenu.js/main.js have no DOM test harness in this repo, so these assert via readFileSync + regex that the shared helper is imported and used at each call site). 3412 full suite green / 270 files, 21/21 regression gates green.
Prior: v0.2.697-alpha - ONE-COMMAND DOCKER INSTALLER: new self-contained `install.sh` (+ `install/lib/ui.sh`, `install/lib/run.sh`) gives a fresh `git clone` of this repo a single `sudo ./install.sh` path to a live instance — Docker install if missing, prompts for domain/email/admin npub, builds + launches the compose stack, verifies over loopback, colourful boxed summary. Multiplayer (`arena-ws`) ships ON by default alongside the game and Nostr relay (`strfry`), matching the standing MP-is-integral decision; owners can reconfigure/duplicate/remove any compose service afterwards. `Caddyfile`+`Dockerfile` reworked so the CSP header is extracted from the real per-build `dist/_headers` at image-build time instead of a hand-copied static value (kills a class of drift — `VPS_INSTALL.md`'s three copy-pasted CSP blocks, found stale with a dead `'strict-dynamic'` directive during this pass, now point at the same live-extraction command instead of a hardcoded string). Fixed a real bash pitfall in `ui_ask`/`ui_confirm`: reopening `/dev/stdin` via redirect for each prompt could read-ahead the whole pipe buffer on some bash builds and flush the unconsumed remainder as literal NUL bytes on exit — now reads the inherited stdin fd directly when no controlling TTY is present, with a real write-probe (not just permission bits) to detect TTY availability; also fixed `run.sh`'s log-dir bootstrap to fall back to a per-user tmp dir instead of crashing with a raw `mkdir: Permission denied` before the friendly non-root message can print. VPS_INSTALL.md gains a new quick-start section pointing at the installer; the manual bare-metal/systemd path stays as documented fallback. 3380 tests green / 267 files, 21/21 regression gates.
Prior: v0.2.695-alpha - MINIMAP REMOVED + SINGLE-K KAMI ENTRY (ADR-0064): (a) removed the in-game minimap entirely — canvas was a sibling (not a child) of its positioning wrapper, so it rendered top-left instead of the intended bottom-right; since a minimap wasn't wanted it was deleted rather than repositioned; (b) a single bare K now enters Kami Mode AND opens the ema note input in one press (was a 2-step flow per ADR-0029); Enter/Esc no longer re-acquire pointer lock, leaving the mouse free to click the emagake rack. 3380 tests green / 267 files, 21/21 regression gates.
Prior: v0.2.685-alpha - ADR-0059 AUCTION PANEL HEADER HARDENING: eliminate every innerHTML write in auctionPanel.js — the header (chips + high-bid) and the legacy fallback bid-list body now render via createElement + textContent, so no relay/profile-derived string (auction_type, currency, bidder name, picture URL) ever reaches innerHTML. renderChips → buildChips (returns [{cls,text}] data), renderBidRow → buildBidRow (returns a plain descriptor); the renderer builds DOM nodes. New malicious-input test proves auction_type/currency/profile-name render as inert text (no element injection). 3368 full suite green / 265 files.
Prior: v0.2.684-alpha - SETTINGS PANEL: PROFILE + RELAY TABS, HOMEPAGE FADE, TOP-RIGHT CLOSE (non-gameplay UI/UX pass, six-item polish request): (1) the homepage centre panel's bounding box corners are now fully rounded and the smoked-glass background fades to true 100% transparency at the edges with no hard edge (radial mask-image tuned to a soft feathered stop). (2) new Relay tab (relayPanel.js, pure renderer) lets the owner view/add/remove the wss:// relays their node publishes heartbeat presence to — list rows with a live-dot + URL + Remove button, a comma/newline-separated add/edit textarea, Save Relays button, and an explicit "never falls back to public relays" note; backed by existing nodeRelays getNodeRelays()/setNodeRelays() storage. (3) Heartbeat toggle now defaults to on (adminPrefs.getHeartbeatIntent() default 'on') with an intuitive red/green switch (hb-switch, red track+OFF label when off, green track+ON label + knob slid right when on) instead of a plain checkbox. (4) removed the stale "To visit another world, use the Torii Gateway inside the NAP zone." copy from the Gateway Setup tab (node travel has gone through the in-world NAP-zone Gateway since v0.2.669-alpha; the tab no longer needed to say so). (5) the admin settings panel's bottom-left Close button is replaced by a standard top-right ✕ button (.ts-close-x, circular, top:10px/right:10px) on every tab. (6) new Profile tab (profilePanel.js, first/top nav item) exposes the standard Nostr kind:0 profile fields an owner needs to customise their Quest installation — Display name, Bio, Avatar URL, Website, NIP-05, Lightning address — with a login gate ("Log in with Nostr to customise your profile.") when signed out, a localStorage draft (profileDraft.js, key torii.profile.draft) so in-progress edits survive a reload, and Save Profile building an unsigned kind:0 event (profileMetadata.js buildProfileMetadataEvent, validates pubkey + sanitizes every field) that is signed via the existing NIP-07 signer and published through the existing fanoutPublish/RELAYS path (states: saved-local → publishing → published/failed). New modules: relayPanel.js, profilePanel.js, profileMetadata.js, profileDraft.js. Settings panel TABS reordered to [Profile, Gateway Setup, Heartbeat, Relay] with Profile as the new default active tab. Zero gameplay-file overlap (bots.js/botModel.js/bot-tactics.js/botNetState.js/botSim.js/physics.js/player.js/weapons.js/scene.js/hud.js/input.js/lod.js/multiplayerHost.js/wireProtocol.js/arenaRuntime.js/server/arena-ws.js/server/bots/botStateGate.js all untouched). 3361 full suite green / 264 files (5 skipped), 21/21 regression gates green. Prior: v0.2.683-alpha - ADR-0058 PRODUCT PANEL NAPPLET CONVERSION: first LIVE nap-torii-world napplet. Shell (marketStall.js) keeps the Plebeian relay subscription + brokers serialized auction snapshots INTO a sandboxed iframe; the napplet renders the bid-list body (DOM nodes + textContent, never innerHTML of untrusted data) while the shell's legacy renderer keeps the panel header. New modules: productNappletSnapshot.js (pure serializeBidList → plain JSON, caps 64, strips Maps/images/functions), productPanelNappletSrcdoc.js (self-contained renderer), productNappletHost.js (browser DI mount/push/destroy/fallback). Updated nappletSrcdoc.js (parent source check, per-mount channelId nonce validated+stamped, world.on/off shell→napplet channel, CSP connect-src 'none' img-src 'none'), NappletSurface.js (nonce + post()), marketStall.js (mount+push, legacy skipBody fallback), worldNappletSurfaceConfig.js (product-stall-panel enabled:true, allowedEmitKinds:[] read-only), worldNappletHandlers.js (world.emit returns accepted:false 'no-handler'), auctionPanel.js (skipBody). Built-in LOCAL identity (NIP-5A deferred). New+updated tests across 4 files; 3366 full suite green / 265 files. Prior: v0.2.682-alpha - ADR-0057 NAPPLET WORLD-SURFACE SHELL SCAFFOLD: first implementation of the nap-torii-world trusted-shell boundary. Six modules under src/engine/napplets/ (nappletEnvelope pure envelope validate/result/error + splitType; nappletIdentity pure (dTag, aggregateHash); nappletSrcdoc pure buildWorldSrcdoc() bootstraps window.napplet.world with the six v0 methods, never reads parent.document; worldNappletHandlers dispatcher implementing world.attach.get + world.zone.list + allow-listed world.emit, returns 'unsupported' for pose.*/visit; worldNappletSurfaceConfig frozen per-surface config keyed by existing proof surface ids, every surface enabled:false, does NOT mutate proofSurfaceSpecs.js; NappletSurface browser-only DI mounts iframe sandbox EXACTLY allow-scripts no allow-same-origin, binds contentWindow to identity, validates every inbound message by MessageEvent.source NOT event.origin, destroy() cleanup). DOM-overlay iframe (NOT iframe-to-texture/WebXR — WebXR immersive renders only the WebGL canvas not DOM, so embedding a live iframe in a 3D scene is unsolved; v0 proof is a flat surface panel so the gap doesn't block). Game host DEFERRED to ADR-0058+; shared core shaped for reuse. No live relay/Blossom/signing/wallet/travel/product-panel conversion — test-only/dormant. 39 new tests + 3338 full suite green / 263 files. Prior: v0.2.681-alpha - ADR-0056 HOTFIX (time base + ring count + error honesty): four correctness bugs caught on advisor review of v0.2.680. (1) the indicator used performance.now() for its age math but the autocap report stores Date.now() wall-clock — the 'last Xs' age + recentOk window were wrong; fixed to Date.now(). (2) the capture tick still ran AFTER renderFrame so the JPEG drained a frame late vs the snapshot — moved BEFORE renderFrame. (3) the ring count used capture ATTEMPTS (captured) instead of stored uploads (uploaded) — fixed to uploaded so the count reflects the real VPS ring. (4) the error state had a 60s grace window that hid a current failure — since markUploaded clears lastError, any lastError present is a current unresolved failure; flattened to isError = !!lastError. markUploaded/markFailed now stamp completion-time Date.now(), not the captured request time. 3299 full suite green. Prior: v0.2.680-alpha - ADR-0056 RECORDING INDICATOR + DASHBOARD SURFACING: a live amber glowing 'RECORDING' HUD overlay (recIndicator.js) renders top-right in the arena for the owner while the ADR-0055 1Hz auto-capture ring is live. Owner-gated (not debug-gated) so the owner sees the ring is recording; non-owners never see it. Pure CSS glow animation (rAF only throttles text/state, ~250ms — near-zero cost); prefers-reduced-motion disables the pulse. Honest states: RECORDING (amber) when active/recent/inflight, REC ERROR (red) when lastError set + no upload since, with ring n/120 + last-capture age as a secondary line. Zero cost when off — no DOM until the owner plays + capability resolved. The static oversight dashboard gains a 'Live diagnostics' section documenting the autocap ring (cap 120, path, separation, gate, retrieval CLI) — static/read-only, NO network calls added. 11 new tests + 3298 full suite green. Prior: v0.2.679-alpha - ADR-0055 HOTFIX (owner gate + URL + bounded ring + timing): the first pass had four real bugs caught on advisor review. (1) POST URL was /mp/kami/autocap but resolveMpHttpBase() already returns the /mp base — fixed to /kami/autocap (matches the manual ema route). (2) the client owner gate was just 'has a hex pubkey' so any logged-in visitor would fire 1Hz captures that 403'd — now uses the real admin capability check (fetchCapability + isAdminOperator), resolved once + cached, so non-admins never capture. (3) the autocap index was append-only jsonl (unbounded at 1Hz) — redesigned to ONE {ema,shot} JSON file per frame, ring-culled to 120 (a TRUE ring: disk strictly bounded). (4) the capture tick ran AFTER renderFrame so the queued JPEG drained a frame late vs the snapshot — moved BEFORE renderFrame so they align in the same render tick; timestamps now use Date.now() (wall-clock) for server-log correlation. Dump CLI updated to read the one-file-per-frame ring. 3287 full suite green. Prior: v0.2.678-alpha - ADR-0055 EMA AUTO-CAPTURE DIAGNOSTIC (1Hz rolling ring): a pure 1Hz auto-capture state machine (kamiAutoCapture.js) grabs a canvas frame + a full debug snapshot every second while the owner plays, seals them to the owner+Kami pubkey (same split-seal as the manual ema), and POSTs to a SEPARATE autocap ring (/var/lib/torii-quest/kami/autocap, cap 120) so it never evicts a real manual ema screenshot (shots/ cap 420) and never appends to ema.jsonl (forever). Hooks the in-arena rAF loop (no new timers). Owner-only + playing-only + inflight backpressure. The manual ema snapshot now reports the ring status so a hung ema points at nearby frames. New retrieval CLI (tools/kami-autocap-dump.mjs) decrypts the ring with KAMI_PRIV into JPEG frames + timeline.jsonl, optionally stitching an MP4 via ffmpeg. Catches the transient phantoms + desync the manual K-press always missed. 29 new tests + 3287 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. No gameplay change - diagnostic capture only. Prior: v0.2.677-alpha - ADR-0053 + ADR-0054 GATEWAY FIXES (live directory + in-place smoked-glass panel): the gateway directory was polluted with 48 stale records (38-56 days old) plus travel handshake (req/res) noise, so no live instance was visible. ADR-0053: readGateways now drops handshake records (state tag) + expired (NIP-40) + stale no-expiration records (1h grace). ADR-0054: the gateway screen opens IN PLACE (no blacked-out backdrop) on a smoked-glass card with three columns — Friends (mutual follows), Follows (you follow), Games (instances that published a game) — reusing classifySections. 5 new tests + 3258 full suite green. Prior: v0.2.676-alpha - SETTINGS PANEL RESTRUCTURE + HOMEPAGE POLISH (non-gameplay UI/UX pass): removed the Instance Settings tab and all its arrival-mode/write-policy admin wiring from the title-screen settings panel (nothing useful there for admins or guests; the underlying signed kind:30078 access-control enforcement in handoffArrival.js/writeAuthority.js is untouched). Split "Publish my node's presence" out of the Gateway Setup tab into its own Heartbeat tab (new heartbeatPanel.js renderer; same underlying state/callback). Settings panel is now exactly 2 tabs: Gateway Setup, Heartbeat. On the homepage: vertically centred the numbered steps (1/2/3) with their row content, moved the "CHARACTER" label above the step rows and reworded it to "Select your character", increased the centre panel's translucency by ~20% and added a radial mask-image so its background fades to full transparency at the edges, removed its light golden border, and removed the title header's table/cell border + dark-brown background (text + torii icon unchanged) while lightening the SOVEREIGNTY · OPEN WORLD · FREE MARKET · ABUNDANCE subtitle text. 3253 full suite green (no new tests needed — no existing test imported the removed modules), 21/21 regression gates green. No gameplay change. Prior: v0.2.675-alpha - ADR-0052 KAMI MODE STATE DIAGNOSTIC (pinpoint the 'stuck in Kami Mode — Esc does nothing' bug): a playtest ema reported being locked in Kami Mode with Esc doing nothing, and the server confirmed KAMI_STATE active=true never cleared. The ema snapshot couldn't tell WHY (active stuck? noteOpen? entering? pointer-lock?). Fix: add a kami sub-report to the debug snapshot (buildKamiReport) capturing active/noteOpen/entering/pointerLocked, wired via installToriiDebug + a new kamiEntering() export. 3 new tests + 3253 full suite green. No gameplay change — read-only diagnostic; the next stuck ema disambiguates the root cause. Prior: v0.2.674-alpha - ADR-0051 WRAP THE POINTER-LOCK YAW TO [-π, π] (fix for BAD_FIELD MOVE rejection): the pointer-lock yaw accumulated without bound (input.js `_yaw -= movementX * SENS`), so after a full 360° turn it exceeded 2π and the server's isRot2 (ROT_ABS = 2π) rejected EVERY MOVE with BAD_FIELD — 1656 rejections in ~10 min, the server silently stopped tracking the player's position. Fix: wrapAngle() normalises to [-π, π] on read in getYaw() — the accumulator stays continuous while every consumer (MOVE rot, ema yaw, fly camera) sees a wrapped value. 6 new tests + 3250 full suite green. No protocol change. Prior: v0.2.673-alpha - HOMEPAGE POLISH (non-gameplay UI/UX pass, rebased onto v0.2.672-alpha): removed the FLY MODE toggle from the homepage (button, pill-switch CSS, and the wiring IIFE in main.js) — in-game F-key free-fly is unaffected, arenaRuntime's #btn-fly-toggle DOM sync is null-guarded. Removed the large standalone #version-label; the version is now shown slightly larger (9px → 11px, .up-row-value) inside the update-preview box below, with a static fallback row added for pre-JS paint + the regression-check version-marker gate. Made the centre panel background highly translucent (background: rgba(30,18,12,0.18), scoped to #title-centre.glass-panel only — the header glass-panel is unaffected). Added large numbered steps (1 2 3) to the left of the character selector, LOGIN WITH NOSTR, and ENTER TORII via a new .step-row/.step-num wrapper. Full suite green, regression check ALL GREEN (21/21). Diff scoped to index.html + src/main.js only — zero gameplay-file overlap with the concurrent ADR-0048/0049/0050 diagnostic work merged just ahead of this commit. Prior: v0.2.672-alpha - ADR-0050 DECOUPLE BOT_STATE BROADCAST FROM THE KAMI MODE ROSTER GATE (root-cause fix for the ~12m desync): ADR-0049 exonerated the client (socket closeCount=0, heartbeat maxGap 2.8s one-time) — the BOT_STATE stall is server-side. The server broadcast BOT_STATE only when players.length > 0, and players EXCLUDED Kami Mode sessions (ADR-0032 bots-ignore-admin), so a sole player in Kami Mode silenced the stream and froze every bot on the client. Fix: extract buildBotTickRoster + shouldBroadcastBotState into server/bots/botStateGate.js — authedCount counts every authed session (Kami or not) while the bot-brain roster still excludes Kami, and the broadcast gate keys off authedCount. Bots still ignore the admin; BOT_STATE keeps flowing. 10 new tests + 3244 full suite green. Confirm live: lastIngestAge should now stay ~66ms across Kami Mode. Prior: v0.2.671-alpha - ADR-0049 WEBSOCKET OPEN/CLOSE + MAIN-THREAD HEARTBEAT DIAGNOSTIC (split the BOT_STATE stall cause): ADR-0048 proved the stream stalls (lastIngestAge 4-7s at shot time in 3 of 4 ema). This deploy adds a pure createConnectionDiagnostics(now) factory (src/engine/diagnostics/connectionDiagnostics.js) tracking (1) the WebSocket transport+protocol lifecycle (connect/open/close/reconnect/state with ages) and (2) a main-thread heartbeat (rAF gap: lastGap/maxGap/stallCount). wsClient emits socket_connect/open/close; multiplayerHost records them; loop.js heartbeats each rAF tick; arenaRuntime captures combat.lastSentShot.conn at shot time. Read: ws.lastCloseAge small ⇒ socket dropped; heartbeat.maxGap large ⇒ main thread froze. 9 new tests + 3234 full suite green, build clean. NO gameplay change — diagnostic only. Prior: v0.2.670-alpha - ADR-0048 BOT_STATE INGEST-RATE DIAGNOSTIC (find why client bots are ~12m stale): ADR-0047 quantified the desync — the client's bots are ~12m stale vs the server (Happy: client (15.2,2.94) vs server (3.62,-1.82), ~5-10s of movement), and the server broadcasts BOT_STATE @15Hz so the stall is client-side. This deploy adds a pure diagnose(nowMs) to botNetState (tracks _ingestCount + _lastIngestAt) exposed via bots.js getBotNetDiagnostic(), captured at SHOT time into combat.lastSentShot.botNet ({ingestCount,lastIngestAge,bots:[{id,sampleCount,newestAge,oldestAge}]}). lastIngestAge ~66ms ⇒ stream flowing (desync is in the client apply/interp path); >>1s ⇒ stream stalled. 3 new tests + 3225 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. NO gameplay change — diagnostic only. Prior: v0.2.669-alpha - HOMEPAGE SIMPLIFICATION + SETTINGS PANEL MODULE + ORANGE/BRONZE RECOLOR (non-gameplay UI/UX pass, rebased onto v0.2.668-alpha): removed the chat panel, leaderboard panel, gateway-preview panel, and player-stats card from the homepage; removed the instance-settings link, project-dashboard link, and gateway-setup button; merged ENTER ARENA + ENTER NAP into a single ENTER TORII button (btn-enter-nap / elNapBtn); deleted a duplicated second title block; moved the torii icon button from top-right to top-left. New settings panel module (src/engine/settings/settingsPanel.js, nav-left/content-right) replaces the old toriiMenu popup on icon click, hosting Instance Settings + Gateway Setup tabs. Gateway Setup tab (src/engine/settings/gatewaySetupPanel.js) has exactly 3 owner-facing cards (Choose Blank, Use My World as Template, Publish My Node) — no 'Visit a Node' card, node travel goes through the Torii Gateway inside the NAP zone instead; Node Directory tab dropped entirely. Orange/bronze recolor over a torii-gate background image with a frosted-glass content panel. Removed a stray unstyled GATEWAY SETUP button IIFE and a dead #btn-enter inline-script fallback listener. Rebound tests/entry-flow-smoke.test.js to btn-enter-nap/elNapBtn/'ENTER TORII'. Updated tools/csp.mjs INLINE_SCRIPT_SHA256 to match the edited inline script. Zero overlap with combat/bot/physics/net files (bots.js, botModel.js, bot-tactics.js, botNetState.js, botSim.js, physics.js, player.js, weapons.js, scene.js, hud.js, input.js, lod.js, multiplayerHost.js, wireProtocol.js, arenaRuntime.js untouched). 3217 full suite green, check ALL GREEN. Prior: v0.2.668-alpha - ADR-0047 RENDERED BOT-POSITION DIAGNOSTIC (exposes client/server desync): ADR-0046 confirmed the camera ray is sent unaltered (usedAimRay=true + sentOrigin==aimOrigin in all 8 ema) — so the miss is NOT camera-vs-muzzle. The server SHOT-RESOLVE log shows the real cause: the SAME camera ray hits a bot on the CLIENT at 6.36m but misses on the SERVER by bodyGap=1.68m — the client's bot collider sits ~2.7m from where the server's bot actually is (a client/server bot-position desync, not a collider-radius shortfall). This deploy captures the client's RENDERED bot positions at shot time into combat.lastSentShot.bots ({id,x,z,alive,hp} via new pure snapshotBotPositions in lastShotStore.js) so a miss ema can be diffed against the server's cur=/rew= per bot id. Also added x/z to getBotRenderStates (ADR-0045). 2 new tests + 3222 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. NO gameplay change — diagnostic only. Prior: v0.2.667-alpha - ADR-0046 SENT-RAY DIAGNOSTIC (proves camera-vs-muzzle on miss): on-target shots at STATIONARY bots miss by bodyGap=0.46-0.53m (ray passes 0.81-0.88m from the capsule axis; radius 0.35m) — confirmed NOT a rewind/lag-comp issue (dxz=0.00). buildShotPayload already prefers the camera aim ray (peer-combat test proves it). The open question: at RUNTIME, is aimOrigin/aimDir actually present when SHOOT fires, or does it fall back to the muzzle ray? Only player.js emits SHOOT (always with aimOrigin=_camPos, aimDir=_camFwd). This deploy adds `combat.lastSentShot` to the ema snapshot: {ts,viewLag,usedAimRay,sentOrigin,sentDir,muzzleOrigin,muzzleDir,aimOrigin,aimDir} — the ACTUAL buildShotPayload output that went to the server, with full muzzle/aim vectors (not just Y, since Y alone couldn't distinguish camera vs barrel). Written to its OWN _lastSentShot slot (independent of _lastShot, which only exists when aimOrigin/aimDir are present) so it is NEVER stale in the usedAimRay=false failure case. usedAimRay=true + sentOrigin≈aimOrigin → camera ray sent (then bot-only collider assist); usedAimRay=false → muzzle ray (fix aimOrigin passing). Server SHOT-RESOLVE log now also records rounded origin/dir vectors + clientTs so the ema's sentOrigin/sentDir/ts can be matched numerically against what the server received. Extracted lastShot store into pure module engine/combat/lastShotStore.js (no three/Rapier) so setLastShotSent/setLastSentShot are unit-testable. 4 new tests + 3219 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. NO gameplay change — diagnostic only. Prior:  - ADR-0041 PLAYTEST UX (reliable kami exit + visible hit feedback): round-1 bot playtest exposed two usability bugs (NOT hit-reg — the server damage chain is correct). (1) Stuck in Kami Mode: ESC is browser-reserved while pointer-locked, so the 2nd ESC never reached exitKamiMode (KAMI_STATE stayed active=true); added a pointer-lock-independent ✕ exit button to the rack box that calls exitKamiMode directly. (2) "many hits and nothing": flashCross was a 0.12s crosshair blip + the stronger screen-edge flashHit was NOT wired to bot hits; BOT_HIT_BY_PLAYER now also calls flashHit (body=25→~0.55 opacity, head=50→0.8) + flashCross duration 0.12→0.25s. Ema store ENOENT was a missing VPS dir (/var/lib/torii-quest/kami); ensure() already uses recursive mkdir so it self-heals — dir created on the VPS. 3190 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. Prior: v0.2.659-alpha - ADR-0040 POLISH (declare undici dep + kind:14 doc/test accuracy): v0.2.658-alpha's `import { WebSocket } from 'undici'` resolved only TRANSITIVELY (node_modules/undici pulled in by another package — `require('module').builtinModules` has NO 'undici', so it is NOT a Node core module). Declared undici (^7.29.0) as an explicit top-level dependency so future `npm install`s are robust against transitive-dep removal. Also corrected ADR-0040's inner-rumor description (kind:14 NIP-17 private direct message, not kind:1) + added test assertions that the unwrapped rumor is kind:14 + sender pubkey === Kami's pubkey. Tool comment clarified + usage example fixed (sudo -u scrubs env → set KAMI_PRIV inside the torii-quest bash -c). Zero production behavior change (deployed v0.2.658-alpha already worked via transitive undici; VPS dry-run re-confirmed after this bump). 3190 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. Prior: v0.2.658-alpha - ADR-0040 HOTFIX (undici WebSocket for Node relay publish): v0.2.657-alpha's tools/kami-nostr-reply.mjs fell back to a non-existent global WebSocket in Node 20 (no undici import), so every relay publish returned 'no-websocket' and the NIP-17 DM never left the VPS. Fix: import WebSocket from Node's built-in undici (browser-API compatible) + pass as WebSocketCtor; robustify onmessage data-coercion for Buffer(ws) vs string(undici). Smoke-tested live: published a real kind:1059 to the owner's npub, accepted by 3/4 relays (plebeian staging + nos.lol + primal); subscribed + confirmed the gift-wrap outer signers are all ephemeral (not Kami's real key ea3ff08e…), #p tag = owner. In-game rack path unaffected. 3190 full suite green, build clean, check ALL GREEN. Prior: v0.2.657-alpha - NOSTR-NATIVE KAMI CHAT BRIDGE (ADR-0040 Stage 1: Kami replies now dual-write — appended to replies.jsonl for the in-game emagake rack (ADR-0039 path unchanged) AND published as a native NIP-17 gift-wrapped DM (kind:1059) to the owner's npub, so Kami replies show up as real Nostr direct messages in Buzz / Amethyst / Nostir. Corrected premise before coding: the browser CANNOT decrypt NIP-44/NIP-17 without window.nostr.nip44 (most extensions don't expose it), so the in-game rack keeps the HTTP replies.jsonl path; Kami (VPS, nostr-tools) only needs the owner's PUBLIC npub to publish. New server module server/kami/kamiNostr.js (buildGiftWrap + publishEventToRelay) + tools/kami-nostr-reply.mjs (dual-write CLI, runs as torii-quest user) + src/engine/kami/kamiNostrCap.js (browser NIP-07 feature detection, foundation for future in-game decrypt, not wired into the rack). Added nostr-tools as a server-only dep (NOT in the browser bundle — confirmed via build). 15 new tests + 3189 full suite green, build clean.) Prior: v0.2.656-alpha - KAMI HANG HOTKEY COPY FIX (ADR-0039 hotfix: the empty-rack + tray-counter copy told the owner to press CTRL+E / CTRL+SHIFT+E to hang an ema, but the actual hotkey has been Shift+K since ADR-0031 unified the editor onto the bare K key. Both stale strings corrected to "SHIFT+K TO HANG". 3175 full suite green, build clean.) Prior: v0.2.655-alpha - 2-WAY EMA COMMS / AI REPLIES IN THE EMAGAKE RACK (ADR-0039: the AI can now reply to the owner's ema and the reply renders in the in-game emagake rack. Direction 1 user→AI unchanged from ADR-0038 — ema sealed in-browser, persisted as ciphertext, AI decrypts with the rotated Kami private key. Direction 2 AI→user is NEW and plaintext, because the browser cannot decrypt kamiSeal envelopes (NIP-07 has no ECDH, and the seal is custom ECDH+HKDF+AES-GCM, not NIP-04/NIP-44) — so an AI reply sealed the same way would be unreadable in-game. Instead a separate `replies.jsonl` feed: `server/kami/kamiReplyStore.js` (append-only, text cap 2000 / quote cap 280) + `server/kami/kamiReplyRoute.js` (pure `parseSince` + `shapeReplyResponse`) + admin-gated `GET /mp/kami/replies?since=<ts>` route in arena-ws.js (owner Bearer token, returns only rows newer than the high-water mark). AI posts via `tools/kami-reply.mjs` run ON THE VPS — reads reply text from `--text-file`/stdin, never trusts shell quoting, validates+caps+appends atomically. Client `kamiMode.js` polls every ~5s while in Kami Mode + owner token present, stops on exit; `emagakePanel.js` renders reply rows as a distinct block above the ema rack (`ema-row kami-reply`, ⛩ stud), newest-first, deduped by id via `mergeReplies`, textContent only never innerHTML. Each reply is self-contained (optional short `quote` of the ema it replies to) since the browser can't decrypt the original. Also deployed the rotated KAMI_PUBKEY `ea3ff08e…` from ADR-0038. 24 new tests + 3175 full suite green, build clean.) Prior: **v0.2.654-alpha** - REOPEN ALL BOARDS ON EVERY TRIGGER PRESS (ADR-0037: setBoardsActive(true) now always force-shows all three boards instead of short-circuiting when already active — fixes the bug where pressing Q a second time after closing the boards only reopened the auction panel, leaving the three boards hidden, because hideOwnerBoard() never reset `_active`. 11 close-button tests + 3151 full suite green.) Prior: **v0.2.653-alpha** - PRODUCT SIGN PROXIMITY+Q TRIGGER (ADR-0036: the auction-panel + three ADR-0035 owner boards no longer auto-show on NAP-zone entry and have zero relationship to Kami Mode — they open only via a proximity+Q trigger on the in-world PRODUCT sign mesh (`product-stall-panel`), each panel with its own explicit close/X button. 25 new tests (15 product-panel-trigger + 10 product-panel-close-buttons) + 3150 full suite green.) Prior: **v0.2.652-alpha** - KAMI VISUAL UNIFICATION + OWNER PRODUCT/AUCTION BOARDS (ADR-0034 + ADR-0035, one deploy: ADR-0034 replaces the full-screen darkened `#kami-overlay` ema editor with an inline bottom bar in the same translucent panel language as the emagake rack — world stays fully visible at all times, no backdrop dimming; a 3rd K press while the editor is already open highlights it instead of opening a second one; `setMarketActive` is now gated off `kamiActive()` so no panel auto-pops over Kami Mode's own components. ADR-0035 replaces the single hardcoded-auction `#auction-panel` with three separate NAP-zone DOM board overlays — Live Products, Live Auctions, Past Auctions — sourced from the owner's own npub via generalized `plebeianRelay.js` (NIP-99 kind:30402 products, kind:30408/1023 auctions/bids), built as `boardModel.js` + `boardPanel.js` + `ownerBoards.js`; visible to any guest, not gated to Kami Mode or the owner; DOM overlays only in this slice, an in-world 3D board render is deferred to the future napplet-architecture work. Also folded in: historical ADRs 0025/0026/0028/0029/0030/0031 fully rewritten in place to read "emagake" throughout (footnote-only approach from ADR-0033 reversed per explicit owner instruction — see ADR-0033's Amendment); `setTimeout` allowlist gate extended to cover `kamiMode.js`/`marketStall.js`/`plebeianRelay.js`'s existing revert/reconnect timers. 28 new tests (9 board-model + 9 board-panel + 8 reduce-author-events + 2 note-bar tests folded into kami-state-machine.test.js) + 3125 full suite green, build clean, regression check all green.) Prior: **v0.2.651-alpha** (EMAGAKE SPELLING FIX — ADR-0033: "emakake" was a spelling mistake; the correct romanization of 絵馬掛け is `emagake` (rendaku k→g), confirmed against Japanese-language sources. Renamed everywhere visible to a Japanese-literate reader — DOM IDs, the `emagakePanel.js` module (renamed from `emakakePanel.js`), exported names, CSS, tests, tracking docs. Historical ADRs 0025/0026/0028–0031 keep their original text with a correction footnote appended rather than being rewritten. 3097 full suite green, zero behavior change. [Amended 2026-08-23: footnote approach reversed per explicit owner instruction — see the v0.2.652-alpha entry above and ADR-0033's Amendment section; ADRs 0025/0026/0028/0029/0030/0031 are now fully rewritten to say "emagake" throughout, footnotes removed.]) Prior: **v0.2.650-alpha** (KAMI SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE INVINCIBILITY — ADR-0032: the admin was still killable by bots/peers in Kami Mode because invincibility never left the client; the server independently resolves all MP combat with zero knowledge of Kami Mode. Fix adds a verified `KAMI_STATE` client→server message (server re-checks the sender's own session pubkey against the admin pubkey — never trusts the client's claim alone), excludes the admin from the bot AI's per-tick target roster, and backstops the bot ray-cast + peer/bot damage-resolution call sites. 8 new tests (wire protocol round-trip, host sender, client enter/exit call sites) + 3097 full suite green.) Prior: **v0.2.649-alpha** (KAMI HOTKEY FIX — ADR-0031: Ctrl/Cmd+E was silently eaten by Brave's address-bar shortcut (no page keydown at all). Switched to bare `K`; Ctrl/Cmd+K explicitly excluded. 2 new tests + 3093 full suite green.)
