| Source version | **v0.2.773-alpha** (build truth — see "Current version" changelog line at the top of this file for what shipped last; historical narrative below retained for archive purposes only, last updated at v0.2.453-alpha and NOT maintained since). v0.2.423 adds render budget v1: 75K four-segment grass plus tiered bloom, shadow-map, and muzzle-light budgets. v0.2.422 makes service-worker registration/precache deploy-base aware and hardens the emitted CSP/bootstrap path; v0.2.421 adds MP net smoothness with real MOVE velocity + immediate PING on WELCOME. v0.2.404 removes the ACC-3 owner write-authority gate from the per-frame MOVE/SHOT handlers in `server/arena-ws.js` (presence/combat are AUTH-time-gated peer actions, not owner mutations) so non-owner peers replicate instead of freezing at spawn. v0.2.403 removes the title-screen MARKET/product card → product proof-surface now in the NAP zone, and splits the gateway card into "your friends" (mutual kind:3 follows) + "arenas" (`created_at` DESC) via pure `src/engine/gateway/gatewaySections.js`. v0.2.400 is ACC-2b owner-editable arrival mode — signed kind:30078 access settings let the instance owner persist only `public` or `follows-only`, reads verify owner/d-tag/id/sig/schema and choose the latest valid event, deploy seam remains the local security floor, relay failure falls back to cached-or-deploy without ever hardcoding public, and unsupported `whitelist` / `invite-only` fail closed to deny-all. v0.2.386 is a COMBAT-FEEL fix on top of v0.2.385 — two changes, no logic/parity-lock/lag-comp touched. (1) Augustink render-scale shrunk `BOSS_TARGET_HEIGHT` 2.5→2.0m in `src/config.js` (~1.2× the ~1.7m player; render-only, boss HP/SPEED/DAMAGE/RADIUS/SHOOT_CD unchanged; `botModel.js` scaling still plants feet at y=0). (2) Bot hit capsule widened ~+15% for hitbox forgiveness — the capsule was narrower than the visual bot model (arms/shoulders), so shots that visually hit the body missed; `BOT_BODY_RADIUS` 0.26→0.30 and `BOT_HEAD_RADIUS` 0.20→0.23 in BOTH the server ray-test colliders (`server/bots/botColliders.js`) and the client Rapier mirror (`src/engine/physics/bodies.js`), kept in parity. Body/head centres (0.76/1.55) unchanged so the foot stays planted (server bottom cap = foot+radius; client capsule bottom = centre−halfH−radius = 0); the capsule only widens (+ a touch taller). The client's derived `BOT_BODY_CENTRE_Y_OFFSET` recomputes 0.76→0.80 (halfH+radius) which keeps the foot planted; the server's explicit `BOT_BODY_CENTRE_Y=0.76` is untouched. Classifier head-proximity backstop derives from `BOT_HEAD_RADIUS` so it tracks the new radius; no damage values, hit zones, `damageTable`, parity lock, or the v0.2.385 lag-comp (botSnapshotRing/rewind/recordSnapshot) or v0.2.383 event-authoritative fix changed. `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`; single-player byte-identical apart from the wider capsule + smaller boss. v0.2.385 is BOT LAG-COMPENSATION for player→bot shots on top of v0.2.384 — player→peer combat already rewound each peer to the shot ts before ray-testing, but player→bot resolved against bots' CURRENT positions while clients render bots ~100ms in the past (`interpDelayMs=100`), so a bot sliding ~2.2 m/s (~0.22m/100ms ≈ capsule radius 0.26) ate shots the player correctly landed on the lagged render. Fix mirrors the peer path: new PURE `server/bots/botSnapshotRing.js` (fixed-capacity ring, records `{ts, bots:[{id,x,z,footY,radius,alive}]}` at the ~20Hz sim tick; `sampleBotsAt` clamps to newest at/after newest.ts, clamps to OLDEST before oldest.ts (no NaN), else interpolates the flanking pair by bot id); `arenaBotSim.recordSnapshot(ts)` stamps it each tick, `resolvePlayerShot(origin,dir,shotTs,now,lagCompMs)` rewinds to `Math.max(now-lagComp, min(shotTs,now))` before `buildBotColliders`+`rayVsBot` (boss `radius/BOT_R` scaling preserved on the rewound row; 2-arg call still falls back to current positions); `server/arena-ws.js` passes `shotMsg.ts` + the shared `now` + `LAG_COMP_MS` through `resolvePlayerShot` (same ts the peer resolver uses) and records a bot snapshot each bot tick; `[SHOT-RESOLVE]` diagnostic enriched with `shotTs`+`rwClamp`. No BOT_HP/BODY/HEADSHOT/BOSS stat, hit-zone, damageTable, parity-lock, or v0.2.383 event-authoritative fix changes; `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`; single-player byte-identical. v0.2.384 is a UI/LEADERBOARD/STATS TRUTH PASS on top of v0.2.383 — combat path untouched. (1) Augustink boss render-scale shrunk ~3.2m→~2.5m via the single `BOSS_TARGET_HEIGHT` render constant (combat HP/SPEED/DAMAGE/RADIUS unchanged). (2) Homescreen leaderboard card de-mocked: title `SCORE — LEADERBOARD` (dropped the `3 · ` prefix), all mock rows (plebshot/nostrich/npub1demo) removed, honest empty/loading state when there is no real local data. (3) The LOCAL board now lists EVERY player who joined this arena instance INCLUDING disconnected ones — `server/combat/scoreLedger.js` gains `retire(id)` (keep the row, mark disconnected) replacing the disconnect-time `drop()`, a reconnect-rekey in `register()` (a returning npub resumes its tally, no double-count), and a `snapshot(limit)` cap so the wire stays ≤32 tallies. (4) Homescreen preview + personal stats (SATS/KILLS/RUNS + ACC ring) now read the SAME authoritative source as the board via a new `EV.SCORE_FRAME` (raw server SCORE frame) — `arenaRuntime` emits it, `main.js` renders the preview from live tallies, `playerStats.js` adopts the self-row for KILLS; single-player byte-identical (no SCORE frames → local fallback). (5) LOGIN button is now a solid mint/green primary CTA. (6) Homescreen bolt logo replaced with an inline monochrome torii-gate + small bolt SVG in both title spots. New tests: score-ledger retire/rekey/limit. v0.2.383 is the REAL player→bot combat FIX for the v0.2.382-diagnosed miss. The server was resolving hits correctly all along; the bug was CLIENT-SIDE: `applyBotHit`/`applyBotKill` set `bot.state.hp`/`alive` immediately, but the next `_syncNetBot` frame re-read `pose.hp`/`pose.alive` from `botNetState.sample()` — the STALE latest BOT_STATE snapshot (~15Hz/~67ms) — and clobbered them (HP snapped back to full, a killed bot flickered alive) for up to one snapshot interval, so the player saw "shots not registering / bots not dying". Fix (Option A): new `botNetState.applyHit(id,hp)` / `applyKill(id)` fold the out-of-band event into `b.hp`/`b.alive` (+ snap on kill) so the next sample is event-authoritative — no clobber; position stays interpolated. Respawn (BOT_STATE alive false→true) still snaps back in; single-player (`_syncBot`), bot→player, and boss all unchanged. New race tests in `tests/multiplayer/bot-net-state.test.js`; the 7 v0.2.382 combat tests stay green. `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`. v0.2.382 is a COMBAT diagnostic build for a reported one-directional break — player→bot shots not registering while bot→player works. Full vertical-coordinate-chain trace + headless repro proved the server geometry HITS in every realistic scenario (peer footY = eye−1.7 = sampleArenaHeight = bot collider footY = client visual bot foot; SHOT wire codec is y-pass-through), so the hypothesised ~0.8m frame mismatch does NOT reproduce headlessly. Per the spec's decision gate, shipped NO speculative fix — instead enriched the ≤1/sec `[SHOT-RESOLVE]` log with `originY`/`nearBot`/`botFootY`/`dy` (via `arenaBotSim.nearestBotDiag`) to confirm the Y-frame on a LIVE server, plus regression tests (`tests/multiplayer/player-bot-combat.test.js`) locking player→bot torso+head HITS for regular AND boss + a clear miss. `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`, single-player byte-identical. v0.2.381 brings back AUGUSTINK as a server-authoritative BOSS bot — a per-bot stat archetype (kind/name/HP~60/slow/high-damage/bigger capsule) layered on the v0.2.377 bot roster: 1 boss/arena, own lazy-loaded `augustink4.glb` + "Augustink" nameplate, synced to all players, single-player byte-identical except the boss is present; additive `kind`/`name`/`scale` on the boss BOT_STATE row only, `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`. v0.2.380 wires the LIVE IN-ARENA LEADERBOARD end-to-end — `server/arena-ws.js` broadcasts the authoritative `SCORE` frame DURING play (on every player-kill + a ~5s `SCORE_TICK_MS` interval gated on `SCORE_ENABLED`), not only on close; a new pure `src/engine/multiplayer/arenaLeaderboard.js` is mounted in `src/arenaRuntime.js` as a toggleable overlay (L / Tab) whose LOCAL tab renders live server tallies with 0 prompts / no signer, a GLOBAL tab reads signed scores back via `leaderboardRelayRead`+`fanoutReq` (read-only, graceful empty/offline), and the footer proxies the existing `#leaderboard-publish-btn` for an opt-in NIP-07 publish (one click, never auto); additive on `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1`. v0.2.379 is a PERFORMANCE build over v0.2.378 — DPR cap 2→1.5, adaptive quality tier (rolling-FPS HIGH/NORMAL/LOW stepping DPR + gating bloom with hysteresis), and a flag-gated debug perf HUD; single-player byte-identical. v0.2.378 is a combat/targeting HOTFIX over v0.2.377 — forces authoritative MP resolution, lifts bot shots into world space, and adds bot target-switch hysteresis; v0.2.377 makes the extracted bot AI server-authoritative — server runs `botSim` on a fixed tick against the live player roster and streams `BOT_STATE`/`BOT_SHOT`/`BOT_HIT`/`BOT_KILL`; clients are render-only in MP, local AI unchanged in single-player, chunk 2 of the bot milestone. v0.2.376 extracted the pure headless bot AI into `src/engine/entities/botSim.js`, chunk 1). Live on chiefmonkey.art/quest/ is **v0.2.402-alpha** — DEPLOYED via Suite v0.7.21-alpha, which fixed `install-quest.sh`'s dirty-checkout deploy abort (generated-but-tracked `public/dashboard.html`+`public/torii-quest-data.json` are rewritten by `npm run build` on every deploy). Open edges fixed in this source: (1) two players cannot see each other (H4) — the peer-avatar `GLTFLoader` in `arenaRuntime.js` was missing a `DRACOLoader`, so `chiefmonkey6.glb` rejected and the roster entry was deleted → peers invisible (fixed v0.2.371); (2) repeating NIP-07 signer prompt on the pause/idle screen — no client keepalive → server idle-dropped silent sessions at 60s → reconnect → re-prompt loop (fixed v0.2.372, keepalive PING); (3) peers rendered high in the sky, backwards, and T-posed — the peer `avatarLoader` returned the raw `gltf.scene` with no feet offset, no π facing, no `AnimationMixer` (fixed v0.2.373: template cache + SkeletonUtils.clone + wrapper offset −gMinY−1.7 + rotation π + IDLE mixer driven by `obj.update(dt)`); (4) players could not damage each other — the client never sent `SHOT` and never handled inbound `HIT`/`KILL` despite the server being authoritative (fixed v0.2.374: two `arenaRuntime.js` seams delegating to a pure `peerCombat.js` — AIM-ray SHOT out, `mp_hit`/`mp_kill` gated on `selfId`, `mp_shot` visual-only); (5) the arena re-prompted the NIP-07 signer on every entry/reconnect — the per-session NIP-42 (kind:22242) challenge is anti-replay so it can't be cached (fixed v0.2.375: a one-time NIP-98 kind:27235 login sign mints a server-issued session token stored as sha256 only; the WS replays the additive `AUTH_TOKEN` frame, NIP-42 kept as fallback; presence auto-publish on login dropped). Source-only; the maintainer must bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.379-alpha (or the merge commit), then a Suite redeploy (builds `--base=/quest/`) will include all five before they work end-to-end. MP-3 Nostr score+leaderboard shipping. |

| Active slice | **N2N TRAVEL TEST WITH BEKKA** (2026-08-31) — chiefmonkey.art/quest/ ↔ torii.plebeian.build; verify open-visit travel + presence discovery (ADR-0081). Both live again after the HashIT outage. (Prior slice was v0.2.244 HOST-SAFE CANONICAL ZONE ROUTE — fixes the v0.2.243 follow-up: the live rendered screenshot of `/zone/plebeian-market-bazaar/` STILL showed the JSON 404 (`{"detail":"No static asset at /zone/plebeian-market-bazaar"}`). ROOT CAUSE: the published exact-path static host (chiefmonkey.art) has NO SPA rewrite and NO directory index and normalises BOTH `/zone/<slug>` AND `/zone/<slug>/` to an exact static-asset lookup → 404, so EVERY `/zone/*` PATH strategy fails (v0.2.242 extensionless → octet-stream download; v0.2.243 directory-index shell → 404). Only the root `/` reliably serves `index.html` as `text/html`. FIX (no backend): the canonical zone route is now the URL FRAGMENT `/#/zone/<slug>` — the fragment is never sent to the server, so the request path is always `/` and the root shell ALWAYS renders on hard refresh; the client parser reads the fragment. `zoneRouteFor` + `handoffRouteFor` build `/#/zone/<slug>`; the portal allowlists are `/#/zone/`; `main._applyZoneRoute` reads the URL hash fragment (+ a `hashchange` listener) and falls back to the path for a LEGACY `/zone/<slug>` link, which the parser still resolves client-side (NON-CANONICAL: a cold `/zone/*` deep-link 404s before the bundle loads, so it is never generated/shared). No per-slug static shell is generated any more (the build step + `tools/zoneShells.mjs` + `tools/generate-zone-shells.mjs` were removed); the dist ships NO `/zone/*` file. Preserves the v0.2.240 service-worker fail-soft precache, the v0.2.238 fail-closed loop, and v0.2.236 NIP-07 login decoupling; root entry flow + ENTER ARENA + ESC pause unchanged. |

- 🏆 **v0.2.603-alpha — 0l component seam release (PRs #79/#80/#81 + integration).** The gameplay→component seam, proven in all three modes: 0l.1 static data expansion (arena.crates, PR #79) + 0l.2 runtime mount/unmount (componentBeacon, PR #80) + 0l.3 portal/NAP data shell (torii.gateway, PR #81). Integration branch `phase0l-complete` merges the stack + adds `tests/0l-integration.test.js` (3 tests) locking the real chiefmonkey-template manifest: component instances (arena.crates + torii.gateway), resolver expands to 9 crates + 1 gateway, runtime host mounts the data shell with zero scene.add(). Curated counts 2838/209 → 2869/214. Tag `v0.2.603-alpha` pushed. NON-GOALS held: NPC/stickers + combat/bots deferred until live validation; gateway travel-into-NAP deferred (0l.3 data shell only).
- 🏆 **v0.2.380-alpha — live in-arena leaderboard wired end-to-end (the "Both" path).** The scaffolding (scoreLedger, leaderboardPanel, livePublish, leaderboardRelayRead, the `_publishMyScore` button) already existed and was tested/pure; this slice wires it LIVE. (1) SERVER — `server/arena-ws.js` already tallied via `addKill`/`addDamage` but only emitted `SCORE` on session close; it now broadcasts the authoritative `SCORE` frame DURING play — immediately after each player-kill `addKill` and on a ~5s `SCORE_TICK_MS` `setInterval` gated on `SCORE_ENABLED` — via `broadcastScoreFrame()` (no-op on empty tallies). (2) CLIENT — new pure `src/engine/multiplayer/arenaLeaderboard.js` (`talliesToCurrentEvents` maps server tallies → aggregate-parseable current events with hex-npub filtering + count clamping; `createArenaLeaderboard` builds the overlay with all impure edges injected — document/fetchGlobal/onPublish/canPublish — so it is node-testable) is mounted in `src/arenaRuntime.js` and bound to L / Tab (collision-checked); `mp_score` frames feed the LOCAL tab as the DEFAULT view (0 prompts, session-scoped, works with NO signer since tally npubs come from the server). (3) PUBLISH — the overlay footer proxies `document.getElementById('leaderboard-publish-btn')?.click()`, reusing the existing main.js NIP-07 handler, so the opt-in publish is one click and never auto-fires. (4) GLOBAL tab reads signed score events back via `readLeaderboardEvents`+`buildScoreFilter` over `fanoutReq(RELAYS,…)` (read-only, 0 prompts, graceful empty/offline cache). Additive on `PROTOCOL_VERSION=1` (no protocol bump). Verified: `npm run build`, `npm run check`, `npm test` all green; new `tests/multiplayer/arena-leaderboard.test.js` + `tests/multiplayer/live-score-broadcast.test.js`. No bot/combat/perf-tier change; no auto-publish-on-death; `godMode` false.
- ⚡ **v0.2.379-alpha — performance build: DPR cap + adaptive quality tier + bloom gate + perf HUD.** GPU fill-rate reduction so two-browser playtests (and weaker hardware) stay smooth (diagnosis confirmed the lag is GPU-side, not network). Four changes: (1) main renderer DPR cap lowered 2→1.5 in `src/scene.js` (matches the existing mirror cap; ~4x→~2.25x pixels at 2x DPR, imperceptible). (2) NEW `src/engine/render/qualityTier.js` — a pure, no-alloc rolling frame-time monitor (60-frame ring) with HIGH (DPR 1.5, bloom on) / NORMAL (1.25, on) / LOW (1.0, bloom off) tiers; steps DOWN when avg FPS <45 for ~2s and UP when >55 for ~3s, with sustained-hold hysteresis + a stall guard so alt-tab spikes don't flap it. On change it re-applies `setPixelRatio`/`setSize` + `composer.setSize` + toggles `bloomPass.enabled`. (3) Bloom quality gate — LOW tier disables the UnrealBloom pass (handled by the tier). (4) NEW `src/engine/render/perfHud.js` — a debug DOM overlay behind `window.__toriiPerf` (or `ToriiDebug.perf`), throttled ~250ms, showing FPS/frame-ms/draw-calls/triangles/DPR/tier/bot+peer counts; zero cost (no DOM) when the flag is off. Wired into `arenaRuntime.update()`: tier fed the frame delta before `renderFrame`, `renderer.info` sampled + HUD refreshed after. Single-player + MP identical; gameplay byte-identical; `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 1. Verified: `npm run build`, `npm run check`, `npm test` all green; new `tests/render/qualityTier.test.js` + `perfHud.test.js`.
- 🔧 **v0.2.378-alpha — combat/targeting hotfix: hits register + bots pick the right target.** Field reports after the v0.2.377 deploy: bullets didn't register on bots or peers, bots shot the player for no damage, and bots chased a far player while a close one stood next to them. Three root causes: (1) `MP_MODE` could be a stale `advisory` in the live `.env` → the `SHOT` case skipped `resolveAndBroadcast`, so no player→peer / player→bot resolution ran. `advisory` was retired when the client dropped inbound HIT (v0.2.374); `server/arena-ws.js` now FORCES `MP_MODE='authoritative'` in code (env is read only to warn) and calls `resolveAndBroadcast` unconditionally on every SHOT. (2) Bot shots used the sim-local `y=EYE_Y≈0.9` as an absolute world height, so on the raised island the bot→player ray started under the floor and missed. The pure `shotCallback(origin,dir)` gained a 3rd `target` arg (player world-eye); both callers (`src/bots.js` + `server/bots/arenaBotSim.js`) lift the origin to `footY+origin.y` and re-aim `normalize(target−worldOrigin)` — the sim's spread is intentionally dropped in favour of a clean world-space aim. (3) Target selection was sticky: `botSim` now tracks its target by a stable key and switches only when a rival is very close (≤8m and nearer) or materially nearer (<70% of the held distance) — no flicker on similar distances, and single-player (one id-less player) never switches so it stays byte-identical. Added a rate-limited `[SHOT-RESOLVE]` diagnostic (~1/s per shooter) as a safety net on the resolve pipeline. `PROTOCOL_VERSION` unchanged (1). Verified: `npm run build`, `npm run check`, `npm test` all green; new hysteresis + world-target tests in `tests/multiplayer/bot-target-select.test.js`. Source-only; do NOT touch the VPS.
- 🔧 **v0.2.375-alpha — session-token arena auth: "1 sign at login, 0 signs in-game."** The arena authed with a per-session NIP-42 (kind:22242) challenge; because it is anti-replay it cannot be cached, so every arena entry and every reconnect re-prompted the NIP-07 signer — with the score-publish and login presence-publish signs that was ~5 prompts. Fix: a one-time login sign mints a server-issued opaque bearer token that the arena WS reuses. SERVER — new pure `server/auth/sessionTokens.js` (one-time-use challenge TTL 60s; token TTL 8h; stores ONLY `sha256(token)`, never the raw token) + two plain-HTTP endpoints in `server/arena-ws.js` (`GET /mp/auth-challenge`, `POST /mp/session` — verifies a NIP-98 kind:27235 event via the shared `verifyNostrEventSig`, returns `{token, npub}`); a new `finishAuth()` unifies WELCOME/JOIN across the AUTH_TOKEN and NIP-42 branches. CLIENT — new `src/engine/multiplayer/sessionAuth.js` (HTTP base derived from the same `MP_WS_PATH` mount as the WS URL — never a hard-coded `/mp`; sessionStorage token store; one-sign login), `src/nostr.js` signs once then falls back to `getPublicKey()`, `wsClient.js` sends the additive `AUTH_TOKEN` frame when a token is stored and only calls `signAuth` (NIP-42) as fallback; a rejected token (AUTH_FAIL) self-clears and reconnects onto the NIP-42 path. Dropped the auto presence-publish on `NOSTR_LOGIN` (presence is WS-roster only); in-game score signs were already zero (`createScoreReporter` is never invoked; the manual "PUBLISH MY SCORE" button stays). `PROTOCOL_VERSION` unchanged (1; AUTH_TOKEN additive). Verified: `npm run build` green, `npm run check` green, `npm test` green; new `tests/multiplayer/session-tokens.test.js` + `session-auth.test.js` + AUTH_TOKEN wire tests + wsClient token-auth tests. **LIVE v0.2.370-alpha keeps re-prompting until a Suite redeploy at `TORII_QUEST_REF=v0.2.379-alpha`** — source-only; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active. `godMode` false; no network/deploy/publish/tag.
- 🔧 **v0.2.374-alpha — peer combat wired client-side: players can damage + kill each other (server-authoritative).** The server already ran authoritative combat (`MP_MODE='authoritative'`: `SHOT` → `resolveAndBroadcast` → `HIT`/`KILL`), but the client never sent `SHOT` and never handled inbound `HIT`/`KILL`, so shots never crossed the wire. Fix: two seams in `src/arenaRuntime.js`, both delegating to a new pure `src/engine/multiplayer/peerCombat.js` (kept testable + free of three/DOM). OUTBOUND — the `EV.SHOOT` handler sends `_mp.sendShot({origin,dir,ts})` for every arena shot when `_mp.selfId` is set and the player is in the arena (`shouldSendShot`), using the AIM ray (`aimOrigin`/`aimDir`) so server hit-detection matches the crosshair, falling back to the muzzle ray (`buildShotPayload`); the server no-ops when it hits no peer. INBOUND — `_mpEmit` delegates `mp_shot`/`mp_hit`/`mp_kill` to `createPeerCombat`: `mp_shot` is VISUAL ONLY (muzzle spark + short tracer via `fx.js`; skips our own echo; NO local hit detection), `mp_hit` applies `takeDamage(dmg)` only when `targetId === selfId` and flashes the crosshair when we shot (wire field `id`), `mp_kill` runs the transition-guarded `killPlayer()` when `victimId === selfId` (deaths bumped once — no double-count vs `takeDamage`) and scores `state.kills++` + a killfeed line when `shooterId === selfId`. Local bot hits stay a SEPARATE client-side path (a shot may hit a bot locally AND resolve a peer hit server-side — expected, not deduped). NO wire-protocol change; bots remain client-side (separate MP milestone, NOT bundled). Verified: `npm run check` green, `npm run build` green, `npm test` green; new `tests/multiplayer/peer-combat.test.js`. **LIVE v0.2.370-alpha has no peer combat until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.374** (bundles v0.2.371 DRACOLoader + v0.2.372 keepalive + v0.2.373 avatar transform + this) — source-only; the maintainer must bump the Suite Quest ref before a redeploy (`--base=/quest/`) picks it up; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active. `godMode` false; no network/deploy/publish/tag.
- 🔧 **v0.2.373-alpha — peer avatars rendered high in the sky, backwards, and T-posed fixed: peer avatarLoader returned the raw `gltf.scene`.** After the v0.2.371 DRACOLoader fix peers became visible but appeared ~4-5 body-heights UP, FACING 180° away, and in a static T-POSE. Root cause: the peer `avatarLoader` in `src/arenaRuntime.js` resolved the RAW `gltf.scene` with none of the setup `playerModel.js`/`botModel.js` apply — (1) chiefmonkey6.glb geometry sits above its origin (large `gMinY`), so with no feet offset the model floats at its authored height (and `sendMove` broadcasts eye-height Y ≈ 1.7, compounding it); (2) the GLB faces +Z while game-forward is −Z, so with no `rotation.y = π` the avatar faced backwards; (3) no `AnimationMixer` → skeleton stuck in bind-pose (T-pose). Fix (mirror `botModel.js` + `playerModel.js` transform): load chiefmonkey6.glb ONCE into a module-level template (scene + clips + geometry `gMinY` via `geometry.boundingBox`), then per peer `SkeletonUtils.clone` into a wrapper `THREE.Group` with `model.position.y = -gMinY - 1.7`, `model.rotation.y = Math.PI`, opaque materials + `frustumCulled=false`, and an `AnimationMixer` playing `Idle_03` (looped; falls back to `animations[0]` + warn) ticked once to leave bind-pose. The wrapper exposes `obj.update(dt) → mixer.update(dt)`; `src/engine/multiplayer/remoteAvatars.js` `tick(renderTime)` now derives a clamped wall-clock `dt` (skips first tick) and calls `entry.obj.update(dt)` per frame. NO movement-based clip switching (IDLE only; WALK-on-move noted as MP-1.5 in a comment); NO `sendMove`/wire-protocol change. Verified: `npm run check` green, `npm run build` green, `npm test` green; new `tests/multiplayer/remote-avatars.test.js` cases assert `tick` drives `obj.update(dt)`, skips the first tick, clamps a large gap, and is safe without an `update()`. **LIVE v0.2.370-alpha keeps peers mis-transformed until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.373** (bundles the v0.2.371 DRACOLoader + v0.2.372 keepalive + this fix) — source-only; the maintainer must bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.373-alpha (or the merge commit) before a redeploy (`--base=/quest/`) picks it up; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active. `godMode` false; no network/deploy/publish/tag.
- 🔧 **v0.2.372-alpha — repeating NIP-07 signer-prompt loop on the pause/idle screen fixed: client keepalive PING.** On the pause/idle screen a paused client sends no MOVE frames, so its connection goes silent. The server idle-drops silent sessions after 60s (`IDLE_DISCONNECT_MS` in `server/arena-ws.js`) and never initiates its own PING, and `src/engine/multiplayer/wsClient.js` only replied with a PONG to a server PING that never came. So a paused session was dropped at ~60s → `ws.onclose` → `_scheduleReconnect` → reconnect → fresh `HELLO` + NIP-42 challenge → `signAuth` → **another NIP-07 signer prompt**, looping every ~60s while paused. Fix (client-side, minimal): `wsClient.js` now sends a keepalive `{ t: MSG.PING, ts: now() }` every 25s (`KEEPALIVE_MS`) while CONNECTED — started in the WELCOME handler (`_startKeepalive`) and cleared in `disconnect()` and `onclose` (`_stopKeepalive`), using the injectable `setTimeoutFn`/`clearTimeoutFn` seams (recursive setTimeout, single-instance guard). NO server change and NO session-token/auth-caching scheme (NIP-42 challenges are per-session + anti-replay, `arena-ws.js:206`); a *genuine* network drop still reconnects + re-auths — a code comment notes the future hardening (server-issued session token). `wsClient.js` is already on the `tools/regression-check.mjs` [3] setTimeout allowlist. Verified: `npm run check` green, `npm run build` green, `npm test` green; new keepalive unit tests added to `tests/multiplayer/ws-client-state.test.js` (asserts keepalive starts on CONNECTED, sends a PING + re-arms on each tick, and is cleared on both `disconnect()` and socket close). No wire/protocol/gameplay/physics/CSP/Nostr change. **LIVE v0.2.370-alpha keeps looping until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.372** (bundles the v0.2.371 DRACOLoader peer-visibility fix + this keepalive fix) — source-only; the maintainer must bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.372-alpha (or the merge commit) before a redeploy (`--base=/quest/`) picks it up; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active. `godMode` false; no network/deploy/publish/tag.
- 🔧 **v0.2.371-alpha — H4 peer-visibility fixed: peer-avatar GLTFLoader was missing a DRACOLoader.** With v0.2.370 deployed, anonymous ENTER ARENA now boots on chiefmonkey.art/quest/, but **two players still cannot see each other** in the arena. Root cause: `src/arenaRuntime.js` (~line 397, MP wiring) built the peer-avatar `GLTFLoader` (`_mpGltf`) WITHOUT a `DRACOLoader` — the ONLY loader touching `chiefmonkey6.glb` that lacked one (`playerModel.js:84-87`, `botModel.js:29-31`, `arena.js:178-181`, `firstPersonBody.js:35-38` all set one). `chiefmonkey6.glb` is Draco-compressed, so `_mpGltf.load(assetUrl('/chiefmonkey6.glb'))` REJECTED (Draco mesh can't decode) for every peer → `remoteAvatars.upsert()` caught the reject and DELETED the roster entry (`remoteAvatars.js:73-77`) → peer avatars never rendered. Wire-level peer discovery already works (`peerJoin`→`roster.upsert` at `multiplayerHost.js:138`, verified at protocol level in QA-MP-BLOCKER-1), so this was purely the loader. Fix (minimal, targeted, mirroring `playerModel.js:84-87`): import `DRACOLoader` next to the `GLTFLoader` import, then `const _mpDraco = new DRACOLoader(); _mpDraco.setDecoderPath(assetUrl('/draco/')); const _mpGltf = new GLTFLoader(); _mpGltf.setDRACOLoader(_mpDraco);`. Also added `console.warn('[mp] avatar_load_error', peer?.id, err)` in the load-error handler so future GLB failures are visible. NO capsule fallback added (kept the fix targeted). No wire/protocol/gameplay/physics/CSP/Nostr change. Verified: `npm run check` green, `npm run build` green, `npm test` green. **LIVE v0.2.370-alpha stays peer-invisible until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.371** — source-only; the maintainer must bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.371-alpha (or the merge commit) before a redeploy (`--base=/quest/`) picks it up; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active. H4 NOT closed until a real-browser two-npub retest on the redeployed build shows both avatars in-world. `godMode` false; no network/deploy/publish/tag.
- 🔧 **v0.2.370-alpha — base-aware pinned-entry import fixed (ENTER ARENA no longer freezes under the `/quest/` mount).** A real-browser test on chiefmonkey.art/quest/ (v0.2.369-alpha, Brave + Firefox, two authenticated npubs) found the pre-auth menu stays responsive but clicking **ENTER ARENA freezes the session** before any scene/canvas/frame — the arena never boots (Rapier itself was ruled out: a direct import/init of the built Rapier module succeeds). Root cause: the vite CSP plugin (`vite.config.js`) emitted the pinned-entry URL **root-relative** — `/assets/torii-entry.js?v=<stamp>` — for BOTH the inline bootstrap `import()` in `index.html` AND every chunk's back-reference import of the entry. Under the Suite's `--base=/quest/` build those root-absolute URLs 404 at host root (they should be `/quest/assets/…`); and because `arenaRuntime.js` **statically imports the entry**, ENTER ARENA's `import('./arenaRuntime.js')` module-graph load REJECTED on the 404, so the arena never started (live symptom: the click "freezes"). Fix: the plugin now captures the resolved deploy base (`configResolved` → `resolvedBase`) and emits `${base}assets/torii-entry.js?v=<stamp>` via a single `entryUrl(base)` helper — `/quest/assets/…` on the Suite, `/assets/…` at root — applied to the inline import append, the (now base-agnostic) `<script>`/`<link>` removal regexes, and the chunk back-reference rewrite. Vite always normalises `base` to end in `/`, so `${base}assets/…` is correct for both mounts. `tools/regression-check.mjs` [16] made base-agnostic (matches `[^'"]*\/assets\/torii-entry.js`). NEW deterministic regression `tests/quest-base-entry.test.js` does a REAL `--base=/quest/` build into a throwaway outDir and asserts: the inline bootstrap entry URL matches `/^\/quest\/assets\/torii-entry\.js\?v=/`, no dist artifact references the root-relative `/assets/torii-entry.js`, the arenaRuntime chunk (the ENTER ARENA graph) back-references the entry under `/quest/`, all entry URLs are byte-identical (single module instance — also guards the v0.2.285 "does not provide export" class), and no static entry `<script>` tag survives. Verified: a `--base=/quest/` build emits `/quest/assets/torii-entry.js?v=<stamp>` in the inline bootstrap AND the arenaRuntime chunk with zero root-relative refs; a puppeteer ENTER-ARENA repro on a faithful `/quest/` static server (SPA fallback) boots the arena — ToriiDebug present, frames advance — with NO "Failed to fetch dynamically imported module". +5 tests (new file), 2264→2269 passing / 161 files; checks green. **LIVE v0.2.366-alpha remains broken until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.370** — source-only; the Suite installer is pinned at `TORII_QUEST_REF=v0.2.367-alpha`, so the maintainer must first bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.370-alpha (or the merge commit) before a redeploy (which builds with `--base=/quest/`) will include it; do NOT touch the VPS while the Continuum session is active on the shared host. No gameplay/physics/CSP-policy/Nostr change; the v0.2.369 assetUrl fix is untouched; no network/deploy/publish/tag; `godMode` false. (Non-causal follow-ups explicitly NOT fixed here: the pre-arena root `/sw.js` 404 and the GitHub releases/latest 404 — neither blocks the arena boot.)
- 🔧 **v0.2.369-alpha — root-relative asset paths fixed for the `/quest/` mount (graphics + peer-avatar visibility).** Real-browser two-npub test on chiefmonkey.art/quest/ (Firefox + Brave) revealed the 3D arena loads and peers auth/join, but **no `.glb` model files load** — bots fall back to capsule "pills" and **peer avatars never appear** (players can't see one another). Root cause: every `GLTFLoader.load('/foo.glb')`, `DRACOLoader.setDecoderPath('/draco/')`, and `TextureLoader.load('/bitcoin-b.png')` in `src/` used **root-relative** paths; under the Suite's `/quest/` path-prefix mount these resolve to `https://chiefmonkey.art/foo.glb` (404) instead of `https://chiefmonkey.art/quest/foo.glb` (200 — confirmed: root 404, `/quest/` 200). The peer-avatar loader (`arenaRuntime.js` `avatarLoader`) has no fallback on GLB failure, so a 404 left peers with no rendered body. Fix: new `src/assetUrl.js` helper (`assetUrl(name)` → `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}${name}` with leading-slash strip + trailing-slash normalisation), mirroring the existing `audio.js` BASE_URL pattern. Applied to all 8 source files (arena.js, firstPersonBody.js, arenaRuntime.js, playerModel.js, weapons.js, botModel.js, napNpc.js, scene.js) — 8 GLB loads, 6 `setDecoderPath` calls, the `bitcoin-b.png` texture, and `char.file` (wrapped at the load site, config values unchanged). Vite inlines `BASE_URL`='/quest/' in Suite builds (verified via a `--base=/quest/` build: `/quest/` literal present in the arenaRuntime chunk + all GLB args passed to the helper) and '/' in local dev, so the same code resolves correctly in both. HARDENED `tools/regression-check.mjs` [16]: every `setDecoderPath` must be wrapped in `assetUrl('/draco/')`, and a NEW narrow guard FAILS on any bare root-relative `.load('/...glb|png|webp|…')` in `src/` (the `/mp` WS path is a WS dial, not a loader call, so it is naturally excluded). +0 net tests (3 existing arena-source assertions updated to expect the `assetUrl('…')`-wrapped form); 2264/2264 pass, 20/20 checks green. **LIVE v0.2.366-alpha remains broken until a Suite redeploy that includes v0.2.369** — the fix is source-only; the Suite installer is pinned at `TORII_QUEST_REF=v0.2.367-alpha`, so the maintainer must first bump the Suite Quest ref to v0.2.369-alpha (or the merge commit) before a redeploy (which builds with `--base=/quest/`) will include it. H4 (full in-world roster visualisation) NOT closed until a real-browser retest on the redeployed build shows both players' avatars in-world. SERVICE-WORKER follow-up (out of scope, non-blocking): `public/sw.js` registration (`register('/sw.js')`) and precache (`/wall-texture.webp`, `/bitcoin-b.png`) are still root-relative and 404 under `/quest/`, so the SW does not register — the game loads all assets via network regardless; making the SW scope-aware is a separate offline-support task. No gameplay/physics/CSP/Nostr change; no network/deploy/publish/tag; `godMode` false.
- ✅ **v0.2.268 — R4 barrel-leak audit (shellReport + shell-entry sweep)** (audit only — no code change; `R4-implementation-report.md`): swept the post-R2 shell entry (`src/main.js` static graph, arenaRuntime dynamic-import excluded) for barrel leaks and oversight strings reachable at first paint. **Finding: shell is already clean — no actionable leak.** (1) `src/engine/debug/shellReport.js` (51,822 B / ~52 KB) is reached ONLY by `proofSurfaceCheck.js` and `toriiDebug.js`, both of which are on the deferred arena path (`toriiDebug` ← `arenaRuntime.js`; `proofSurfaceCheck` ← proofSurface render/gate ← `arena.js`). Confirmed two ways: a static BFS from `main.js` (arenaRuntime excluded) does NOT reach `shellReport.js`, and its UNIQUE fixture string `npub1demo0gateway0report…` appears in `dist/assets/arenaRuntime-*.js` (1×) but NOT in the shell chunk `dist/assets/torii-entry.js` (0×). (2) The only re-export barrel in `src/` is `src/sdk/index.js` (54 re-exports) — imported ONLY by `proofSurfaceCheck.js`, i.e. also arena-only, never reached from the shell. (3) No shell module is a non-first-paint heavyweight: the 35 shell-reachable modules (258 KB src → 103.64 KB / 35.66 KB gzip built) are all genuine pre-arena flow (gateway read/travel/handshake, nostr relay/profile/leaderboard previews, consent gate, hud, state, zoneRoute); the largest (`main.js` 18.6 KB, `gatewayRead` 15.2 KB, `travelRequest` 14.3 KB, `nostr` 13.5 KB) are all used before ENTER ARENA. (4) No non-three module is duplicated across shell/arena (26 shared modules live once in the entry chunk; arenaRuntime references them). (5) The only demo-fixture strings in the shell (`npub1demo0player0fixture…`, ~60 B each in `main.js`) are intentional preview fallbacks for the pre-arena product/leaderboard/update preview blocks. **Bundle UNCHANGED (audit only): torii-entry.js 103.64 KB raw / 35.66 KB gzip; arenaRuntime 193.17 KB / 63.86 KB gzip; three-vendor 625.07 KB / 160.55 KB gzip (still deferred behind ENTER ARENA — R2 boundary intact).** No code modified, so semantics/tests identical — `npm run check` 16/16 GREEN, `npx vitest run` 1843/1843 across 122 files. - ✅ **v0.2.223 — MVP playtest results surfaced on the Continuum dashboard** (`buildPlaytestResultsCardModel`/`PLAYTESTRESULTS_LASTKNOWN`/`PLAYTESTRESULTS_BADGE` + `_playtestResultsSection` in `src/engine/dashboard/toriiQuestData.js`; live gather in `tools/build-torii-quest-dashboard.mjs`; `tests/torii-quest-dashboard.test.js`): the Torii Quest oversight dashboard now carries a compact **Playtest results** section placed BETWEEN the MVP-approval card and the Manual-validation card, so the page shows whether the actual MVP playtest has been RECORDED — not only that approval is pending. The committed `MVP_PLAYTEST_RESULTS.md` recording file ships with all 17 items across 13 sections blank (Result cells empty → reads **not-run**) and version/commit left blank so the tester records the build they actually tested — distinct from the build-regenerated, ephemeral `MVP_PLAYTEST_RESULTS_TEMPLATE.md`. The new PURE, node-safe `tools/playtestResultsState.mjs` exports `summarizePlaytestForState`/`formatPlaytestResultsState` + frozen `PLAYTEST_RESULTS_STATE_SCHEMA`(`torii.playtest-results-state`)/`_SCHEMA_VERSION`(1)/`_BADGE`/`_FILE`(`MVP_PLAYTEST_RESULTS.md`)/`_STATUSES`; it WRAPS the existing `summarizePlaytestResults` parser, accepts either raw results markdown OR a summary, and maps to statuses **unknown** (no items) / **not-run** (every item blank — the shipped default) / **incomplete** (some recorded, some blank) / **attention** (any FAIL) / **complete** (all PASS/N-A, no FAIL). `approvalImplied` is HARD-pinned **false** in EVERY branch — including a fully complete playtest — so the state can never, by itself, imply approval; a recorded playtest is necessary but NOT sufficient. The thin CLI `tools/playtest-results-status.mjs` reads/summarises the file read-only (text / `--json`), and under an explicit `--write` seeds a BLANK canonical record ONLY IF the file is absent (NO-CLOBBER — never destroys a tester's recorded results), the path confined in-repo via the shared `resolveHandoffWritePath`. The new PURE, browser-safe `buildMvpApprovalModel(input?)` renders the standard card shape (badge/kind/band/statusLabel/pill/metrics/note), takes plain data (`status`/`approved`/`version`/`approvedBy`/`approvedAt`), and degrades to an honest `MVPAPPROVAL_LASTKNOWN` pending fallback (`kind:'last-known'`) with no input (never throws); `tools/build-torii-quest-dashboard.mjs` reads the on-disk record and re-shapes it through `buildApprovalState`+`summarizeApprovalForState` (the strict `isApproved()` floor) before folding it via `buildToriiQuestModel({mvpApproval})`. Rendering approved is STRICT: only an exact `approved` status WITH the `approved:true` flag AND `approved_by`+`approved_at` renders as approved (`band:'approved'`, `pill:'no-blocker'`); anything else stays PENDING (`band:'pending'`, `pill:'manual'`), so a partial/garbled record can never look approved. - ✅ **v0.2.220 — MVP manual-approval state placeholder** (`tools/mvpApproval.mjs` pure module + CLI `tools/mvp-approval-state.mjs`, `npm run approval:state` + `MVP_APPROVAL_STATE.json`; wired into `tools/nextActionState.mjs`/`tools/next-action-state.mjs`; `tests/mvp-approval-state.test.js` + `tests/next-action-state.test.js`): a clean, auditable single source to record MVP approval when the user explicitly approves — instead of hand-editing many docs or guessing. The PURE, node-safe `tools/mvpApproval.mjs` models a `{kind:'torii.mvp-approval-state'/v1, status, version, commit, approved_by, approved_at, notes, safety{deploy/publish/push/tag/networkWrite/nostrWrite/godMode all false}}` record and EXPORTS `buildApprovalState`/`validateApprovalState`/`isApproved`/`formatApprovalState`/`summarizeApprovalForState` + frozen `MVP_APPROVAL_SCHEMA`(`torii.mvp-approval-state`)/`_SCHEMA_VERSION`(1)/`_BADGE`/`_FILE`(`MVP_APPROVAL_STATE.json`)/`_STATUSES`/`APPROVAL_REQUIRED_FIELDS`(`approved_by`,`approved_at`). It is designed so it CANNOT silently approve: `buildApprovalState` coerces any `status` other than `'approved'` to `'pending'` and carries provenance verbatim (trimmed→null); `validateApprovalState` returns `{ok,errors,warnings}` and ERRORS on a wrong kind/schemaVersion/status, a bad version marker, a non-null/non-string commit, an APPROVED record missing any required provenance field or a version marker, OR a PENDING record that carries approver fields; `isApproved` is strict (status `'approved'` AND validation ok). The committed `MVP_APPROVAL_STATE.json` defaults to **pending** (`approved_by`/`approved_at`/`commit` null, pending note, safety all-false, valid). The thin CLI `tools/mvp-approval-state.mjs` reads the record re-shaped through `buildApprovalState` (else a default pending for the current config version), renders text / `--json`, and under an explicit `--write` emits ONLY a PENDING record for the current version — there is deliberately NO `--approve` path in this slice. The machine-readable next-action state now folds the record via `summarizeApprovalForState` (`mvpApproval:{status,approved,approvedBy,approvedAt,version}` — `mvpApproval` added to `NEXT_ACTION_STATE_REQUIRED_KEYS`; the CLI gathers it from `MVP_APPROVAL_STATE.json`), and the text/markdown formatters print an `MVP approval:` line, so a FUTURE approval flips ONE state file rather than scattered docs. +1 new test file `tests/mvp-approval-state.test.js` (build coercion/defaults, validate safety floor, isApproved strictness, formatters, committed-artifact pending+valid+tracks `VERSION`) + 3 added `tests/next-action-state.test.js` cases (pending fold → `approved:false`; approved valid vs partial; unknown when no record) → suite 1417→1443, files 87→88. - ✅ **v0.2.219 — Service-worker cache-version hygiene + guard** (`public/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION`; `tools/regression-check.mjs` `[5]` block): changed `public/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` from the static `tq-v1` to a version-tracking `tq-v0.2.219-alpha` so every shipped version bump now mints a fresh service-worker cache name (`torii-quest-tq-v0.2.219-alpha`) and the existing `activate` handler purges the prior version's caches — no stale assets after an asset-changing deploy. HARDENED the regression-check `[5]` version-marker block with a guard that reads the `CACHE_VERSION` literal via regex and FAILS if it does not EMBED the current `EXPECTED_VERSION` (so it can never silently rot back to a static value like `tq-v1`; emits a `public/sw.js CACHE_VERSION tracks v0.2.219-alpha` pass line). Resolves the v0.2.217/v0.2.218 security-review advisory (non-blocking: `public/sw.js` `CACHE_VERSION` remained `tq-v1`). `public/sw.js` is copied VERBATIM by Vite (it lives in `public/`, no module system), so it cannot `import` config.js — `CACHE_VERSION` stays a hardcoded string literal kept in lockstep with the other version markers via the new guard, the same enforcement pattern as the `package.json` version. All install/activate/fetch/`skipWaiting`/`clients.claim`/old-cache-purge SW safety behavior is UNCHANGED, and the cache-first-for-assets / network-first-for-JS-CSS-HTML strategy is untouched. No test file added — the existing 15-check gate carries the guard, so the Vitest suite stays at 1417/87 (no `CURRENT_TEST_STATUS`/`DEFAULT_TEST_STATUS` numeric change). SERVICE-WORKER/TOOLING-ONLY — no runtime/gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; no network/deploy/publish/tag/release/self-update; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`.

Resolves the v0.2.217 security-review advisory (low risk: `package.json` lacked `"private": true`). The [5] block was refactored to read the whole `pkg` object once so both the version match and the privacy flag are checked from a single parse. No test file added — the existing 15-check gate carries the guard, so the Vitest suite stays at 1417/87 (no `CURRENT_TEST_STATUS`/`DEFAULT_TEST_STATUS` numeric change). - ✅ **v0.2.212 — Release-manifest shell-less report discovery** (`selectRecentReports` + `RELEASE_MANIFEST_REPORT_RE`/`_CAP` in `tools/releaseManifest.mjs`; `tools/release-manifest.mjs` CLI; `tests/release-manifest.test.js`): the release-manifest CLI's recent-report discovery no longer shells out to `execSync('ls torii-v*-report.md 2>/dev/null')`. A v0.2.211 security review SHIPPED but noted the hardcoded shell glob has no injection vector yet is cleaner/more portable as plain JS. The new PURE `selectRecentReports(names, cap=RELEASE_MANIFEST_REPORT_CAP)` filters names matching the exported `RELEASE_MANIFEST_REPORT_RE` (`/^torii-v.*-report\.md$/`), sorts them lexicographically for a deterministic order (matching the old `ls` default), and `.slice(-cap)` to the most recent (cap 6; a non-positive/garbled cap falls back to the default); it is null-safe and never throws. `recentReports()` in the CLI now calls `selectRecentReports(readdirSync(ROOT))` — no `child_process`, identical output, fully unit-testable. `execSync` stays imported only for the read-only `git rev-parse` commit stamp. +5 tests (`tests/release-manifest.test.js`; 1372→1377, files unchanged at 86): report-shape filtering drops non-reports, deterministic sort regardless of input order, cap to the most recent, custom/garbled cap handling, and `RELEASE_MANIFEST_REPORT_RE`/`_CAP` exposure + null-safety (`undefined`/non-array/garbled entries → `[]`, never throws). DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY — build-time CLI discovery + pure helper + tests; no generator output-shape change; no runtime/gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; no network/deploy/publish/tag/release/self-update; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`.
- ✅ **v0.2.211 — Release artifact integrity manifest** (`tools/releaseManifest.mjs` + CLI `tools/release-manifest.mjs`, `npm run release:manifest` + `tests/release-manifest.test.js`): a pure, node-safe, read-only manifest that records the RC package artifacts a future GitHub release / VPS self-update flow must verify, with present/missing status + stable sha256 checksums. The frozen `RELEASE_MANIFEST_REQUIRED` (`RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT.md`, `MVP_RELEASE_PACKAGE.md`, `GITHUB_RELEASE_DRY_RUN.md`, `public/release-metadata.json`, `package.json`, `index.html`) + `RELEASE_MANIFEST_OPTIONAL` (RC snapshot, playtest checklist/results, handoff, VPS install, continuum-data) each carry `{key,file,label,category}`; the CLI reads each in-repo text doc / served build-metadata file, computes a sha256 + byte size via `node:crypto`, and injects a `{present,sha256,bytes}` map into `buildReleaseManifestModel(inputs)` → `{schema:'torii.release-manifest'/v1, badge, title, manifest:true, status, complete, version, packageVersion, gitCommit, liveUrl, required[], optional[], missingRequired[], counts:{required,requiredPresent,requiredMissing,optional,optionalPresent,hashed}, notes[], latestReports[], safety (released/tagged/published/selfUpdated/served/wrote/network/hashedSecrets all false), rendered:false, actionable:false}`. It bands **COMPLETE** iff every REQUIRED artifact is present (an unknown present flag is never treated as missing) else **INCOMPLETE**; an injected non-64-hex sha256 is rejected; checksums cover in-repo text docs + small served build-metadata JSON only — no secrets, no large binaries. Null/garbled inputs degrade to honest UNKNOWNs and never throw. `formatReleaseManifest()`/`formatReleaseManifestMarkdown()` render stable text/markdown (null-safe; `'release-manifest: (no manifest)'` / `'_(no manifest)_'`). The thin CLI does the fs/git I/O (read-only `git rev-parse`), modes text / `--json` (schema `torii.release-manifest` v1) / `--markdown`; READ-ONLY except an opt-in bounded in-repo `--write[=path]` (default `RELEASE_ARTIFACT_MANIFEST.md`, confined via the SHARED `resolveHandoffWritePath` — absolute / `..` rejected); always exits 0 (rejected `--write` path → exit 2) — VISIBILITY, not a gate (the authority stays `npm run test:release`). MANIFEST ONLY — creates no GitHub release, no git tag, no push, no deploy/publish/self-update, and reaches no network/server. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; read-only except the explicit `--write` output; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +14 tests (`tests/release-manifest.test.js`; 1358→1372, files 85→86; asserts every REQUIRED artifact exists on disk so a missing RC artifact is caught locally).
- ✅ **v0.2.210 — MVP RC snapshot / freeze-candidate summary** (`tools/rcSnapshot.mjs` + CLI `tools/rc-snapshot.mjs`, `npm run rc:snapshot` + `tests/rc-snapshot.test.js`): a pure, node-safe, read-only RC snapshot that COMPOSES the already-pure local verdicts — `buildMvpRcGate()` + `runMvpReadiness()` + `buildGithubReleaseDryRunModel()` + `gatherReleaseReadiness()` + the curated test counts + a present/missing map of every RC package doc — into ONE freeze-candidate document (`MVP_RC_SNAPSHOT.md`), re-deriving no check. `buildRcSnapshotModel(inputs)` → `{schema:'torii.rc-snapshot'/v1, badge, title, snapshot:true, status, freezeCandidate, version, packageVersion, gitCommit, liveUrl, versionConsistency, rcGate, mvpReadiness, releaseDryRun, tests, regression, docs, advisories, manualValidation, releaseSteps, latestReports, safety (released/tagged/published/announced/served/navigated/wrote/network all false), rendered:false, actionable:false}`; it bands **FREEZE-CANDIDATE** (RC gate is a candidate AND the dry-run is not blocked) / **NEAR** / **BLOCKED**, surfaces what STILL needs manual user validation and what cutting a real GitHub release/tag would require, and flags version drift across the folded sources via `rcSnapshotVersionConsistency()`. Null/garbled inputs degrade to honest UNKNOWNs and never throw. `formatRcSnapshot()`/`formatRcSnapshotMarkdown()` render stable text/markdown (null-safe; `'rc-snapshot: (no snapshot)'` / `'_(no snapshot)_'`). The thin CLI does the fs/git I/O (read-only `git rev-parse`/`status --porcelain`/`rev-parse @{u}` — NO fetch), stat-s each RC doc for present/missing, lists recent `torii-v*-report.md`; modes text / `--json` (schema `torii.rc-snapshot` v1) / `--markdown`; it is READ-ONLY except an opt-in bounded in-repo `--write[=path]` (default `MVP_RC_SNAPSHOT.md`, confined via the SHARED `resolveHandoffWritePath` — absolute / `..` rejected); always exits 0 (rejected `--write` path → exit 2) — VISIBILITY, not a gate (the authority stays `npm run test:release`). SNAPSHOT ONLY — creates no GitHub release, no git tag, no push, no deploy/publish/announce, and reaches no network/server. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; read-only except the explicit `--write` output; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +19 tests (`tests/rc-snapshot.test.js`; 1339→1358, files 84→85; asserts every referenced RC doc exists on disk so the doc list cannot silently rot).
- ✅ **v0.2.209 — Generated commit-stamp clarity** (`tools/commitStamp.mjs` + `tools/agentHandoff.mjs`/`releasePackage.mjs`/`playtestChecklist.mjs`/`releaseNotes.mjs`/`githubReleaseDryRun.mjs`/`handoffSummary.mjs` + `tests/agent-handoff.test.js`): the tracked generated artifacts (`HANDOFF.generated.md`, `MVP_RELEASE_PACKAGE.md`, `MVP_PLAYTEST_CHECKLIST.md`, `RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT.md`, `GITHUB_RELEASE_DRY_RUN.md`) are written BEFORE the commit that will contain them, so the short commit they stamped was repo HEAD AT GENERATION — the PARENT of each file's own commit. Security reviews kept flagging a bare "commit"/"Git commit" label as cosmetic-but-stale/misleading. Added one shared pure helper (`sourceCommitLabel` → `<hash> (source commit at generation — precedes this file's own commit)`/`(unavailable)`; `sourceCommitInline` → ` @ <hash> (source)`/`''`; `SOURCE_COMMIT_NOTE`) and wired all five generator libs plus the handoff-summary brief to render the stamp with explicit non-misleading wording — inline ` @ <hash> (source)` on the version line, and a dedicated `**Source commit:**` field (was `**Git commit:**`) in the handoff exports. `tools/playtestResults.mjs` deliberately UNTOUCHED (its `Commit` row is a human-filled intake prefill asserted verbatim in its test). +5 tests (1334→1339, files unchanged at 84): hash + "source commit" present, null/empty/garbled → `(unavailable)`/`''`, markdown uses `**Source commit:**` never `**Git commit:**`. No generator schema/output-shape change; affected artifacts regenerated so committed copies carry the honest wording. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; no runtime/gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; no network/deploy/publish; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`.
- ✅ **v0.2.208 — Progress parser gap cleanup** (`tools/toriiQuestDashboardParse.mjs` `deriveToriiQuestData()` + `tests/torii-quest-dashboard.parse.test.js`): closed the Continuum build-parser gaps the build logs flagged (`activeNow: no usable items parsed from torii-quest-progress.md` and `completed24h: no usable items parsed from torii-quest-progress.md`, which forced the dashboard to keep curated defaults). Root cause was the per-section `tryList(key, items, {min,max})` bounds, not the parsing logic: torii-quest-progress.md's `## Active now` / `## Completed last 24h` are running logs (each shipped slice prepends an entry) that had grown past the original v0.2.174 guard ceilings — activeNow had 34 top-level bullets vs `max:16`, completed24h had 26 struck bullets vs `max:24` — so both sections were dropped as `gaps`. The running-log format is intended, so per the work-order's "add parser tolerance with tests" option the bounds were RAISED (activeNow/completed24h `max:60`, archive `max:40`, next12 `max:24`) while KEEPING the protective guard — a `>60`-item garbled section still degrades to the curated default. After the fix `npm run build:torii-quest-dashboard` derives all four lists from torii-quest-progress.md (`next12 (12), activeNow (34), completed24h (26), archive (11)`; gaps: none). +2 tests (15→17): a long-but-bounded 34/26 parse with no gap, and an absurd 61-item list that still falls back. Pure build-time parser change only — no parser CALL site, schema, or dashboard render path changed. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`.
- ✅ **v0.2.207 — GitHub MVP release dry-run checklist** (`buildGithubReleaseDryRunModel`/`formatGithubReleaseDryRun`/`formatGithubReleaseDryRunMarkdown` in `tools/githubReleaseDryRun.mjs` + CLI `tools/github-release-dry-run.mjs`, `npm run release:dry-run`): a pure, node-safe LOCAL dry-run that validates the prerequisites for a FUTURE GitHub MVP proof release WITHOUT creating it. It folds a frozen 9-item prerequisite set (`GITHUB_RELEASE_DRY_RUN_PREREQUISITES` — version marker [gating], version↔package sync [gating], clean working tree [soft], pushed commit [soft], release-notes draft present [gating], release package present [gating], RC gate ready [gating], public live URL [gating], no autoUpdate/actionable release metadata [gating]) plus 3 known advisories (rapier bundle / SDK_DEBUG / alpha). The model carries `{schema:'torii.github-release-dry-run'/v1, badge, title, dryRun:true, version, packageVersion, gitCommit, status('ready'/'near'/'blocked'), statusLabel, ready, prerequisites[] (each {key,label,gating,state∈ok/blocked/pending/unknown,detail}), missing[], advisories[], approvalGate, approvalRequired:true, futureCommands[] (git tag / git push / gh release — each note demands approval), safety (tagged/released/pushed/published/deployed/announced/served/navigated/wrote/network all false), rendered:false, actionable:false}`; null/garbled inputs degrade to honest unknowns and never throw. Verdict: a gating prerequisite in `blocked` → BLOCKED; a gating `unknown` or a soft-pending (expected dirty tree / unpushed HEAD before the parent pushes) → NEAR; else READY — and even READY reads "READY (pending manual approval)" because the manual-approval gate is a standing always-shown requirement, never a scored item. The thin CLI stamps version (`configVersion()`) + package version + best-effort short commit + read-only git (`git status --porcelain` for clean-tree, `git rev-parse HEAD` vs `@{u}` for pushed — NO fetch) + the SHARED live URL + the release-metadata autoUpdate/actionable read; it builds with `gateReady:null` (the RC gate is deliberately NOT run by the tool, holding gate-ready at `unknown`→NEAR rather than over-claiming); modes text / `--json` (schema `torii.github-release-dry-run` v1) / `--markdown`; it is READ-ONLY except an opt-in bounded in-repo `--write[=path]` (default `GITHUB_RELEASE_DRY_RUN.md`, confined via the SHARED `resolveHandoffWritePath` — absolute / `..` rejected); always exits 0 (rejected `--write` path → exit 2). DRY-RUN ONLY — it runs NO `git tag`, NO `git push`, NO `gh release`, NO deploy/publish/announce, and reaches NO network/server; the suggested future commands are TEXT ONLY and each carries an explicit "do not run without user approval". DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; read-only except the explicit `--write` output; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +16 tests (`tests/github-release-dry-run.test.js`).
- ✅ **v0.2.206 — MVP release package index** (`buildReleasePackageModel`/`formatReleasePackage`/`formatReleasePackageMarkdown` in `tools/releasePackage.mjs` + CLI `tools/release-package.mjs`, `npm run release:package`): a pure, node-safe release-package INDEX generator producing a single discoverability artifact (`MVP_RELEASE_PACKAGE.md`) so humans and future agents can find every relevant MVP-proof file fast. The frozen `RELEASE_PACKAGE_ENTRIES` index (10 entries grouped by category) points at `RELEASE_NOTES_DRAFT.md`, `MVP_PLAYTEST_CHECKLIST.md`, `MVP_PLAYTEST_RESULTS_TEMPLATE.md`, `HANDOFF.generated.md`, `torii-quest-handoff.md`, `torii-quest-progress.md`, `torii-quest-todo.md`, `UPDATE_CHECK.md`, `VPS_INSTALL.md`, `ZONE_FALLBACK_READINESS.md`, and folds in version/commit, the curated test-count, the live URL, the known non-blocking advisories, and the recommended next safe action. The model carries `{schema:'torii.release-package'/v1, badge, title, index:true, version, gitCommit, liveUrl, tests, regression, entries[] (each {key,file,label,category,present}), advisories[], latestReports[], nextAction, safety (all false), rendered:false, actionable:false}`; the `present` map is INJECTED by the CLI so the helper stays fs-free, and null/garbled inputs degrade to honest unknowns and never throw. `formatReleasePackage()`/`formatReleasePackageMarkdown()` render text/markdown grouped by category with a present/missing/unknown mark per file (null-safe). The thin CLI stamps version (`configVersion()`) + best-effort short commit + the SHARED live URL + the curated `CURRENT_TEST_STATUS`, stat-s each indexed file for present/missing, and lists recent `torii-v*-report.md`; modes text / `--json` (schema `torii.release-package` v1) / `--markdown`; it is READ-ONLY except an opt-in bounded in-repo `--write[=path]` (default `MVP_RELEASE_PACKAGE.md`, confined via the SHARED `resolveHandoffWritePath` — absolute / `..` rejected); always exits 0 (rejected `--write` path → exit 2). INDEX ONLY — creates no GitHub release, no git tag, no public announcement, no deploy/publish, and reaches no network/server; NOT a gameplay change and not a live browser test. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; read-only except the explicit `--write` output; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +12 tests (`tests/release-package.test.js`).
- ✅ **v0.2.205 — Playtest checklist doc warning cleanup** (`tools/playtestChecklist.mjs`): cleared the non-blocking WARN from the v0.2.204 security review where two illustrative `v0.2.203-alpha` example strings (plus a dated `(v0.2.203)` authorship stamp in the file header) lingered in the checklist tooling's comments/examples and were re-flagged each review as "stale". Made them **version-neutral** so future security reviews stop tripping on them: the `LAUNCH-1` `expected` version-label example now reads "matching the build (the current vX.Y.Z-alpha marker)", the `buildPlaytestChecklistModel` `version` input doc-comment now reads "config.js VERSION (a 'vX.Y.Z-alpha' marker)", and the file-header authorship stamp drops the bare `(v0.2.203)`. Docs/comment cleanup ONLY — no behavior change, no CLI/schema/output change, and no new test file, so the suite count is unchanged at 1304/82 (the existing `tests/playtest-checklist.test.js` only asserts `expected` is a non-empty string, so the example wording is free to change). NON-GOALS held: no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`.
- ✅ **v0.2.204 — MVP manual playtest results intake template** (`buildPlaytestResultsTemplate`/`formatPlaytestResultsTemplate`/`formatPlaytestResultsTemplateMarkdown` + `parsePlaytestResults`/`summarizePlaytestResults`/`formatPlaytestResultsSummary` in `tools/playtestResults.mjs` + CLI `tools/playtest-results.mjs`, `npm run playtest:results`): a pure, node-safe results-intake generator that gives a structured way to RECORD manual playtest results from the v0.2.203 `MVP_PLAYTEST_CHECKLIST.md` and feed failures back into todo/progress/handoff without ambiguity. The blank results template DERIVES its 17 items across 13 sections from `PLAYTEST_CHECKLIST_SECTIONS` (imported from `tools/playtestChecklist.mjs`), so it stays in lock-step with the checklist as the single source of truth — carrying build/version, commit, live URL, tester, date, environment + overall meta fields and per-item result (PASS/FAIL/N/A), severity, repro notes, screenshot/video refs and a recommended next action. The thin CLI stamps version (`configVersion()`) + best-effort short commit + the SHARED live URL; modes text / `--json` (schema `torii.playtest-results` v1) / `--markdown`; it is READ-ONLY except an opt-in bounded in-repo `--write[=path]` (default `MVP_PLAYTEST_RESULTS_TEMPLATE.md`, confined via the SHARED `resolveHandoffWritePath` — absolute / `..` rejected) plus a read-only `--summarize[=path]` (in-repo read only); always exits 0 (rejected `--write`/`--summarize` path → exit 2). RESULTS INTAKE ONLY — runs no browser, automates nothing, navigates/serves/deploys/publishes/writes (beyond the explicit `--write`) NOTHING; NOT a gameplay change and not a live browser test. DOCS/TOOLING-ONLY; read-only except the explicit `--write` output; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +16 tests (`tests/playtest-results.test.js`).

- ✅ **v0.2.201 — MVP release-candidate gate** (`buildMvpRcGate`/`formatMvpRcGate`/`formatMvpRcGateMarkdown` in `tools/mvpRcGate.mjs` + CLI `tools/mvp-rc-gate.mjs`, `npm run rc:gate`): a pure, node-safe read-only gate that answers ONE question — is this build ready to call an MVP proof-of-concept *release candidate*? It COMPOSES the existing readiness signals only (no re-derivation): the `runMvpReadiness()` rollup (Nostr read health / gateway-travel / update-flow / host-route smoke + version/meta/test/VPS/docs floor) and the `gatherReleaseReadiness()`→`buildReleaseReadiness()` fold (versionSync / tests / regression / zoneFallback / docs required signals, bundle advisory), with `buildHandoffSummary()` as the next-safe-task fallback. Verdict: **READY** iff release is ready AND the MVP rollup is green AND there are no release unknowns; **BLOCKED** if inputs are missing, a release blocker exists, or ≥2 MVP signals fail; else **NEAR**. `pct` blends passing MVP signals + the five required release signals; `reasons` lists `release:<key>` / `mvp:<key>: <detail>` / `release:<key> (not checked this pass)`; `nextTasks` leads with `Clear top blocker: …` then the safe task, deduped + capped at 2. Every report pins `served/deployed/published/navigated/released/tagged/wrote/network=false`; null/garbled inputs degrade to an honest BLOCKED at 0% and never throw. The thin CLI runs `gatherReleaseReadiness` + `runMvpReadiness` + `buildHandoffSummary`, supports text / `--json` (schema `torii.mvp-rc-gate` v1) / `--markdown`; it is READ-ONLY/local/no-network, NEVER writes, creates no release, cuts no git tag, and always exits 0. INFRA/TOOLING-ONLY; no real release creation / git tag / GitHub release / deploy / network / server; read-only; no gameplay/physics/shooter/Rapier change; no Nostr signing/publishing/live network write; `godMode` stays false; no new `setTimeout`/`Vector3`/`Matrix4`. +14 tests (`tests/mvp-rc-gate.test.js`).
- ✅ **v0.2.200 — Dashboard metric freshness cleanup** (`CURRENT_TEST_STATUS` + `testCountLabel()` in `src/engine/dashboard/toriiQuestData.js`): resolves the recurring stale LAST-KNOWN test-count issue noted in security reviews — `HEALTH_LASTKNOWN.totalTests` was a SECOND hand-maintained copy stuck at `'1180 passing'` while the suite was 1246/78, so the engineering-health "Total tests" metric and the metrics "Tests" row had drifted apart. Now a SINGLE curated source of truth `CURRENT_TEST_STATUS` `{passing:1246, files:78, fastProfile:5, foundationProfile:25}` + a `testCountLabel(status?)` helper (canonical `"<passing> passing / <files> files"`, null/partial-safe) feed BOTH surfaces: `HEALTH_LASTKNOWN.totalTests` now DERIVES from `testCountLabel()` and the metrics "Tests" row renders `testCountLabel()` + the profile counts, so the two cannot diverge again. To catch FUTURE drift, a new `tests/torii-quest-dashboard.test.js` block ("test-count freshness — single source of truth", +5 cases) asserts the source is frozen + well-shaped; the curated `files` equals the REAL on-disk `*.test.js` count via `readdirSync(tests/)` (forces a bump when files are added/removed); `testCountLabel()` is correct + degrades safely on null/partial; BOTH displayed surfaces derive from the single source and `totalTests` no longer matches `/1180/`; and `mvpReadiness.DEFAULT_TEST_STATUS` (the separate readiness-rollup capture, bumped to 1246/78) agrees with `CURRENT_TEST_STATUS` — enforced by a cross-capture test WITHOUT cross-importing (keeps the dashboard↔status concerns split). - ✅ **v0.2.191 — stale-doc detector** (`detectStaleDocs` in `tools/staleDocs.mjs` + `npm run docs:stale` / `tools/stale-docs.mjs`): a new ADVISORY detector that catches docs/status/version drift earlier and more clearly than the gated docConsistency guard. The pure helper reuses the docConsistency primitives (`CONTINUITY_DOCS`/`findVersionMarkers`/`versionInText`) and flags precise version-HEADER drift in each continuity doc, a continuity doc that never mentions the current version, a newest report no continuity doc links to, a newest report that lags the current version, and disagreeing test counts across the continuity docs. Low false positives by design: a HEADER assertion requires ONLY separator/markup chars between "version" and the marker, and backtick/double-quote spans are stripped first, so changelog prose ("version drift fixed (v0.2.137)") and quoted examples are ignored. The thin CLI (text default; `--json`) is local/read-only/network-free and ALWAYS exits 0 — ADVISORY, deliberately NOT wired into `npm run check` (the hard gate stays docConsistency [14], so the finer/higher-recall signals are surfaced, not enforced — keeping false positives from blocking safe dev). The CLI `--json` path prints `JSON.stringify(...)` to stdout and exits 0 — pure parseable JSON (node tools/release-readiness.mjs --json is pure JSON; plain `npm run` prepends a lifecycle banner to stdout, so scripted consumers use `npm run --silent release:status:json`) — while the default human block + the `realpathSync` run-guard are unchanged. TOOLING/DOCS-ONLY + read-only/local-only/no network; no gameplay/portal/physics/controls/Nostr change. +7 tests (`tests/release-readiness.test.js`).

The pure, node-safe `buildReadinessModel({zoneFallback})` folds the read-only `checkZoneFallbackReadiness({docs,dist})` result into a render-ready `{badge,status,statusLabel,checks,errors,warnings,note}` model with honest states — **READY** (docs ok + dist checked) / **DOCS READY · BUILD CHECK PENDING** (docs ok, no build yet) / **NOT READY** (a required doc/dist shape fails) / **NOT CHECKED** (no input) — and a four-row per-check table (SPA fallback documented, built dist route shape, host fallback configured = MANUAL, auto-update = MANUAL).
